# Proverbs

משלי

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs)

מספרי החוכמה, ספר הדרכה לחיי מוסר, חוכמה וצדק שעיקרו פתגמים ואמרי מוסר שיריים.

Guidance for living a wise, moral, and righteous life, in the form of poems and short statements.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 915, License: CC-BY-SA)

Versions (En):
  - The Koren Jerusalem Bible (Segments: 915, License: CC-BY-NC)
  - The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein (Segments: 915, License: CC-BY)


## Chapter 1

###### Proverbs 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:1)


מִ֭שְׁלֵי שְׁלֹמֹ֣ה בֶן־דָּוִ֑ד מֶ֝֗לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The proverbs of Shelomo the son of David, king of Yisra᾽el;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[These are] the analogues of Solomon, son of David, king of Israel [e.g., Torah is compared to a good woman; idolatry, to an adulteress.]


###### Proverbs 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:2)


לָדַ֣עַת חׇכְמָ֣ה וּמוּסָ֑ר לְ֝הָבִ֗ין אִמְרֵ֥י בִינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to know wisdom and instruction; to perceive the words of understanding;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[He stated these analogues] to make known [(to men the importance of studying Torah, which is)] wisdom and mussar (reproof), to make understood words of understanding.


###### Proverbs 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:3)


לָ֭קַחַת מוּסַ֣ר הַשְׂכֵּ֑ל צֶ֥דֶק וּ֝מִשְׁפָּ֗ט וּמֵשָׁרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to receive the instruction of wisdom, justice, and judgment, and equity;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To take [from them (the analogues)] wise instruction: charity, justice [(in accordance with the truth)], and justness [(compromise)].


###### Proverbs 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:4)


לָתֵ֣ת לִפְתָאיִ֣ם עׇרְמָ֑ה לְ֝נַ֗עַר דַּ֣עַת וּמְזִמָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to give prudence to the simple, to the young man knowledge and discretion.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To give to the innocent, wisdom; to the youth, [(who has not yet learned anything)], knowledge and counsel.


###### Proverbs 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:5)


יִשְׁמַ֣ע חָ֭כָם וְי֣וֹסֶף לֶ֑קַח וְ֝נָב֗וֹן תַּחְבֻּל֥וֹת יִקְנֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wise man will hear, and will increase learning; and a man of understanding shall attain to wise counsels:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise one will hear [these analogues] and he will add to his learning, and the understanding one [(who understands one thing from another)] will [(in addition to what he hears)] acquire stratagems.


###### Proverbs 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:6)


לְהָבִ֣ין מָ֭שָׁל וּמְלִיצָ֑ה דִּבְרֵ֥י חֲ֝כָמִ֗ים וְחִידֹתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to understand a proverb, and a figure; the words of the wise, and their riddles.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[They will take heart] to understand [both] the analogue and [even] the figure [e.g., (2:16) Beware of idolatry (the analogue); Beware of the strange woman (the figure)], the words of the wise [(the Torah expositors)] and their allusions.


###### Proverbs 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:7)


יִרְאַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה רֵאשִׁ֣ית דָּ֑עַת חׇכְמָ֥ה וּ֝מוּסָ֗ר אֱוִילִ֥ים בָּֽזוּ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The fear of the Lord is the beginning of knowledge: but fools despise wisdom and instruction.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(The book proper begins now.)] The fear of the L-rd is the first [i.e., the tithe] of knowledge [("Put your fear before your knowledge")], for fools [(those who do not fear the L-rd)] spurn wisdom and mussar.


###### Proverbs 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:8)


שְׁמַ֣ע בְּ֭נִי מוּסַ֣ר אָבִ֑יךָ וְאַל־תִּ֝טֹּ֗שׁ תּוֹרַ֥ת אִמֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, hear the instruction of thy father, and do not forsake the Tora of thy mother:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear, my son, the mussar of your Father [(what the L-rd gave to Moses, written and oral)], and do not forsake the Torah of your mother [(the congregation of Israel)] [("the Torah of your mother" - the words of the scribes, what they originated and added, making safeguards for the Torah)].


###### Proverbs 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:9)


כִּ֤י**׀** לִוְיַ֤ת חֵ֓ן הֵ֬ם לְרֹאשֶׁ֑ךָ וַ֝עֲנָקִ֗ים לְגַרְגְּרֹתֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for they shall be a graceful garland for thy head, and chains about thy neck.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For they are a chaplet of grace to your head and a necklace to your throat.


###### Proverbs 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:10)


בְּנִ֡י אִם־יְפַתּ֥וּךָ חַ֝טָּאִ֗ים אַל־תֹּבֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, if sinners entice thee, do not consent.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My son, if sinners entice you, do not yield.


###### Proverbs 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:11)


אִם־יֹאמְרוּ֮ לְכָ֢ה אִ֫תָּ֥נוּ נֶאֶרְבָ֥ה לְדָ֑ם נִצְפְּנָ֖ה לְנָקִ֣י חִנָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If they say, Come with us, let us lie in wait for blood, let us lurk for the innocent without cause:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If they say: Come with us; let us lie in wait for blood. Let us lurk for the innocent one [to spill his blood] — without cause —


###### Proverbs 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:12)


נִ֭בְלָעֵם כִּשְׁא֣וֹל חַיִּ֑ים וּ֝תְמִימִ֗ים כְּי֣וֹרְדֵי בֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
let us swallow them up alive like She᾽ol; and whole, like those who go down into the pit:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let us swallow them [(the innocent)] up alive, like Sheol [(which swallows the body entire)], and whole [i.e., at the height of their prosperity], as those who descend to the pit [i.e., let us kill them and inherit their wealth],


###### Proverbs 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:13)


כׇּל־ה֣וֹן יָקָ֣ר נִמְצָ֑א נְמַלֵּ֖א בָתֵּ֣ינוּ שָׁלָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
we shall find all precious goods; we shall fill our houses with spoil:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All precious wealth is found [with them]; we will fill up our houses with spoil,


###### Proverbs 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:14)


גּ֭וֹרָ֣לְךָ תַּפִּ֣יל בְּתוֹכֵ֑נוּ כִּ֥יס אֶ֝חָ֗ד יִהְיֶ֥ה לְכֻלָּֽנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
throw in thy lot among us; we shall all have one purse:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cast your lot among us; let there be one pouch for all of us —


###### Proverbs 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:15)


בְּנִ֗י אַל־תֵּלֵ֣ךְ בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ אִתָּ֑ם מְנַ֥ע רַ֝גְלְךָ֗ מִנְּתִיבָתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
my son, do not walk in the way with them; restrain thy foot from their path:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My son, do not walk in the way with them; keep your feet from their path.


###### Proverbs 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:16)


כִּ֣י רַ֭גְלֵיהֶם לָרַ֣ע יָר֑וּצוּ וִ֝ימַהֲר֗וּ לִשְׁפׇּךְ־דָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for their feet run to evil, and they make haste to shed blood.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For their feet run to [their own] evil, and they [heedlessly] hasten to spill blood.


###### Proverbs 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:17)


כִּֽי־חִ֭נָּם מְזֹרָ֣ה הָרָ֑שֶׁת בְּ֝עֵינֵ֗י כׇּל־בַּ֥עַל כָּנָֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For no heed is taken of the net which is spread in the sight of any bird.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For [though] the net is spread [(with food morsels)] for naught [i.e., for no apparent reason] in the eyes of all winged creatures,


###### Proverbs 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:18)


וְ֭הֵם לְדָמָ֣ם יֶאֱרֹ֑בוּ יִ֝צְפְּנ֗וּ לְנַפְשֹׁתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they lie in wait for their blood; they lurk for their lives.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They [(the hunters)] are lying in wait for their blood and lurking for their lives.


###### Proverbs 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:19)


כֵּ֗ן אׇ֭רְחוֹת כׇּל־בֹּ֣צֵֽעַ בָּ֑צַע אֶת־נֶ֖פֶשׁ בְּעָלָ֣יו יִקָּֽח׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So are the ways of everyone who is greedy of gain; it takes away the life of its owner.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So, [(as with the birds)] are the ways of all committers of theft [(The "morsels" are tempting to them, and easily attainable, but, in the end,)] it [the theft] takes the life of its owner [i.e., the thief].


###### Proverbs 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:20)


חׇ֭כְמוֹת בַּח֣וּץ תָּרֹ֑נָּה בָּ֝רְחֹב֗וֹת תִּתֵּ֥ן קוֹלָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wisdom cries aloud in the street; she utters her voice in the squares:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wisdom [of Torah] sings in the thoroughfare [i.e., in the house of study]; in the expanses [i.e., in the places where Torah is "expanded upon,"] it lifts its voice.


###### Proverbs 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:21)


בְּרֹ֥אשׁ הֹמִיּ֗וֹת תִּ֫קְרָ֥א בְּפִתְחֵ֖י שְׁעָרִ֥ים בָּעִ֗יר אֲמָרֶ֥יהָ תֹאמֵֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
she cries in the chief place of concourse, at the entrances of the gates: in the city she utters her words, saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the fore of [its] din, it calls out, at the entrances of the gates [(where the elders sit)]. It spreads its words throughout the city, [saying:]


###### Proverbs 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:22)


עַד־מָתַ֣י׀פְּתָיִם֮ תְּֽאֵהֲב֫וּ־פֶ֥תִי וְלֵצִ֗ים לָ֭צוֹן חָמְד֣וּ לָהֶ֑ם וּ֝כְסִילִ֗ים יִשְׂנְאוּ־דָֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How long, you simple ones, will you love being simple? and how long, O scorners, will you delight in scoffing, and fools hate knowledge?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How long, O simpletons, will you love deception, and scoffers lust mockery, and fools hate wisdom?


###### Proverbs 1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:23)


תָּשׁ֗וּבוּ לְֽת֫וֹכַחְתִּ֥י הִנֵּ֤ה אַבִּ֣יעָה לָכֶ֣ם רוּחִ֑י אוֹדִ֖יעָה דְבָרַ֣י אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
turn at my reproof: behold, I will pour out my spirit to you, I will make known to you my words.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Return to my reproof. Do I not speak my spirit to you and make known my words to you?


###### Proverbs 1:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:24)


יַ֣עַן קָ֭רָאתִי וַתְּמָאֵ֑נוּ נָטִ֥יתִי יָ֝דִ֗י וְאֵ֣ין מַקְשִֽׁיב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Because I have called, and you refused; I have stretched out my hand, and no man regarded;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(But)] because I have called and you have refused; because I have stretched forth my arm [(to signal you to turn to me)], and you have not listened,


###### Proverbs 1:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:25)


וַתִּפְרְע֥וּ כׇל־עֲצָתִ֑י וְ֝תוֹכַחְתִּ֗י לֹ֣א אֲבִיתֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but you have set at nought all my counsel, and would none of my reproof:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you have voided all of my counsel [(whose purpose was to exalt you in the world)], and did not desire my reproof,


###### Proverbs 1:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:26)


גַּם־אֲ֭נִי בְּאֵידְכֶ֣ם אֶשְׂחָ֑ק אֶ֝לְעַ֗ג בְּבֹ֣א פַחְדְּכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I also will laugh at your calamity; I will mock when your fear comes upon you;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I, likewise, will laugh at your misfortune and make mock when your fear arrives.


###### Proverbs 1:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:27)


בְּבֹ֤א (כשאוה) [כְשׁוֹאָ֨ה**׀**] פַּחְדְּכֶ֗ם וְֽ֭אֵידְכֶם כְּסוּפָ֣ה יֶאֱתֶ֑ה בְּבֹ֥א עֲ֝לֵיכֶ֗ם צָרָ֥ה וְצוּקָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when your fear comes like a storm, and your calamity comes like a tempest; when distress and anguish come upon you.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When your fear comes as a [sudden] cloud, and your misfortune, as a whirlwind; when affliction and oppression come upon you,


###### Proverbs 1:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:28)


אָ֣ז יִ֭קְרָאֻנְנִי וְלֹ֣א אֶעֱנֶ֑ה יְ֝שַׁחֲרֻ֗נְנִי וְלֹ֣א יִמְצָאֻֽנְנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then shall they call upon me, but I will not answer; they shall seek me early, but they will not find me:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then they will call me, and I will not answer; they will search for me, but they will not find me.


###### Proverbs 1:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:29)


תַּ֭חַת כִּֽי־שָׂ֣נְאוּ דָ֑עַת וְיִרְאַ֥ת יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה לֹ֣א בָחָֽרוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for they hated knowledge, and did not choose the fear of the Lord:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Because they hated wisdom and did not choose to fear the L-rd.


###### Proverbs 1:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:30)


לֹא־אָב֥וּ לַעֲצָתִ֑י נָ֝אֲצ֗וּ כׇּל־תּוֹכַחְתִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
they would none of my counsel: they despised all my reproof.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They did not desire my counsel; they spurned all of my reproof.


###### Proverbs 1:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:31)


וְֽ֭יֹאכְלוּ מִפְּרִ֣י דַרְכָּ֑ם וּֽמִמֹּעֲצֹ֖תֵיהֶ֣ם יִשְׂבָּֽעוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore they shall eat of the fruit of their own way, and be filled with their own devices.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They will eat the fruits of their ways and will be sated with their counsels [(by afflictions in this world; and the principal will be guarded for them in Gehinnom)].


###### Proverbs 1:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:32)


כִּ֤י מְשׁוּבַ֣ת פְּתָיִ֣ם תַּהַרְגֵ֑ם וְשַׁלְוַ֖ת כְּסִילִ֣ים תְּאַבְּדֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the turning away of the simple shall slay them, and the erring of fools shall destroy them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the waywardness of the simpletons will slay them, and the smugness of the fools [(in seeing the prosperity of the wicked and following in their footsteps)] will cause them to go lost.


###### Proverbs 1:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/1:33)


וְשֹׁמֵ֣עַֽ לִ֭י יִשְׁכׇּן־בֶּ֑טַח וְ֝שַׁאֲנַ֗ן מִפַּ֥חַד רָעָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he who listens to me shall dwell safely, and shall be at ease without fear of evil.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But he who listens to me will rest secure [in this world], and in quiet [(in the world to come)] from the fright of evil [(the judgment of Gehinnom)].



## Chapter 2

###### Proverbs 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:1)


בְּ֭נִי אִם־תִּקַּ֣ח אֲמָרָ֑י וּ֝מִצְוֺתַ֗י תִּצְפֹּ֥ן אִתָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, if thou wilt receive my words, and treasure up my commandments with thee;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[The L-rd says: I will deem you] My son if you take My words [to heart] and store up My mitzvoth with you.


###### Proverbs 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:2)


לְהַקְשִׁ֣יב לַחׇכְמָ֣ה אׇזְנֶ֑ךָ תַּטֶּ֥ה לִ֝בְּךָ֗ לַתְּבוּנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so that thou incline thy ear to wisdom, and apply thy heart to understanding indeed,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[My words are] that your ears listen to wisdom [Torah] and incline your heart to understanding.


###### Proverbs 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:3)


כִּ֤י אִ֣ם לַבִּינָ֣ה תִקְרָ֑א לַ֝תְּבוּנָ֗ה תִּתֵּ֥ן קוֹלֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if thou criest after wisdom, and liftest up thy voice for understanding;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you call out to understanding, when you put forth your voice for understanding —


###### Proverbs 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:4)


אִם־תְּבַקְשֶׁ֥נָּה כַכָּ֑סֶף וְֽכַמַּטְמוֹנִ֥ים תַּחְפְּשֶֽׂנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if thou seekest her like silver, and searchest for her as for hidden treasures;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you seek for it as silver, if you search for it as hidden treasure —


###### Proverbs 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:5)


אָ֗ז תָּ֭בִין יִרְאַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֑ה וְדַ֖עַת אֱלֹהִ֣ים תִּמְצָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then shalt thou understand the fear of the Lord, and find the knowledge of God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you will understand the fear of the L-rd and find the knowledge of G-d.


###### Proverbs 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:6)


כִּֽי־יְ֭הֹוָה יִתֵּ֣ן חׇכְמָ֑ה מִ֝פִּ֗יו דַּ֣עַת וּתְבוּנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the Lord gives wisdom: out of his mouth come knowledge and understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(And this, indeed, warrants the effort of acquisition)] for the L-rd [Himself] gives wisdom; from His mouth is knowledge and understanding.


###### Proverbs 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:7)


(וצפן) [יִצְפֹּ֣ן] לַ֭יְשָׁרִים תּוּשִׁיָּ֑ה מָ֝גֵ֗ן לְהֹ֣לְכֵי תֹֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He lays up sound wisdom for the righteous: he is a shield to those who walk uprightly,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He hid Torah for the just [(He secreted it with Himself for twenty-six generations until He gave it to the generation of the desert.)] It is a shield for those who walk in innocence,


###### Proverbs 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:8)


לִ֭נְצֹר אׇרְח֣וֹת מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וְדֶ֖רֶךְ חֲסִידָ֣ו יִשְׁמֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to keep the paths of justice; and he preserves the way of his pious ones.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[to teach them] to observe the paths of justice; and He will guard the way of His saints [(that they not stumble)].


###### Proverbs 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:9)


אָ֗ז תָּ֭בִין צֶ֣דֶק וּמִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּ֝מֵישָׁרִ֗ים כׇּל־מַעְגַּל־טֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then shalt thou understand righteousness, and justice and equity, every good path:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you will understand charity, justice, and justness [viz. 1:3] — every good path.


###### Proverbs 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:10)


כִּֽי־תָב֣וֹא חׇכְמָ֣ה בְלִבֶּ֑ךָ וְ֝דַ֗עַת לְֽנַפְשְׁךָ֥ יִנְעָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for wisdom shall enter thy heart, and knowledge be pleasant to thy soul;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When wisdom enters your heart, and knowledge is pleasant to your soul,


###### Proverbs 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:11)


מְ֭זִמָּה תִּשְׁמֹ֥ר עָלֶ֗יךָ תְּבוּנָ֥ה תִנְצְרֶֽכָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
discretion shall preserve thee, understanding shall keep thee:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Torah will watch over you; understanding will guard you.


###### Proverbs 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:12)


לְ֭הַצִּ֣ילְךָ מִדֶּ֣רֶךְ רָ֑ע מֵ֝אִ֗ישׁ מְדַבֵּ֥ר תַּהְפֻּכֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to deliver thee from the way of the evil man, from the man who speaks perverse things;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To save you from the way of evil, from the man who speaks contraries.


###### Proverbs 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:13)


הַ֭עֹ֣זְבִים אׇרְח֣וֹת יֹ֑שֶׁר לָ֝לֶ֗כֶת בְּדַרְכֵי־חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who leave the paths of uprightness, to walk in the ways of darkness;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From those who forsake just paths to walk in ways of darkness.


###### Proverbs 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:14)


הַ֭שְּׂמֵחִים לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת רָ֑ע יָ֝גִ֗ילוּ בְּֽתַהְפֻּכ֥וֹת רָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who rejoice to do evil, and delight in the perverseness of the wicked;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who are happy in the doing of evil, who rejoice in evil contrariness,


###### Proverbs 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:15)


אֲשֶׁ֣ר אׇרְחֹתֵיהֶ֣ם עִקְּשִׁ֑ים וּ֝נְלוֹזִ֗ים בְּמַעְגְּלוֹתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
whose ways are crooked, and who are underhand in their paths:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Whose ways are crooked and who are crooked in their ways —


###### Proverbs 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:16)


לְ֭הַצִּ֣ילְךָ מֵאִשָּׁ֣ה זָרָ֑ה מִ֝נׇּכְרִיָּ֗ה אֲמָרֶ֥יהָ הֶחֱלִֽיקָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to deliver thee from the strange woman, from the alien woman who speaks so smoothly;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To save you from the strange woman [(idolatry)], from the foreign woman, who smooths over her words.


###### Proverbs 2:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:17)


הַ֭עֹזֶבֶת אַלּ֣וּף נְעוּרֶ֑יהָ וְאֶת־בְּרִ֖ית אֱלֹהֶ֣יהָ שָׁכֵֽחָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who forsakes the friend of her youth, and forgets the covenant of her God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who forsakes the master [(the husband)] of her youth and forgets the covenant of her G-d.


###### Proverbs 2:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:18)


כִּ֤י שָׁ֣חָה אֶל־מָ֣וֶת בֵּיתָ֑הּ וְאֶל־רְ֝פָאִ֗ים מַעְגְּלֹתֶֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For her house inclines to death, and her paths to the dead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(reversion to 2:16)] for her house descends [i.e., causes you to descend] to death, and her paths, to the shades [(and Torah will guard you from this fall)].


###### Proverbs 2:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:19)


כׇּל־בָּ֭אֶיהָ לֹ֣א יְשׁוּב֑וּן וְלֹֽא־יַ֝שִּׂ֗יגוּ אׇרְח֥וֹת חַיִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
None that go to her return, nor do they regain the paths of life:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All who come to her will not return, and will not attain the paths of life.


###### Proverbs 2:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:20)


לְמַ֗עַן תֵּ֭לֵךְ בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ טוֹבִ֑ים וְאׇרְח֖וֹת צַדִּיקִ֣ים תִּשְׁמֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that thou mayst walk in the way of good men, and keep the paths of the righteous.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(reversion to 2:12)] So that you walk in the way of the good and heed the path of the righteous.


###### Proverbs 2:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:21)


כִּֽי־יְשָׁרִ֥ים יִשְׁכְּנוּ־אָ֑רֶץ וּ֝תְמִימִ֗ים יִוָּ֥תְרוּ בָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the upright shall dwell in the land, and the innocent shall remain in it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the just will dwell in the land [(in the world to come)], and the innocent will remain in it [(when the wicked descend to Gehinnom)].


###### Proverbs 2:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/2:22)


וּ֭רְשָׁעִים מֵאֶ֣רֶץ יִכָּרֵ֑תוּ וּ֝בוֹגְדִ֗ים יִסְּח֥וּ מִמֶּֽנָּה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the wicked shall be cut off from the earth, and the faithless shall be rooted out of it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the wicked will be cut off from the land, and the faithless uprooted from it.



## Chapter 3

###### Proverbs 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:1)


בְּ֭נִי תּוֹרָתִ֣י אַל־תִּשְׁכָּ֑ח וּ֝מִצְוֺתַ֗י יִצֹּ֥ר לִבֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, forget not my Tora; but let thy heart keep my commandments:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My son, do not forget my Torah, and let your heart guard my mitzvoth.


###### Proverbs 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:2)


כִּ֤י אֹ֣רֶךְ יָ֭מִים וּשְׁנ֣וֹת חַיִּ֑ים וְ֝שָׁל֗וֹם יוֹסִ֥יפוּ לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For length of days, and long life, and peace, shall they add to thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For length of days and years of life, and peace will they bequeath to you.


###### Proverbs 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:3)


חֶ֥סֶד וֶאֱמֶ֗ת אַֽל־יַ֫עַזְבֻ֥ךָ קׇשְׁרֵ֥ם עַל־גַּרְגְּרוֹתֶ֑יךָ כׇּ֝תְבֵ֗ם עַל־ל֥וּחַ לִבֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let not loyal love and truth forsake thee: bind them about thy neck; write them upon the table of thy heart:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let lovingkindness and truth not forsake you. Entwine them around your throat; inscribe them on the tablet of your heart.


###### Proverbs 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:4)


וּמְצָא־חֵ֥ן וְשֵֽׂכֶל־ט֑וֹב בְּעֵינֵ֖י אֱלֹהִ֣ים וְאָדָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so shalt thou find grace and good understanding in the sight of God and man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will find favor and [(be regarded as possessing)] goodly wisdom in the eyes of G-d and man.


###### Proverbs 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:5)


בְּטַ֣ח אֶל־יְ֭הֹוָה בְּכׇל־לִבֶּ֑ךָ וְאֶל־בִּ֥֝ינָתְךָ֗ אַל־תִּשָּׁעֵֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Trust in the Lord with all thy heart; and do not lean upon thy own understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Trust to the L-rd with all your heart [(and spend liberally to find a teacher to learn from)], and do not rely upon your own understanding.


###### Proverbs 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:6)


בְּכׇל־דְּרָכֶ֥יךָ דָעֵ֑הוּ וְ֝ה֗וּא יְיַשֵּׁ֥ר אֹֽרְחֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In all thy ways acknowledge him, and he shall direct thy paths.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In all of your ways know Him, and He will straighten your paths.


###### Proverbs 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:7)


אַל־תְּהִ֣י חָכָ֣ם בְּעֵינֶ֑יךָ יְרָ֥א אֶת־יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה וְס֣וּר מֵרָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Be not wise in thy own eyes: fear the Lord, and depart from evil.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not be wise in your own eyes [(to spurn the reproof of your chastisers)]; fear the L-rd and depart from evil.


###### Proverbs 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:8)


רִ֭פְאוּת תְּהִ֣י לְשׇׁרֶּ֑ךָ וְ֝שִׁקּ֗וּי לְעַצְמוֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It shall be health to thy navel, and marrow to thy bones.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For it [wisdom] is healing to your navel and marrow to your bones.


###### Proverbs 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:9)


כַּבֵּ֣ד אֶת־יְ֭הֹוָה מֵהוֹנֶ֑ךָ וּ֝מֵרֵאשִׁ֗ית כׇּל־תְּבוּאָתֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Honour the Lord with thy substance, and with the firstfruits of all thy increase:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Honor the L-rd from [all] that He has bequeathed to you [(even a pleasing voice)], and from the first of all your produce.


###### Proverbs 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:10)


וְיִמָּלְא֣וּ אֲסָמֶ֣יךָ שָׂבָ֑ע וְ֝תִיר֗וֹשׁ יְקָבֶ֥יךָ יִפְרֹֽצוּ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so shall thy barns be filled with plenty, and thy vats shall burst with new wine.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your silos will be filled to satiety, and your vats will burst forth with wine.


###### Proverbs 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:11)


מוּסַ֣ר יְ֭הֹוָה בְּנִ֣י אַל־תִּמְאָ֑ס וְאַל־תָּ֝קֹ֗ץ בְּתוֹכַחְתּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, do not despise the chastening of the Lord; nor be weary of his correction:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The afflictions of the L-rd, my son, do not despise, and do not reject His chastisement.


###### Proverbs 3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:12)


כִּ֤י אֶ֥ת־אֲשֶׁ֣ר יֶאֱהַ֣ב יְהֹוָ֣ה יוֹכִ֑יחַ וּ֝כְאָ֗ב אֶת־בֵּ֥ן יִרְצֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for the Lord reproves him whom he loves: even as a father the son in whom he delights.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For whom the L-rd loves, He chastises, and [(afterwards He conciliates him)] as a father conciliates [his] son.


###### Proverbs 3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:13)


אַשְׁרֵ֣י אָ֭דָם מָצָ֣א חׇכְמָ֑ה וְ֝אָדָ֗ם יָפִ֥יק תְּבוּנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Happy is the man who finds wisdom, and the man who gets understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Happy is the man who has found wisdom and the man who [is so conversant with it that he] "spouts" understanding.


###### Proverbs 3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:14)


כִּ֤י ט֣וֹב סַ֭חְרָהּ מִסְּחַר־כָּ֑סֶף וּ֝מֵחָר֗וּץ תְּבוּאָתָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the merchandise of it is better than the merchandise of silver, and its gain than fine gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For better is its commerce [i.e., its interchange] than the commerce of silver [(for in the latter instance, one takes one commodity, and the other, another; but in the instance of wisdom, both [(ideas)] remain with each)], and its produce is better than fine gold.


###### Proverbs 3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:15)


יְקָ֣רָה הִ֭יא (מפניים) [מִפְּנִינִ֑ים] וְכׇל־חֲ֝פָצֶ֗יךָ לֹ֣א יִֽשְׁווּ־בָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She is more precious than rubies: and all the things thou canst desire are not to be compared to her.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is more precious than pearls, and all of your desires do not equal it [(in worth)].


###### Proverbs 3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:16)


אֹ֣רֶךְ יָ֭מִים בִּֽימִינָ֑הּ בִּ֝שְׂמֹאולָ֗הּ עֹ֣שֶׁר וְכָבֽוֹד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Length of days is in her right hand; and in her left hand are riches and honour.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is length of days [(and, it goes without saying, wealth and honor)] in its right hand [i.e., to those who pursue it for its own sake]; wealth and honor [(but not length of days)] in its left hand [i.e., to those who do not pursue it for its own sake].


###### Proverbs 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:17)


דְּרָכֶ֥יהָ דַרְכֵי־נֹ֑עַם וְֽכׇל־נְתִ֖יבוֹתֶ֣יהָ שָׁלֽוֹם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Her ways are ways of pleasantness, and all her paths are peace.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Its ways are ways of pleasantness and all of its paths are peace.


###### Proverbs 3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:18)


עֵץ־חַיִּ֣ים הִ֭יא לַמַּחֲזִיקִ֣ים בָּ֑הּ וְֽתֹמְכֶ֥יהָ מְאֻשָּֽׁר׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She is a tree of life to those who lay hold on her: and happy are those who hold her fast.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is a tree of life to those who hold fast to it, and those who bolster it are fortunate.


###### Proverbs 3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:19)


יְֽהֹוָ֗ה בְּחׇכְמָ֥ה יָסַד־אָ֑רֶץ כּוֹנֵ֥ן שָׁ֝מַ֗יִם בִּתְבוּנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord by wisdom founded the earth; by understanding he estab­lished the heavens.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd with wisdom [Torah] founded the earth; He established the heavens with understanding [Torah].


###### Proverbs 3:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:20)


בְּ֭דַעְתּוֹ תְּהוֹמ֣וֹת נִבְקָ֑עוּ וּ֝שְׁחָקִ֗ים יִרְעֲפוּ־טָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By his knowledge the depths were broken up, and the clouds drop down dew.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Through its [Torah's] knowledge, the depths were cleaved, and the heavens dripped dew.


###### Proverbs 3:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:21)


בְּ֭נִי אַל־יָלֻ֣זוּ מֵעֵינֶ֑יךָ נְצֹ֥ר תֻּ֝שִׁיָּ֗ה וּמְזִמָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, let them not depart from thy eyes: keep sound wisdom and discretion:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Therefore, the entire world having been created through Torah,] do not let it depart from your eyes. Heed [it in] act and thought.


###### Proverbs 3:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:22)


וְיִֽהְי֣וּ חַיִּ֣ים לְנַפְשֶׁ֑ךָ וְ֝חֵ֗ן לְגַרְגְּרֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so shall they be life to thy soul, and grace to thy neck.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they [(the Torah's wisdom, knowledge, etc.)] will be life to your soul and grace to your throat.


###### Proverbs 3:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:23)


אָ֤ז תֵּלֵ֣ךְ לָבֶ֣טַח דַּרְכֶּ֑ךָ וְ֝רַגְלְךָ֗ לֹ֣א תִגּֽוֹף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then shalt thou walk in thy way safely, and thy foot shall not stumble.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you will walk your way securely and your foot will not stumble.


###### Proverbs 3:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:24)


אִם־תִּשְׁכַּ֥ב לֹֽא־תִפְחָ֑ד וְ֝שָׁכַבְתָּ֗ וְֽעָרְבָ֥ה שְׁנָתֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou liest down, thou shalt not be afraid: indeed, thou shalt lie down, and thy sleep shall be sweet.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you lie down, you will not fear, and when you recline, your sleep will be pleasant.


###### Proverbs 3:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:25)


אַל־תִּ֭ירָא מִפַּ֣חַד פִּתְאֹ֑ם וּמִשֹּׁאַ֥ת רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים כִּ֣י תָבֹֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Be not afraid of sudden fear, nor of the ruin of the wicked, when it comes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[For] you will not fear a sudden fright or the destruction of the wicked when it comes.


###### Proverbs 3:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:26)


כִּֽי־יְ֭הֹוָה יִֽהְיֶ֣ה בְכִסְלֶ֑ךָ וְשָׁמַ֖ר רַגְלְךָ֣ מִלָּֽכֶד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the Lord shall be thy confidence, and shall keep thy foot from being caught.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the L-rd will be with you in your trust, and He will guard your feet from entrapment.


###### Proverbs 3:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:27)


אַל־תִּמְנַע־ט֥וֹב מִבְּעָלָ֑יו בִּֽהְי֨וֹת לְאֵ֖ל (ידיך) [יָדְךָ֣] לַעֲשֽׂוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Withhold not good from those to whom it is due, when it is in the power of thy hand to do it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not withhold good from its owners [(i.e., if your neighbor wants to help the poor, do not stop him)] if you have the power to do [so].


###### Proverbs 3:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:28)


אַל־תֹּ֘אמַ֤ר (לרעיך) [לְרֵעֲךָ֨**׀**] לֵ֣ךְ וָ֭שׁוּב וּמָחָ֥ר אֶתֵּ֗ן וְיֵ֣שׁ אִתָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Say not to thy neighbour, Go, and come back, and to morrow I will give; when thou hast it by thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And] do not tell your [poor] friend: "Go, and return, and tomorrow I will give [it to you]," if you have it [now].


###### Proverbs 3:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:29)


אַל־תַּחֲרֹ֣שׁ עַל־רֵעֲךָ֣ רָעָ֑ה וְהֽוּא־יוֹשֵׁ֖ב לָבֶ֣טַח אִתָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not devise evil against thy neighbour, seeing he dwells securely by thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not plot evil against your friend. Does he not dwell trustingly with you?


###### Proverbs 3:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:30)


אַל־[תָּרִ֣יב] (תרוב) עִם־אָדָ֣ם חִנָּ֑ם אִם־לֹ֖א גְמָלְךָ֣ רָעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not strive with a man without cause, if he have done thee no harm.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not [bring a] quarrel [i.e., a complaint] upon a man in vain, if he has not done you evil [(it being written [Leviticus 19:18]: "And you shall love your fellow as yourself.")]


###### Proverbs 3:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:31)


אַל־תְּ֭קַנֵּא בְּאִ֣ישׁ חָמָ֑ס וְאַל־תִּ֝בְחַ֗ר בְּכׇל־דְּרָכָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not envy the oppressor, and choose none of his ways.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not envy the man of violence [(to do as he does if you see him prospering)], and do not choose any of his ways.


###### Proverbs 3:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:32)


כִּ֤י תוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֣ה נָל֑וֹז וְֽאֶת־יְשָׁרִ֥ים סוֹדֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the underhand man is abomination to the Lord: and he shares his counsel with the righteous.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the abomination of the L-rd is the crooked one, and His counsel is with the just.


###### Proverbs 3:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:33)


מְאֵרַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה בְּבֵ֣ית רָשָׁ֑ע וּנְוֵ֖ה צַדִּיקִ֣ים יְבָרֵֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The curse of the Lord is in the house of the wicked: but he blesses the habitation of the just.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The blight of the L-rd is in the house of the wicked, and He blesses the abode of the righteous.


###### Proverbs 3:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:34)


אִם־לַלֵּצִ֥ים הֽוּא־יָלִ֑יץ (ולעניים) [וְ֝לַעֲנָוִ֗ים] יִתֶּן־חֵֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely he scorns the scorners: but he gives grace to the humble.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If one follows scoffers, he [himself] will scoff with them, and if [he follows] the humble, he will give [i.e., project] favor [i.e., his deeds will find favor in the eyes of men].


###### Proverbs 3:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/3:35)


כָּ֭בוֹד חֲכָמִ֣ים יִנְחָ֑לוּ וּ֝כְסִילִ֗ים מֵרִ֥ים קָלֽוֹן׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wise shall inherit honour: but fools shall get shame.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise inherit honor and the fools reap shame [for themselves].



## Chapter 4

###### Proverbs 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:1)


שִׁמְע֣וּ בָ֭נִים מ֣וּסַר אָ֑ב וְ֝הַקְשִׁ֗יבוּ לָדַ֥עַת בִּינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear, you children, the instruction of a father, and attend to know understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear, children, the Father's mussar, and listen to know understanding.


###### Proverbs 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:2)


כִּ֤י לֶ֣קַח ט֭וֹב נָתַ֣תִּי לָכֶ֑ם תּ֝וֹרָתִ֗י אַֽל־תַּעֲזֹֽבוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I give you good doctrine, forsake not my Tora.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"For I [(says the prophet, speaking in the name of the L-rd)] have given you a goodly acquisition; do not forsake My Torah."


###### Proverbs 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:3)


כִּֽי־בֵ֭ן הָיִ֣יתִי לְאָבִ֑י רַ֥ךְ וְ֝יָחִ֗יד לִפְנֵ֥י אִמִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I was my father’s son, tender and the only one in the sight of my mother.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For I was a son to my Father [viz. II Samuel 7:14], a tender and only (child) before my mother [i.e., select and beloved by my people].


###### Proverbs 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:4)


וַיֹּרֵ֗נִי וַיֹּ֥אמֶר לִ֗י יִֽתְמׇךְ־דְּבָרַ֥י לִבֶּ֑ךָ שְׁמֹ֖ר מִצְוֺתַ֣י וֶחְיֵֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He taught me also, and said to me, Let thy heart hold fast to my words: keep my commandments, and live.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He taught me and He said to me: My words will sustain your heart; heed My mitzvoth and live.


###### Proverbs 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:5)


קְנֵ֣ה חׇ֭כְמָה קְנֵ֣ה בִינָ֑ה אַל־תִּשְׁכַּ֥ח וְאַל־תֵּ֝֗ט מֵאִמְרֵי־פִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Get wisdom, get understanding: do not forget it; nor decline from the words of my mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Acquire wisdom; acquire understanding. Do not forget and do not stray from the words of My mouth.


###### Proverbs 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:6)


אַל־תַּעַזְבֶ֥הָ וְתִשְׁמְרֶ֑ךָּ אֱהָבֶ֥הָ וְתִצְּרֶֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not forsake her, and she shall preserve thee: love her, and she shall keep thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not forsake it [Torah], and it will guard you; love it, and it will protect you.


###### Proverbs 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:7)


רֵאשִׁ֣ית חׇ֭כְמָה קְנֵ֣ה חׇכְמָ֑ה וּבְכׇל־קִ֝נְיָנְךָ֗ קְנֵ֣ה בִינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The beginning of wisdom is, Get wisdom: therefore use all your means to acquire understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The first of [your] wisdom [is to] acquire wisdom [from your teacher], and [afterwards,] in all of your acquisitions, acquire understanding [(of one thing from another)].


###### Proverbs 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:8)


סַלְסְלֶ֥הָ וּֽתְרוֹמְמֶ֑ךָּ תְּ֝כַבֵּ֗דְךָ כִּ֣י תְחַבְּקֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Exalt her, and she shall promote thee: she will bring thee to honour, when thou dost embrace her.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Search it [(wisdom)] out and it will uplift you; it will honor you when you embrace it.


###### Proverbs 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:9)


תִּתֵּ֣ן לְ֭רֹאשְׁךָ לִוְיַת־חֵ֑ן עֲטֶ֖רֶת תִּפְאֶ֣רֶת תְּמַגְּנֶֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She shall give to thy head an ornament of grace: a crown of glory shall she bestow on thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It will set upon your head a chaplet of grace; it will bestow a crown of glory upon you.


###### Proverbs 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:10)


שְׁמַ֣ע בְּ֭נִי וְקַ֣ח אֲמָרָ֑י וְיִרְבּ֥וּ לְ֝ךָ֗ שְׁנ֣וֹת חַיִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear, O my son, and receive my sayings; and the years of thy life shall be many.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear my son and take My words, and they will add years of life unto you.


###### Proverbs 4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:11)


בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ חׇ֭כְמָה הֹרֵיתִ֑יךָ הִ֝דְרַכְתִּ֗יךָ בְּמַעְגְּלֵי־יֹֽשֶׁר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have taught thee in the way of wisdom; I have led thee in paths of rectitude.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have instructed you in the way of wisdom; I have led you in just paths.


###### Proverbs 4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:12)


בְּֽ֭לֶכְתְּךָ לֹא־יֵצַ֣ר צַעֲדֶ֑ךָ וְאִם־תָּ֝ר֗וּץ לֹ֣א תִכָּשֵֽׁל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou goest, thy steps shall not be confined; and when thou runnest, thou shalt not stumble.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you walk, your steps will not be constricted [(so that you will not fall)], and when you run, you will not stumble.


###### Proverbs 4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:13)


הַחֲזֵ֣ק בַּמּוּסָ֣ר אַל־תֶּ֑רֶף נִ֝צְּרֶ֗הָ כִּי־הִ֥יא חַיֶּֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take fast hold of instruction; let her not go: keep her; for she is thy life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hold fast to mussar [(Torah)]; do not let it go. Guard it, for it is your life.


###### Proverbs 4:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:14)


בְּאֹ֣רַח רְ֭שָׁעִים אַל־תָּבֹ֑א וְאַל־תְּ֝אַשֵּׁ֗ר בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ רָעִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not enter into the path of the wicked, and do not go in the way of evil men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not come to the path of the wicked, and do not tread in the way of the evildoers.


###### Proverbs 4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:15)


פְּרָעֵ֥הוּ אַל־תַּעֲבׇר־בּ֑וֹ שְׂטֵ֖ה מֵעָלָ֣יו וַעֲבֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Avoid it, do not pass by it, turn from it, and pass on.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Shun it; do not cross it. Veer away from it and pass on.


###### Proverbs 4:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:16)


כִּ֤י לֹ֣א יִֽ֭שְׁנוּ אִם־לֹ֣א יָרֵ֑עוּ וְֽנִגְזְלָ֥ה שְׁ֝נָתָ֗ם אִם־לֹ֥א (יכשולו) [יַכְשִֽׁילוּ]׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For they sleep not, unless they have done mischief; and their sleep is taken away, unless they cause some to fall.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For they [(those who walk upon it)] do not sleep if they do no evil; their sleep is stolen if they cause no stumbling.


###### Proverbs 4:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:17)


כִּ֣י לָ֭חֲמוּ לֶ֣חֶם רֶ֑שַׁע וְיֵ֖ין חֲמָסִ֣ים יִשְׁתּֽוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For they eat the bread of wickedness, and drink the wine of violence.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For they eat the bread of wickedness and drink the wine of violence.


###### Proverbs 4:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:18)


וְאֹ֣רַח צַ֭דִּיקִים כְּא֣וֹר נֹ֑גַהּ הוֹלֵ֥ךְ וָ֝א֗וֹר עַד־נְכ֥וֹן הַיּֽוֹם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the path of just men is like the gleam of sunlight, that shines ever more brightly until the height of noonday.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But the path of the righteous is as the shining light [of day], growing in brightness until the day is fast [i.e., until mid-day].


###### Proverbs 4:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:19)


דֶּ֣רֶךְ רְ֭שָׁעִים כָּאֲפֵלָ֑ה לֹ֥א יָ֝דְע֗וּ בַּמֶּ֥ה יִכָּשֵֽׁלוּ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The way of the wicked is like darkness: they know not at what they stumble.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But the way of the wicked is as pitch darkness; they know not what they stumble on.


###### Proverbs 4:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:20)


בְּ֭נִי לִדְבָרַ֣י הַקְשִׁ֑יבָה לַ֝אֲמָרַ֗י הַט־אׇזְנֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, attend to my words; incline thy ear to my sayings.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My son, listen to My words; incline your ear to My exhortations.


###### Proverbs 4:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:21)


אַל־יַלִּ֥יזוּ מֵעֵינֶ֑יךָ שׇׁ֝מְרֵ֗ם בְּת֣וֹךְ לְבָבֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let them not depart from thy eyes; keep them in the midst of thy heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let them not depart from your eyes; guard them within your heart.


###### Proverbs 4:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:22)


כִּֽי־חַיִּ֣ים הֵ֭ם לְמֹצְאֵיהֶ֑ם וּֽלְכׇל־בְּשָׂר֥וֹ מַרְפֵּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For they are life to those who find them, and health to all their flesh.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For they are life to him who finds them, and healing to all his flesh.


###### Proverbs 4:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:23)


מִֽכׇּל־מִ֭שְׁמָר נְצֹ֣ר לִבֶּ֑ךָ כִּֽי־מִ֝מֶּ֗נּוּ תּוֹצְא֥וֹת חַיִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Keep thy heart with the greatest vigilance; for out of it are the issues of life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From every guarding [(From everything of which the Torah said: "Guard yourself")], guard your heart [(whether it be a slight or a severe transgression)], for from it [(i.e., even from the seemingly slight one)] are the issues [(reward and punishment, respectively,)] of life.


###### Proverbs 4:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:24)


הָסֵ֣ר מִ֭מְּךָ עִקְּשׁ֣וּת פֶּ֑ה וּלְז֥וּת שְׂ֝פָתַ֗יִם הַרְחֵ֥ק מִמֶּֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Put away from thee a dissembling mouth, and perverse lips put far from thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remove from yourself crooking of the mouth [(of others in derision of your deeds)]; and widening of the lips [in condemnation of you] keep far from you.


###### Proverbs 4:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:25)


עֵ֭ינֶיךָ לְנֹ֣כַח יַבִּ֑יטוּ וְ֝עַפְעַפֶּ֗יךָ יַיְשִׁ֥רוּ נֶגְדֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let thy eyes look right on, and let thy eyelids look straight before thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let your eyes look before you [(for the straight and the true)], and your lids will straighten [the path] before you.


###### Proverbs 4:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:26)


פַּ֭לֵּס מַעְגַּ֣ל רַגְלֶ֑ךָ וְֽכׇל־דְּרָכֶ֥יךָ יִכֹּֽנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Make even the path of thy foot, and let all thy ways be firm.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Weigh the path of your feet [(the loss of a mitzvah against its reward; the "reward" of a transgression against its omission)], and all of your ways will be [correctly] established.


###### Proverbs 4:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/4:27)


אַֽל־תֵּט־יָמִ֥ין וּשְׂמֹ֑אול הָסֵ֖ר רַגְלְךָ֣ מֵרָֽע׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not turn to the right hand or to the left: remove thy foot from evil.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not turn right or left [(from the just weight)]; remove your feet from evil.



## Chapter 5

###### Proverbs 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:1)


בְּ֭נִי לְחׇכְמָתִ֣י הַקְשִׁ֑יבָה לִ֝תְבוּנָתִ֗י הַט־אׇזְנֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, attend to my wisdom, and bend thy ear to my understanding:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My son, be attentive to my wisdom; give ear to my understanding,


###### Proverbs 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:2)


לִשְׁמֹ֥ר מְזִמּ֑וֹת וְ֝דַ֗עַת שְׂפָתֶ֥יךָ יִנְצֹֽרוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that thou mayst preserve discretion, and that thy lips may keep knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To heed thoughts — and your lips will guard wisdom.


###### Proverbs 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:3)


כִּ֤י נֹ֣פֶת תִּ֭טֹּפְנָה שִׂפְתֵ֣י זָרָ֑ה וְחָלָ֖ק מִשֶּׁ֣מֶן חִכָּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the lips of a strange woman drip honey, and her mouth is smoother than oil:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the lips of the strange woman [(heresy)] drip as a honeycomb, and her palate is smoother than oil.


###### Proverbs 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:4)


וְֽ֭אַחֲרִיתָהּ מָרָ֣ה כַֽלַּעֲנָ֑ה חַ֝דָּ֗ה כְּחֶ֣רֶב פִּיּֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but her end is bitter as wormwood, sharp as a two edged sword.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But her end is bitter as wormwood, as sharp as a two-edged sword.


###### Proverbs 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:5)


רַ֭גְלֶיהָ יֹרְד֣וֹת מָ֑וֶת שְׁ֝א֗וֹל צְעָדֶ֥יהָ יִתְמֹֽכוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Her feet go down to death: her steps take hold of She᾽ol.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Her footpaths lead to death; her steps approach Sheol.


###### Proverbs 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:6)


אֹ֣רַח חַ֭יִּים פֶּן־תְּפַלֵּ֑ס נָע֥וּ מַ֝עְגְּלֹתֶ֗יהָ לֹ֣א תֵדָֽע׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She is far from measuring her way of life; her paths wander, and she is ignorant.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not weigh the path of life [(i.e., which path to take) by the standards of "the strange woman," for] her paths terminate [in Sheol, and] you will not know [(to take heed until you have fallen there)].


###### Proverbs 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:7)


וְעַתָּ֣ה בָ֭נִים שִׁמְעוּ־לִ֑י וְאַל־תָּ֝ס֗וּרוּ מֵאִמְרֵי־פִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear me now therefore, O children, and do not depart from the words of my mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, [my] sons, listen to me, and do not depart from the words of my mouth.


###### Proverbs 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:8)


הַרְחֵ֣ק מֵעָלֶ֣יהָ דַרְכֶּ֑ךָ וְאַל־תִּ֝קְרַ֗ב אֶל־פֶּ֥תַח בֵּיתָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Remove thy way far from her, and do not come near the door of her house:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remove your ways far from her, and do not come close to the doors of her house.


###### Proverbs 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:9)


פֶּן־תִּתֵּ֣ן לַאֲחֵרִ֣ים הוֹדֶ֑ךָ וּ֝שְׁנֹתֶ֗יךָ לְאַכְזָרִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
lest thou give thy vigour to others, and thy years to the cruel one:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lest you give your glory to others, [i.e., lest you turn your heart to other gods)], and your [remaining] years to the cruel one [(the prince of Gehinnom)].


###### Proverbs 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:10)


פֶּֽן־יִשְׂבְּע֣וּ זָרִ֣ים כֹּחֶ֑ךָ וַ֝עֲצָבֶ֗יךָ בְּבֵ֣ית נׇכְרִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
lest strangers be filled with thy strength; and thy labours go to the house of a stranger:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lest strangers [(the prophets of the ba'al)] be sated with your strength [(the money they extort from you by their falsehoods)], and [the products of] your toil [be found] in the house of a strange [god].


###### Proverbs 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:11)


וְנָהַמְתָּ֥ בְאַחֲרִיתֶ֑ךָ בִּכְל֥וֹת בְּ֝שָׂרְךָ֗ וּשְׁאֵרֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou moan when thy end comes, when thy flesh and thy body are consumed,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will make moan in your latter end, when your flesh and form fall away.


###### Proverbs 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:12)


וְֽאָמַרְתָּ֗ אֵ֭יךְ שָׂנֵ֣אתִי מוּסָ֑ר וְ֝תוֹכַ֗חַת נָאַ֥ץ לִבִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou say, How have I hated instruction, and my heart despised reproof!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will say: "How could I have hated mussar and my heart spurned rebuke?"


###### Proverbs 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:13)


וְֽלֹא־שָׁ֭מַעְתִּי בְּק֣וֹל מוֹרָ֑י וְ֝לִֽמְלַמְּדַ֗י לֹא־הִטִּ֥יתִי אׇזְנִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I have not obeyed the voice of my teachers, nor bent my ear to those who instructed me!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"And [how could] I not have listened to the voice of my masters and not inclined my ear to my teachers?"


###### Proverbs 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:14)


כִּ֭מְעַט הָיִ֣יתִי בְכׇל־רָ֑ע בְּת֖וֹךְ קָהָ֣ל וְעֵדָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I was almost in all evil in the midst of the congregation and assembly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"There is but a step between me and all evil [(Gehinnom)] in the midst of congregation and assembly."


###### Proverbs 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:15)


שְׁתֵה־מַ֥יִם מִבּוֹרֶ֑ךָ וְ֝נֹזְלִ֗ים מִתּ֥וֹךְ בְּאֵרֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Drink waters out of thy own cistern, and running waters out of thy own well.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Drink [(gathered)] water from your pit [(the Torah of Moses)], and [(in consequence,)] flowing water from your well, [and, in consequence of that)],


###### Proverbs 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:16)


יָפ֣וּצוּ מַעְיְנֹתֶ֣יךָ ח֑וּצָה בָּ֝רְחֹב֗וֹת פַּלְגֵי־מָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So will thy spring be dispersed abroad, and streams of water will flow in the broad places.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your springs will spread outwards [i.e., you will acquire many disciples]. In the thoroughfares [of the city] the streams of [your] waters will spread.


###### Proverbs 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:17)


יִֽהְיוּ־לְךָ֥ לְבַדֶּ֑ךָ וְאֵ֖ין לְזָרִ֣ים אִתָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let them be only thy own, and not strangers’ with thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They [(the waters)] will be yours alone, strangers not sharing them with you [(as opposed to 5:10), and, in consequence],


###### Proverbs 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:18)


יְהִֽי־מְקוֹרְךָ֥ בָר֑וּךְ וּ֝שְׂמַ֗ח מֵאֵ֥שֶׁת נְעוּרֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let thy fountain be blessed: and rejoice with the wife of thy youth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your source will be blessed and you will rejoice in the wife of your youth [(i.e., in the Torah that you learned from your youth)].


###### Proverbs 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:19)


אַיֶּ֥לֶת אֲהָבִ֗ים וְֽיַ֫עֲלַת־חֵ֥ן דַּ֭דֶּיהָ יְרַוֻּ֣ךָ בְכׇל־עֵ֑ת בְּ֝אַהֲבָתָ֗הּ תִּשְׁגֶּ֥ה תָמִֽיד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A loving hind and a pleasant roe; let her breasts satisfy thee at all times; and be thou ravished always with her love.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[She will be to you] a beloved hind, of excellent favor; her breasts will sate you at all times; in their love, you will stray always [(from your other affairs, and she will guard them for you)].


###### Proverbs 5:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:20)


וְלָ֤מָּה תִשְׁגֶּ֣ה בְנִ֣י בְזָרָ֑ה וּ֝תְחַבֵּ֗ק חֵ֣ק נׇכְרִיָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And why wilt thou, my son, be ravished with a strange woman, and embrace the bosom of an alien?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why, then, my son, should you stray with a strange woman and embrace the lap of a woman who is foreign to you?


###### Proverbs 5:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:21)


כִּ֤י נֹ֨כַח**׀** עֵינֵ֣י יְ֭הֹוָה דַּרְכֵי־אִ֑ישׁ וְֽכׇל־מַעְגְּלֹתָ֥יו מְפַלֵּֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the ways of man are before the eyes of the Lord, and he ponders all his goings.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For before the eyes of the L-rd are the ways of a man, and He weighs all of his paths [(for transgression or merit)].


###### Proverbs 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:22)


עֲֽווֹנֹתָ֗יו יִלְכְּדֻנ֥וֹ אֶת־הָרָשָׁ֑ע וּבְחַבְלֵ֥י חַ֝טָּאת֗וֹ יִתָּמֵֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His own iniquities shall trap the wicked man, and he shall be caught fast in the cords of his sins.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The transgressions of the wicked one will entrap him, and he will be hoisted in the cords of his sin.


###### Proverbs 5:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/5:23)


ה֗וּא יָ֭מוּת בְּאֵ֣ין מוּסָ֑ר וּבְרֹ֖ב אִוַּלְתּ֣וֹ יִשְׁגֶּֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall die for want of instruction; and in the greatness of his folly he shall go astray.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will die for refusal of chastisement, and for the abundance of his foolishness in which he has strayed.



## Chapter 6

###### Proverbs 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:1)


בְּ֭נִי אִם־עָרַ֣בְתָּ לְרֵעֶ֑ךָ תָּקַ֖עְתָּ לַזָּ֣ר כַּפֶּֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, if thou be surety for thy friend, if thou hast struck thy palms for a stranger,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My son, if you have gone security for your friend; if you have put forth your hand [in pledge] for a stranger,


###### Proverbs 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:2)


נוֹקַ֥שְׁתָּ בְאִמְרֵי־פִ֑יךָ נִ֝לְכַּ֗דְתָּ בְּאִמְרֵי־פִֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if thou art snared with the words of thy mouth, caught with the sayings of thy mouth:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Or if] you have been trapped by the words of your mouth, you have been ensnared by the words of your mouth.


###### Proverbs 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:3)


עֲשֵׂ֨ה זֹ֥את אֵפ֪וֹא׀בְּנִ֡י וְֽהִנָּצֵ֗ל כִּ֘י־בָ֤אתָ בְכַף־רֵעֶ֑ךָ לֵ֥ךְ הִ֝תְרַפֵּ֗ס וּרְהַ֥ב רֵעֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
do this now, my son, and deliver thyself, when thou art come into the hand of thy neighbour; go, abase thyself, and importune thy neighbour.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do this, then, my son, and be saved; for you have fallen into your friend's hand. Go and free [your] palm [i.e., pay him his money]; and [(if you owe him no money, but "have been trapped by the words of your mouth," to speak harshly against him,)] then multiply your friends [i.e., send many friends to him to ask him to forgive you].


###### Proverbs 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:4)


אַל־תִּתֵּ֣ן שֵׁנָ֣ה לְעֵינֶ֑יךָ וּ֝תְנוּמָ֗ה לְעַפְעַפֶּֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Give no sleep to thy eyes, nor slumber to thy eyelids.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Give no sleep to your eyes or slumber to your lids.


###### Proverbs 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:5)


הִ֭נָּצֵל כִּצְבִ֣י מִיָּ֑ד וּ֝כְצִפּ֗וֹר מִיַּ֥ד יָקֽוּשׁ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Deliver thyself like a gazelle from the hand of the hunter, and like a bird from the hand of the fowler.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Rescue yourself [from there] as the deer from the hand [of the hunter], and as the bird from the [fowler's] snare.


###### Proverbs 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:6)


לֵֽךְ־אֶל־נְמָלָ֥ה עָצֵ֑ל רְאֵ֖ה דְרָכֶ֣יהָ וַחֲכָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Go to the ant, thou sluggard; consider her ways, and be wise:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Go to the ant, you sluggard; see its ways and grow wise.


###### Proverbs 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:7)


אֲשֶׁ֖ר אֵֽין־לָ֥הּ קָצִ֗ין שֹׁטֵ֥ר וּמֹשֵֽׁל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
which having no guide, overseer, or ruler;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Though there is no officer, or guard, or ruler over her [to spur her on or to keep one ant from stealing from the other],


###### Proverbs 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:8)


תָּכִ֣ין בַּקַּ֣יִץ לַחְמָ֑הּ אָגְרָ֥ה בַ֝קָּצִ֗יר מַאֲכָלָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
provides her bread in the summer, and gathers her food in the harvest.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Still, she prepares her [own] food in the summer and stores up her [own] food in the harvest time.


###### Proverbs 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:9)


עַד־מָתַ֖י עָצֵ֥ל׀תִּשְׁכָּ֑ב מָ֝תַ֗י תָּק֥וּם מִשְּׁנָתֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How long wilt thou sleep, O sluggard? when wilt thou arise out of thy sleep?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How long will you recline, O sluggard? When will you arise from your sleep?


###### Proverbs 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:10)


מְעַ֣ט שֵׁ֭נוֹת מְעַ֣ט תְּנוּמ֑וֹת מְעַ֓ט**׀** חִבֻּ֖ק יָדַ֣יִם לִשְׁכָּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A little sleep, a little slumber, a little folding of the hands to sleep:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A little sleep, a little slumber, a little folding of the hands to recline,


###### Proverbs 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:11)


וּבָֽא־כִמְהַלֵּ֥ךְ רֵאשֶׁ֑ךָ וּ֝מַחְסֹרְךָ֗ כְּאִ֣ישׁ מָגֵֽן׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so shall thy poverty come like a marauder, and thy want like an armed man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your poverty [(in Torah)] will arrive as an unexpected guest, and your want as a shielded warrior [to defend his master].


###### Proverbs 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:12)


אָדָ֣ם בְּ֭לִיַּעַל אִ֣ישׁ אָ֑וֶן ה֝וֹלֵ֗ךְ עִקְּשׁ֥וּת פֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A base person, a wicked man, is one that walks with a crowed mouth,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The faithless man is a man of wrong, walking with contorted mouth [(in slanderous intent)].


###### Proverbs 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:13)


קֹרֵ֣ץ בְּ֭עֵינָו מֹלֵ֣ל בְּרַגְלָ֑ו מֹ֝רֶ֗ה בְּאֶצְבְּעֹתָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that winks with his eyes, that taps with his feet, that points with his fingers;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He winks with his eyes, "speaks" with his feet, points with his fingers.


###### Proverbs 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:14)


תַּ֥הְפֻּכ֨וֹת**׀** בְּלִבּ֗וֹ חֹרֵ֣שׁ רָ֣ע בְּכׇל־עֵ֑ת (מדנים) [מִדְיָנִ֥ים] יְשַׁלֵּֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
perverseness is in his heart; he devises mischief continually; he sows discord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There are contraries in his heart. He plots evil all the time and stirs up strife [(between man and his Creator)].


###### Proverbs 6:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:15)


עַל־כֵּ֗ן פִּ֭תְאֹם יָב֣וֹא אֵיד֑וֹ פֶּ֥תַע יִ֝שָּׁבֵ֗ר וְאֵ֣ין מַרְפֵּֽא׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore shall his calamity come suddenly; suddenly shall he be broken without remedy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, his undoing will come with suddenness; he will be broken in an instant, not to be healed.


###### Proverbs 6:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:16)


שֶׁשׁ־הֵ֭נָּה שָׂנֵ֣א יְהֹוָ֑ה וְ֝שֶׁ֗בַע (תועבות) [תּוֹעֲבַ֥ת] נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There are six things which the Lord hates, and seven which are an abomination to him:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These six are hated by the L-rd, and the seventh is the abomination of His soul:


###### Proverbs 6:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:17)


עֵינַ֣יִם רָ֭מוֹת לְשׁ֣וֹן שָׁ֑קֶר וְ֝יָדַ֗יִם שֹׁפְכ֥וֹת דָּם־נָקִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
a proud look, a lying tongue, and hands that shed innocent blood,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Haughty eyes, a false tongue, and hands spilling innocent blood,


###### Proverbs 6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:18)


לֵ֗ב חֹ֭רֵשׁ מַחְשְׁב֣וֹת אָ֑וֶן רַגְלַ֥יִם מְ֝מַהֲר֗וֹת לָר֥וּץ לָרָעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
a heart that devises wicked thoughts, feet that are swift in running to mischief,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A heart plotting vain thoughts, feet hastening to run to evil,


###### Proverbs 6:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:19)


יָפִ֣יחַ כְּ֭זָבִים עֵ֣ד שָׁ֑קֶר וּמְשַׁלֵּ֥חַ מְ֝דָנִ֗ים בֵּ֣ין אַחִֽים׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
a false witness that breathes out lies, and one that sows discord among brethren.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A false witness spouting lies, and the inciter of strife among brothers.


###### Proverbs 6:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:20)


נְצֹ֣ר בְּ֭נִי מִצְוַ֣ת אָבִ֑יךָ וְאַל־תִּ֝טֹּ֗שׁ תּוֹרַ֥ת אִמֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, keep thy father’s commandment, and forsake not the Tora of thy mother:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Heed, my son, the command of your father, and do not forsake the instruction of your mother.


###### Proverbs 6:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:21)


קׇשְׁרֵ֣ם עַל־לִבְּךָ֣ תָמִ֑יד עׇ֝נְדֵ֗ם עַל־גַּרְגְּרֹתֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
bind them continually upon thy heart, and tie them about thy neck.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Tie them to your heart; entwine them around your throat.


###### Proverbs 6:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:22)


בְּהִתְהַלֶּכְךָ֨**׀** תַּנְחֶ֬ה אֹתָ֗ךְ בְּֽ֭שׇׁכְבְּךָ תִּשְׁמֹ֣ר עָלֶ֑יךָ וַ֝הֲקִיצ֗וֹתָ הִ֣יא תְשִׂיחֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou walkest, it shall lead thee; when thou liest down, it shall keep thee; and when thou awakest, it shall talk with thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In your going [i.e., in your life], it shall guide you. In your lying down [(in the grave)], it shall guard you. And when you awake [(at the resurrection)], it will speak for you [(i.e., in your behalf)].


###### Proverbs 6:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:23)


כִּ֤י נֵ֣ר מִ֭צְוָה וְת֣וֹרָה א֑וֹר וְדֶ֥רֶךְ חַ֝יִּ֗ים תּוֹכְח֥וֹת מוּסָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the commandment is a lamp; and Tora is light; and reproofs of instruction are the way of life:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For a mitzvah is a lamp [(which illumines for a given time)], and Torah is light [(which illumines forever)], and the chastisements of mussar are [(what impel a man to)] the road of life.


###### Proverbs 6:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:24)


לִ֭שְׁמׇרְךָ מֵאֵ֣שֶׁת רָ֑ע מֵ֝חֶלְקַ֗ת לָשׁ֥וֹן נׇכְרִיָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to keep thee from the evil woman, from the smoothness of the tongue of an alien.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To guard you from an evil woman [(idolatry)], from the sealing [of your eyes] by the foreign woman's tongue.


###### Proverbs 6:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:25)


אַל־תַּחְמֹ֣ד יׇ֭פְיָהּ בִּלְבָבֶ֑ךָ וְאַל־תִּ֝קָּחֲךָ֗ בְּעַפְעַפֶּֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lust not after her beauty in thy heart; nor let her take thee with her eyelids.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not lust her beauty in your heart, and let her not take you [i.e., your wisdom] with [the winking of] her lids.


###### Proverbs 6:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:26)


כִּ֤י בְעַד־אִשָּׁ֥ה זוֹנָ֗ה עַֽד־כִּכַּ֫ר־לָ֥חֶם וְאֵ֥שֶׁת אִ֑ישׁ נֶ֖פֶשׁ יְקָרָ֣ה תָצֽוּד׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For by means of a harlot a man is brought to a piece of bread: and the adulteress will hunt for the precious life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For [lust] for a harlot [reduces one] to [begging for] a loaf of bread, and the adulterous woman ensnares the precious soul.


###### Proverbs 6:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:27)


הֲיַחְתֶּ֤ה אִ֓ישׁ אֵ֬שׁ בְּחֵיק֑וֹ וּ֝בְגָדָ֗יו לֹ֣א תִשָּׂרַֽפְנָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Can a man take fire in his bosom, and his clothes not be burned?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will a man draw forth fire into his lap and his clothes not be burned?


###### Proverbs 6:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:28)


אִם־יְהַלֵּ֣ךְ אִ֭ישׁ עַל־הַגֶּחָלִ֑ים וְ֝רַגְלָ֗יו לֹ֣א תִכָּוֶֽינָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
