Can one walk upon hot coals, and his feet not be scorched?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will a man walk on coals and his feet not be scorched?


###### Proverbs 6:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:29)


כֵּ֗ן הַ֭בָּא אֶל־אֵ֣שֶׁת רֵעֵ֑הוּ לֹ֥א יִ֝נָּקֶ֗ה כׇּֽל־הַנֹּגֵ֥עַ בָּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So he that goes in to his neighbour’s wife; whoever touches her shall not go unpunished.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So, he who comes to his friend's wife; no one who touches her will go clean.


###### Proverbs 6:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:30)


לֹא־יָב֣וּזוּ לַ֭גַּנָּב כִּ֣י יִגְנ֑וֹב לְמַלֵּ֥א נַ֝פְשׁ֗וֹ כִּ֣י יִרְעָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not men despise a thief, even if he steals to satisfy his soul when he is hungry?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The thief is not despised when he steals to fill his soul; for he is hungry.


###### Proverbs 6:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:31)


וְ֭נִמְצָא יְשַׁלֵּ֣ם שִׁבְעָתָ֑יִם אֶת־כׇּל־ה֖וֹן בֵּית֣וֹ יִתֵּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if he be found, he shall restore sevenfold; he shall give all the substance of his house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, [in addition,] when he is found, he can [make indemnification (to the point of)] paying sevenfold [i.e., a great sum], [and, if necessary,] he will give all the wealth of his house.


###### Proverbs 6:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:32)


נֹאֵ֣ף אִשָּׁ֣ה חֲסַר־לֵ֑ב מַֽשְׁחִ֥ית נַ֝פְשׁ֗וֹ ה֣וּא יַעֲשֶֽׂנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who commits adultery with a woman lacks understanding: he who does that destroys his soul.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But one who commits adultery, lacks a heart; the destroyer of his soul — he will do it [(the act)].


###### Proverbs 6:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:33)


נֶגַע־וְקָל֥וֹן יִמְצָ֑א וְ֝חֶרְפָּת֗וֹ לֹ֣א תִמָּחֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wound and dishonour shall he get; and his reproach shall not be wiped away.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Plague and shame will he find [(Idolatry and adultery are visited with plague)], and his shame will not be wiped out.


###### Proverbs 6:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:34)


כִּֽי־קִנְאָ֥ה חֲמַת־גָּ֑בֶר וְלֹא־יַ֝חְמ֗וֹל בְּי֣וֹם נָקָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For jealousy is the rage of a man: and he will not spare in the day of vengeance.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the wrath of the Strong One is aroused against him, and He will not be merciful on the day of revenge.


###### Proverbs 6:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/6:35)


לֹֽא־יִ֭שָּׂא פְּנֵ֣י כׇל־כֹּ֑פֶר וְלֹא־יֹ֝אבֶ֗ה כִּ֣י תַרְבֶּה־שֹּֽׁחַד׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He will not regard any ransom; nor will he rest content, though thou givest many gifts.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will not accept any ransom [(to atone for his idolatry)]; and He will refuse though you raise the bribe.



## Chapter 7

###### Proverbs 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:1)


בְּ֭נִי שְׁמֹ֣ר אֲמָרָ֑י וּ֝מִצְוֺתַ֗י תִּצְפֹּ֥ן אִתָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, keep my words, and lay up my commandments with thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My son, heed My words, and store up My mitzvoth with you.


###### Proverbs 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:2)


שְׁמֹ֣ר מִצְוֺתַ֣י וֶחְיֵ֑ה וְ֝תוֹרָתִ֗י כְּאִישׁ֥וֹן עֵינֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Keep my commandments, and live; and my Tora as the apple of thy eye.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Heed my mitzvoth and live, and My Torah as the pupil of your eye.


###### Proverbs 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:3)


קׇשְׁרֵ֥ם עַל־אֶצְבְּעֹתֶ֑יךָ כׇּ֝תְבֵ֗ם עַל־ל֥וּחַ לִבֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Bind them upon thy fingers, write them upon the tablet of thy heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Tie them on your fingers; inscribe them on the tablet of your heart.


###### Proverbs 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:4)


אֱמֹ֣ר לַ֭חׇכְמָה אֲחֹ֣תִי אָ֑תְּ וּ֝מֹדָ֗ע לַבִּינָ֥ה תִקְרָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Say to wisdom, Thou art my sister; and call understanding thy kinswoman:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Say to wisdom: "You are my sister," and call understanding your kin.


###### Proverbs 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:5)


לִ֭שְׁמׇרְךָ מֵאִשָּׁ֣ה זָרָ֑ה מִ֝נׇּכְרִיָּ֗ה אֲמָרֶ֥יהָ הֶחֱלִֽיקָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that they may keep thee from the strange woman, from the alien woman who makes smooth her words.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To guard you from a strange woman, from the foreign woman who smooths over her words.


###### Proverbs 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:6)


כִּ֭י בְּחַלּ֣וֹן בֵּיתִ֑י בְּעַ֖ד אֶשְׁנַבִּ֣י נִשְׁקָֽפְתִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For at the window of my house I looked out through my lattice,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For from the window of my house, through my lattice, I looked out.


###### Proverbs 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:7)


וָאֵ֤רֶא בַפְּתָאיִ֗ם אָ֘בִ֤ינָה בַבָּנִ֗ים נַ֣עַר חֲסַר־לֵֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and beheld among the simple ones, I discerned among the youths, a young man void of understanding,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I saw among the simple, I discerned among the boys, a youth void of understanding.


###### Proverbs 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:8)


עֹבֵ֣ר בַּ֭שּׁוּק אֵ֣צֶל פִּנָּ֑הּ וְדֶ֖רֶךְ בֵּיתָ֣הּ יִצְעָֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
passing through the street near her corner; and he went the way to her house,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Passing in the market-place near her corner, walking by her house.


###### Proverbs 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:9)


בְּנֶֽשֶׁף־בְּעֶ֥רֶב י֑וֹם בְּאִישׁ֥וֹן לַ֝֗יְלָה וַאֲפֵלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
in the twilight, in the evening, in the blackness of a dark night:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the darkness, in the eve of day, in the heart of night and pitch darkness.


###### Proverbs 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:10)


וְהִנֵּ֣ה אִ֭שָּׁה לִקְרָאת֑וֹ שִׁ֥ית ז֝וֹנָ֗ה וּנְצֻ֥רַת לֵֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and, behold, there met him a woman with the attire of a harlot, and wily of heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, behold, a woman came before him, in the nakedness of a harlot and her heart besieged [(by lewdness)].


###### Proverbs 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:11)


הֹמִיָּ֣ה הִ֣יא וְסֹרָ֑רֶת בְּ֝בֵיתָ֗הּ לֹא־יִשְׁכְּנ֥וּ רַגְלֶֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
(She is noisy and ungovernable: her feet do not remain in her house:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Moaning [in enticement] and straying [(from the path)], her feet not frequenting her house.


###### Proverbs 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:12)


פַּ֤עַם**׀** בַּח֗וּץ פַּ֥עַם בָּרְחֹב֑וֹת וְאֵ֖צֶל כׇּל־פִּנָּ֣ה תֶאֱרֹֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
now is she outside, now in the streets, and she lies in wait at every corner.)


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Sometimes in the courtyard, sometimes in the streets — she lurks at every corner.


###### Proverbs 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:13)


וְהֶחֱזִ֣יקָה בּ֭וֹ וְנָ֣שְׁקָה לּ֑וֹ הֵעֵ֥זָה פָ֝נֶ֗יהָ וַתֹּ֣אמַר לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So she caught hold of him, and kissed him, and with an impudent face said to him,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And she seized him and kissed him; she thrust forth her face and said to him:


###### Proverbs 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:14)


זִבְחֵ֣י שְׁלָמִ֣ים עָלָ֑י הַ֝יּ֗וֹם שִׁלַּ֥מְתִּי נְדָרָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have had to sacrifice peace offerings; this day have I paid my vows.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have prepared a great feast, [for] today I have paid my vows [i.e., I have sacrificed my vow-offerings and my peace-offerings.]


###### Proverbs 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:15)


עַל־כֵּ֭ן יָצָ֣אתִי לִקְרָאתֶ֑ךָ לְשַׁחֵ֥ר פָּ֝נֶ֗יךָ וָאֶמְצָאֶֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So I came out to meet thee, diligently to seek thy face, and I have found thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
That is why I have gone out to you, to look for you and to find you.


###### Proverbs 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:16)


מַ֭רְבַדִּים רָבַ֣דְתִּי עַרְשִׂ֑י חֲ֝טֻב֗וֹת אֵט֥וּן מִצְרָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have decked my bed with coverings, with tapestry of Miżrian yarn.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have decked my bed with spreads, braided with Egyptian yarns.


###### Proverbs 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:17)


נַ֥פְתִּי מִשְׁכָּבִ֑י מֹ֥ר אֲ֝הָלִ֗ים וְקִנָּמֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have perfumed my bed with myrrh, aloes, and cinnamon.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have scented my bed with myrrh, aloes, and cinnamon.


###### Proverbs 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:18)


לְכָ֤ה נִרְוֶ֣ה דֹ֭דִים עַד־הַבֹּ֑קֶר נִ֝תְעַלְּסָ֗ה בׇּאֳהָבִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Come, let us take our fill of love until the morning: let us delight ourselves with loves.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Come, let us sate ourselves with love until the morning; let us rejoice in love-making.


###### Proverbs 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:19)


כִּ֤י אֵ֣ין הָאִ֣ישׁ בְּבֵית֑וֹ הָ֝לַ֗ךְ בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ מֵרָחֽוֹק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For my husband is not at home, he is gone a long journey:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the Man is not at home [(You see that G-d has removed His shechinah and has given all of His good to the idolators.)]


###### Proverbs 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:20)


צְֽרוֹר־הַ֭כֶּסֶף לָקַ֣ח בְּיָד֑וֹ לְי֥וֹם הַ֝כֵּ֗סֶא יָבֹ֥א בֵיתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he has taken a bag of money with him, and will come home at the full moon.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He has taken the money-pouch with him [i.e., He has killed the best of them]; He will [not] come home until the appointed time.


###### Proverbs 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:21)


הִ֭טַּתּוּ בְּרֹ֣ב לִקְחָ֑הּ בְּחֵ֥לֶק שְׂ֝פָתֶ֗יהָ תַּדִּיחֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
With her much fair speech she causes him to yield, with the smoothness of her lips she seduces him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She has led him astray with her store of sophistication; she has felled him with the smoothness of her lips.


###### Proverbs 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:22)


ה֤וֹלֵ֥ךְ אַחֲרֶ֗יהָ פִּ֫תְאֹ֥ם כְּ֭שׁוֹר אֶל־טֶ֣בַח יָבֹ֑א וּ֝כְעֶ֗כֶס אֶל־מוּסַ֥ר אֱוִֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He goes after her at once, as an ox goes to the slaughter, and as a man in chains to the chastisement of a fool;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Suddenly, he goes after her; as an ox to the slaughter he comes, as [a snake spewing] venom [speeding] to the chastisement of a fool.


###### Proverbs 7:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:23)


עַ֤ד יְפַלַּ֪ח חֵ֡ץ כְּֽבֵד֗וֹ כְּמַהֵ֣ר צִפּ֣וֹר אֶל־פָּ֑ח וְלֹא־יָ֝דַ֗ע כִּֽי־בְנַפְשׁ֥וֹ הֽוּא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
till a dart strike through his liver; as a bird hastens to the snare, and knows not that it is for his life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
— Until [her] arrow splits his liver. [He is] as a bird racing to the trap, not knowing that it will cost him his life.


###### Proverbs 7:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:24)


וְעַתָּ֣ה בָ֭נִים שִׁמְעוּ־לִ֑י וְ֝הַקְשִׁ֗יבוּ לְאִמְרֵי־פִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hearken to me now therefore, O you children, and attend to the words of my mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, [my] sons, listen to me, and heed the words of my mouth.


###### Proverbs 7:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:25)


אַל־יֵ֣שְׂטְ אֶל־דְּרָכֶ֣יהָ לִבֶּ֑ךָ אַל־תֵּ֝֗תַע בִּנְתִיבוֹתֶֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let not thy heart decline to her ways, do not go astray in her paths.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not incline your heart to her ways; do not stray in her paths.


###### Proverbs 7:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:26)


כִּֽי־רַבִּ֣ים חֲלָלִ֣ים הִפִּ֑ילָה וַ֝עֲצֻמִ֗ים כׇּל־הֲרֻגֶֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For she has cast down many wounded: and many strong men have been slain by her.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For she has taken many lives; the number of her victims is legion.


###### Proverbs 7:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/7:27)


דַּרְכֵ֣י שְׁא֣וֹל בֵּיתָ֑הּ יֹ֝רְד֗וֹת אֶל־חַדְרֵי־מָֽוֶת׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Her house is the way to She᾽ol, going down to the chambers of death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Her house is the road to Sheol, descending to the chambers of death.



## Chapter 8

###### Proverbs 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:1)


הֲלֹֽא־חׇכְמָ֥ה תִקְרָ֑א וּ֝תְבוּנָ֗ה תִּתֵּ֥ן קוֹלָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Does not wisdom call? and understanding put forth her voice?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Does wisdom [(Torah)] not call out [(what follows [verse 4, etc.])] and [does] understanding not give forth its voice?


###### Proverbs 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:2)


בְּרֹאשׁ־מְרֹמִ֥ים עֲלֵי־דָ֑רֶךְ בֵּ֖ית נְתִיב֣וֹת נִצָּֽבָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She stands at the top of high places by the way, where the paths meet.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Atop the heights, overlooking the many-pathed way, it stands.


###### Proverbs 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:3)


לְיַד־שְׁעָרִ֥ים לְפִי־קָ֑רֶת מְב֖וֹא פְתָחִ֣ים תָּרֹֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She cries out at the gates, at the entry of the city, at the coming in of the doors.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Near the gateways, atop the dome [(of the gate, where people sit)], the doored entry-way, it shouts:


###### Proverbs 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:4)


אֲלֵיכֶ֣ם אִישִׁ֣ים אֶקְרָ֑א וְ֝קוֹלִ֗י אֶל־בְּנֵ֥י אָדָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
To you, O men, I call; and my voice is to the sons of man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To you, men, do I call, and I lift my voice to the sons of men.


###### Proverbs 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:5)


הָבִ֣ינוּ פְתָאיִ֣ם עׇרְמָ֑ה וּ֝כְסִילִ֗ים הָבִ֥ינוּ לֵֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O you simple, understand prudence: and, you fools, be of an understanding heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Simpletons, understand wisdom, and fools, make your hearts understand.


###### Proverbs 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:6)


שִׁ֭מְעוּ כִּֽי־נְגִידִ֣ים אֲדַבֵּ֑ר וּמִפְתַּ֥ח שְׂ֝פָתַ֗י מֵישָׁרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear; for I will speak excellent things; and the opening of my lips shall be rectitude.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Give ear, for I will speak noble thoughts, and my mouth will open to just words.


###### Proverbs 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:7)


כִּֽי־אֱ֭מֶת יֶהְגֶּ֣ה חִכִּ֑י וְתוֹעֲבַ֖ת שְׂפָתַ֣י רֶֽשַׁע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For my mouth shall speak truth; and wickedness is an abomination to my lips.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For my palate will utter truth, and evil is abominated by my lips.


###### Proverbs 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:8)


בְּצֶ֥דֶק כׇּל־אִמְרֵי־פִ֑י אֵ֥ין בָּ֝הֶ֗ם נִפְתָּ֥ל וְעִקֵּֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All the words of my mouth are in righteousness; there is nothing crooked or perverse in them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All the words of my mouth are spoken in righteousness; they contain no twisting or perversion.


###### Proverbs 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:9)


כֻּלָּ֣ם נְ֭כֹחִים לַמֵּבִ֑ין וִ֝ישָׁרִ֗ים לְמֹ֣צְאֵי דָֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They are all plain to him that understands, and right to those who find knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They are all correct to him who understands, and just to the finders of wisdom.


###### Proverbs 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:10)


קְחֽוּ־מוּסָרִ֥י וְאַל־כָּ֑סֶף וְ֝דַ֗עַת מֵחָר֥וּץ נִבְחָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Receive my instruction, and not silver; and knowledge rather than choice gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take my mussar rather than silver, and knowledge rather than choice fine gold.


###### Proverbs 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:11)


כִּֽי־טוֹבָ֣ה חׇ֭כְמָה מִפְּנִינִ֑ים וְכׇל־חֲ֝פָצִ֗ים לֹ֣א יִֽשְׁווּ־בָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For wisdom is better than rubies; and all the things that may be desired are not to be compared to it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For wisdom is better than pearls, and all one's desires cannot compare to it [in worth].


###### Proverbs 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:12)


אֲֽנִי־חׇ֭כְמָה שָׁכַ֣נְתִּי עׇרְמָ֑ה וְדַ֖עַת מְזִמּ֣וֹת אֶמְצָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I, wisdom, dwell with prudence, and find knowledge and discretion.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I [Torah] am wisdom. I dwell [near] subtlety [(One who has learned Torah is subtle in all matters)]. I provide knowledge for thoughts [(to combat the evil inclination)].


###### Proverbs 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:13)


יִ֥רְאַ֣ת יְהֹוָה֮ שְֽׂנֹ֫את־רָ֥ע גֵּ֘אָ֤ה וְגָא֨וֹן**׀** וְדֶ֣רֶךְ רָ֭ע וּפִ֨י תַהְפֻּכ֬וֹת שָׂנֵֽאתִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The fear of the Lord is to hate evil: pride, and arrogancy, and the evil way, and the sly mouth I hate.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(the "mussar" [verse 4]):] The fear of the L-rd is hatred of evil — pride and haughtiness, the path of evil, and a mouth that speaks opposites [of what one thinks]. I hate [all of these].


###### Proverbs 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:14)


לִֽי־עֵ֭צָה וְתוּשִׁיָּ֑ה אֲנִ֥י בִ֝ינָ֗ה לִ֣י גְבוּרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Counsel is mine, and sound wisdom: I am understanding; I have strength.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With me there is counsel [against the evil inclination] and weakening [of it]. I [give] understanding; with me is strength.


###### Proverbs 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:15)


בִּ֭י מְלָכִ֣ים יִמְלֹ֑כוּ וְ֝רֹזְנִ֗ים יְחֹ֣קְקוּ צֶֽדֶק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By me kings reign, and princes decree justice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Through me, kings will rule [(for I teach them the laws and the judgments)], and princes will legislate righteously.


###### Proverbs 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:16)


בִּ֭י שָׂרִ֣ים יָשֹׂ֑רוּ וּ֝נְדִיבִ֗ים כׇּל־שֹׁ֥פְטֵי צֶֽדֶק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By me princes rule, and nobles, even all the judges of the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Through me, regents will hold sway, and the lordliest of all those who judge in righteousness.


###### Proverbs 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:17)


אֲ֭נִי (אהביה) [אֹהֲבַ֣י] אֵהָ֑ב וּ֝מְשַׁחֲרַ֗י יִמְצָאֻֽנְנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I love those who love me; and those who seek me early shall find me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I [(the L-rd)] love its [(Torah's)] lovers, and My probers [i.e., those who probe Me through Torah] shall find Me.


###### Proverbs 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:18)


עֹֽשֶׁר־וְכָב֥וֹד אִתִּ֑י ה֥וֹן עָ֝תֵ֗ק וּצְדָקָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Riches and honour are with me; yea, durable riches and righteousness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Wealth and honor are with me [(Torah)]; great fortune, given freely.


###### Proverbs 8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:19)


ט֣וֹב פִּ֭רְיִי מֵחָר֣וּץ וּמִפָּ֑ז וּ֝תְבוּאָתִ֗י מִכֶּ֥סֶף נִבְחָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My fruit is better than gold, yea, than fine gold; and my produce than choice silver.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My fruits are better than gold and fine gold, and my produce is choicer than silver.


###### Proverbs 8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:20)


בְּאֹֽרַח־צְדָקָ֥ה אֲהַלֵּ֑ךְ בְּ֝ת֗וֹךְ נְתִיב֥וֹת מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I walk in the way of righteousness, in the midst of the paths of justice:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I lead [men] in the way of righteousness, in the midst of the paths of justice.


###### Proverbs 8:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:21)


לְהַנְחִ֖יל אֹהֲבַ֥י׀יֵ֑שׁ וְאֹצְרֹ֖תֵיהֶ֣ם אֲמַלֵּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
That I may cause those who love me to inherit substance; and I will fill their treasures.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have much to bequeath to my lovers, and I shall fill their stores.


###### Proverbs 8:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:22)


יְֽהֹוָ֗ה קָ֭נָנִי רֵאשִׁ֣ית דַּרְכּ֑וֹ קֶ֖דֶם מִפְעָלָ֣יו מֵאָֽז׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord created me as the beginning of his way, the first of his works of old.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd acquired me [(Torah)] (as) the beginning of His way [i.e., before the creation of the universe)], before His deeds of yore.


###### Proverbs 8:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:23)


מֵ֭עוֹלָם נִסַּ֥כְתִּי מֵרֹ֗אשׁ מִקַּדְמֵי־אָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I was set up from everlasting, from the beginning, before ever the earth was.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I was enthroned from the beginning. I am among the first, [(Seven things, Torah among them, preceded the creation of the world)], [among] the precursors of the earth.


###### Proverbs 8:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:24)


בְּאֵין־תְּהֹמ֥וֹת חוֹלָ֑לְתִּי בְּאֵ֥ין מַ֝עְיָנ֗וֹת נִכְבַּדֵּי־מָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When there were no depths, I was brought forth; when there were no fountains abounding with water.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When there were yet no depths, I was formed; when there were no pools rich with water.


###### Proverbs 8:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:25)


בְּטֶ֣רֶם הָרִ֣ים הׇטְבָּ֑עוּ לִפְנֵ֖י גְבָע֣וֹת חוֹלָֽלְתִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Before the mountains were settled, before the hills was I brought forth:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Before the mountains were sunk [(into the waters)]; before the hills, was I formed.


###### Proverbs 8:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:26)


עַד־לֹ֣א עָ֭שָׂה אֶ֣רֶץ וְחוּצ֑וֹת וְ֝רֹ֗אשׁ עַפְר֥וֹת תֵּבֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
while as yet he had not made the earth, or the fields, or the highest part of the dust of the world.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Before there had been made the land [(Eretz Yisrael)] and its environs [(the other lands)], and the head of the dust of the world [(Adam)].


###### Proverbs 8:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:27)


בַּהֲכִינ֣וֹ שָׁ֭מַיִם שָׁ֣ם אָ֑נִי בְּחֻ֥קוֹ ח֝֗וּג עַל־פְּנֵ֥י תְהֽוֹם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When he established the heavens, I was there: when he drew a circle over the surface of the deep:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When He prepared the heavens, there was I; when He etched out the globe [of the earth] upon the face of the deep [(as an impassable bound)].


###### Proverbs 8:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:28)


בְּאַמְּצ֣וֹ שְׁחָקִ֣ים מִמָּ֑עַל בַּ֝עֲז֗וֹז עִינ֥וֹת תְּהֽוֹם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when he established the clouds above: when the fountains of the deep became strong:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When He strengthened the heavens above; when He fortified the basins of the depths.


###### Proverbs 8:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:29)


בְּשׂ֘וּמ֤וֹ לַיָּ֨ם**׀** חֻקּ֗וֹ וּ֭מַיִם לֹ֣א יַֽעַבְרוּ־פִ֑יו בְּ֝חוּק֗וֹ מ֣וֹסְדֵי אָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when he gave to the sea its decree, that the waters should not pass his commandment: when he appointed the foundations of the earth:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When He set His statute to the sea [(at the creation, that it split in the time of Moses)], when He etched out the foundations of the earth.


###### Proverbs 8:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:30)


וָאֶהְיֶ֥ה אֶצְל֗וֹ אָ֫מ֥וֹן וָאֶהְיֶ֣ה שַׁ֭עֲשׁוּעִים י֤וֹם**׀** י֑וֹם מְשַׂחֶ֖קֶת לְפָנָ֣יו בְּכׇל־עֵֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then I was by him, as a nurseling: and I was daily his delight, playing always before him;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I was then His nursling, [His] delight, day [by] day [(two thousand years [the L-rd's "day" being a thousand years] — Torah being created two thousand years before the creation of the universe)], playing before Him at all times.


###### Proverbs 8:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:31)


מְ֭שַׂחֶקֶת בְּתֵבֵ֣ל אַרְצ֑וֹ וְ֝שַׁעֲשֻׁעַ֗י אֶת־בְּנֵ֥י אָדָֽם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
playing with the universe, his earth; and my delights were with the sons of men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Laughing at the world, His earth, [("laughing" at all the generations of the wicked, from Adam until Noach, and from Noach until Abraham)] and [finding] my delight with the sons of man [(the generations of the desert, which accepted the Torah)].


###### Proverbs 8:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:32)


וְעַתָּ֣ה בָ֭נִים שִׁמְעוּ־לִ֑י וְ֝אַשְׁרֵ֗י דְּרָכַ֥י יִשְׁמֹֽרוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore hearken to me, O you children: for happy are they who keep my ways.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, children, listen to me — and fortunate are they who heed my ways.


###### Proverbs 8:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:33)


שִׁמְע֖וּ מוּסָ֥ר וַחֲכָ֗מוּ וְאַל־תִּפְרָֽעוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear instruction, and be wise, and refuse it not.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear mussar, and grow wise, and do not reject [my mussar].


###### Proverbs 8:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:34)


אַ֥שְֽׁרֵי־אָדָם֮ שֹׁמֵ֢עַֽ֫ לִ֥י לִשְׁקֹ֣ד עַל־דַּ֭לְתֹתַי י֤וֹם**׀** י֑וֹם לִ֝שְׁמֹ֗ר מְזוּזֹ֥ת פְּתָחָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Happy is the man who hearkens to me, watching daily at my gates, waiting at the posts of my doors.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Fortunate is the man who gives ear to me, to guard my doors each day [(to enter the house of study and the house of prayer first and to leave last)], to guard my doorposts.


###### Proverbs 8:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:35)


כִּ֣י מֹ֭צְאִי (מצאי) [מָצָ֣א] חַיִּ֑ים וַיָּ֥פֶק רָ֝צ֗וֹן מֵיְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For whoever finds me finds life, and obtains favour of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For he who finds me finds life and elicits favor from the L-rd.


###### Proverbs 8:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/8:36)


וְֽ֭חֹטְאִי חֹמֵ֣ס נַפְשׁ֑וֹ כׇּל־מְ֝שַׂנְאַ֗י אָ֣הֲבוּ מָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he who sins against me wrongs his own soul: all they who hate me love death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But he who sins against me despoils his soul; all who hate me love death.



## Chapter 9

###### Proverbs 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:1)


חׇ֭כְמוֹת בָּנְתָ֣ה בֵיתָ֑הּ חָצְבָ֖ה עַמּוּדֶ֣יהָ שִׁבְעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wisdom has built her house, she has hewn out her seven pillars:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Wisdom has built her house [i.e., the L-rd has built the world with wisdom]. She has carved out its seven pillars [(the seven days of creation)].


###### Proverbs 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:2)


טָבְחָ֣ה טִ֭בְחָהּ מָסְכָ֣ה יֵינָ֑הּ אַ֝֗ף עָרְכָ֥ה שֻׁלְחָנָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
she has killed her beasts; she has mingled her wine; she has also furnished her table.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She has slaughtered her slaughtering and diluted her wine and also set her table [(with all the creations, wet and dry)].


###### Proverbs 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:3)


שָׁלְחָ֣ה נַעֲרֹתֶ֣יהָ תִקְרָ֑א עַל־גַּ֝פֵּ֗י מְרֹ֣מֵי קָֽרֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She has sent forth her maidens: she calls upon the highest places of the city, Whoever is simple, let him turn in here:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She has sent out to call [to] her young ones [Adam and Eve] on the wings of the city heights:


###### Proverbs 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:4)


מִֽי־פֶ֭תִי יָסֻ֣ר הֵ֑נָּה חֲסַר־לֵ֝֗ב אָ֣מְרָה לּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
as for him that lacks understanding, she says to him,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"Who is a simpleton? Let him turn in here" [and gain wisdom]. [And so] will she say to him who is void of understanding.


###### Proverbs 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:5)


לְ֭כוּ לַחֲמ֣וּ בְֽלַחֲמִ֑י וּ֝שְׁת֗וּ בְּיַ֣יִן מָסָֽכְתִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Come, eat of my bread, and drink of the wine which I have mingled.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"Come and eat of my bread, and drink of the wine that I have poured."


###### Proverbs 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:6)


עִזְב֣וּ פְתָאיִ֣ם וִֽחְי֑וּ וְ֝אִשְׁר֗וּ בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ בִּינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Forsake foolishness, and live; and go in the way of understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"Leave [your paths], simpletons, and live, and walk in the way of understanding."


###### Proverbs 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:7)


יֹ֤סֵ֨ר**׀** לֵ֗ץ לֹקֵ֣חַֽ ל֣וֹ קָל֑וֹן וּמוֹכִ֖יחַ לְרָשָׁ֣ע מוּמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who corrects a scorner brings shame on himself: and he who rebukes a wicked man brings on himself his blemish.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(an exhortation against reproving inciters to idolatry:)] ([But] he who chastises the scoffer brings shame upon himself; and he who reproves the wicked one [brings upon himself] his blemish [i.e., the wicked one shames him and shuns him].)


###### Proverbs 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:8)


אַל־תּ֣וֹכַח לֵ֭ץ פֶּן־יִשְׂנָאֶ֑ךָּ הוֹכַ֥ח לְ֝חָכָ֗ם וְיֶאֱהָבֶֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not reprove a scorner, lest he hate thee: rebuke a wise man, and he will love thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not chastise the scoffer, lest he hate you. Chastise the wise man, and he will love you.


###### Proverbs 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:9)


תֵּ֣ן לְ֭חָכָם וְיֶחְכַּם־ע֑וֹד הוֹדַ֥ע לְ֝צַדִּ֗יק וְי֣וֹסֶף לֶֽקַח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Give instruction to a wise man, and he will be yet wiser: teach a just man, and he will increase in learning.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Give [words of wisdom] to the wise one [(an apt disciple)] and he will become even wiser. Make known [wisdom] to the righteous one, and he will add to his acquisitions [(by his own reasoning)].


###### Proverbs 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:10)


תְּחִלַּ֣ת חׇ֭כְמָה יִרְאַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֑ה וְדַ֖עַת קְדֹשִׁ֣ים בִּינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The fear of the Lord is the beginning of wisdom: and the knowledge of holy matters is understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The beginning of wisdom is fear of the L-rd, and the knowledge of the holy ones is [the essence of] understanding.


###### Proverbs 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:11)


כִּי־בִ֭י יִרְבּ֣וּ יָמֶ֑יךָ וְיוֹסִ֥יפוּ לְּ֝ךָ֗ שְׁנ֣וֹת חַיִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For by me thy days shall be multiplied, and the years of thy life shall be increased.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For through me [(wisdom)] your days will increase, and they [(these days)] will add for you years of life [i.e., years of sustenance and wealth].


###### Proverbs 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:12)


אִם־חָ֭כַמְתָּ חָכַ֣מְתָּ לָּ֑ךְ וְ֝לַ֗צְתָּ לְֽבַדְּךָ֥ תִשָּֽׂא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou art wise, thou art wise for thyself: and if thou scornest, thou alone shalt bear it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you have become wise, you have become wise for your own good, and if you have been a scoffer, you alone will bear [your punishment].


###### Proverbs 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:13)


אֵ֣שֶׁת כְּ֭סִילוּת הֹמִיָּ֑ה פְּ֝תַיּ֗וּת וּבַל־יָ֥דְעָה מָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The woman Folly is clamorous: she is simple, and knows nothing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The foolish woman moans foolishly, not knowing why.


###### Proverbs 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:14)


וְֽ֭יָשְׁבָה לְפֶ֣תַח בֵּיתָ֑הּ עַל־כִּ֝סֵּ֗א מְרֹ֣מֵי קָֽרֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For she sits at the door of her house, on a seat in the high places of the city.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She sits at the door of her house, [(to scout out the passers-by)], [and] upon a chair atop the city heights


###### Proverbs 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:15)


לִקְרֹ֥א לְעֹֽבְרֵי־דָ֑רֶךְ הַֽ֝מְיַשְּׁרִ֗ים אֹֽרְחוֹתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Calling out to passers by who are going straight on their ways:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
to call out to the passers-by, who walk a just path.


###### Proverbs 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:16)


מִֽי־פֶ֭תִי יָסֻ֣ר הֵ֑נָּה וַחֲסַר־לֵ֝֗ב וְאָ֣מְרָה לּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whoever is simple, let him turn in here: and as for him that lacks understanding, she says to him,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
(Whoever is a simpleton, let him turn in here, and [whoever is] void of understanding). And thus will she say to him:


###### Proverbs 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:17)


מַֽיִם־גְּנוּבִ֥ים יִמְתָּ֑קוּ וְלֶ֖חֶם סְתָרִ֣ים יִנְעָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Stolen waters are sweet, and bread eaten in secret is pleasant.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"Stolen waters are sweet, and bread eaten secretly, more pleasing" [i.e., cohabitation with a married woman is more pleasurable than that with an unmarried one.]


###### Proverbs 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/9:18)


וְֽלֹא־יָ֭דַע כִּֽי־רְפָאִ֣ים שָׁ֑ם בְּעִמְקֵ֖י שְׁא֣וֹל קְרֻאֶֽיהָ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he knows not that the dead are there; and that her guests are in the depths of She᾽ol.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they [the passers-by] do not know that the shades are there, that those [who heed] her call [descend] to the depths of Sheol.



## Chapter 10

###### Proverbs 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:1)


מִשְׁלֵ֗י שְׁלֹ֫מֹ֥ה בֵּ֣ן חָ֭כָם יְשַׂמַּח־אָ֑ב וּבֵ֥ן כְּ֝סִ֗יל תּוּגַ֥ת אִמּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The proverbs of Shelomo. A wise son makes a glad father: but a foolish son is the grief of his mother.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The analogues of Solomon: The wise son rejoices his Father, and the foolish one [e.g., Yeravam ben Nevat] is the sorrow of his mother [(the congregation of Israel)].


###### Proverbs 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:2)


לֹֽא־י֭וֹעִילוּ אוֹצְר֣וֹת רֶ֑שַׁע וּ֝צְדָקָ֗ה תַּצִּ֥יל מִמָּֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Treasures of wickedness profit nothing: but righteousness delivers from death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Treasures of wickedness will not avail, but charity rescues from death. [(And if you would ask: Whence is he who is profuse in his charity to derive sustenance?)]


###### Proverbs 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:3)


לֹא־יַרְעִ֣יב יְ֭הֹוָה נֶ֣פֶשׁ צַדִּ֑יק וְהַוַּ֖ת רְשָׁעִ֣ים יֶהְדֹּֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord will not suffer the soul of the righteous to famish: but he thrusts away the desire of the wicked.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd will not cause the soul of the righteous one to hunger, and the destructiveness of the wicked will cast [them] down.


###### Proverbs 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:4)


רָ֗אשׁ עֹשֶׂ֥ה כַף־רְמִיָּ֑ה וְיַ֖ד חָרוּצִ֣ים תַּעֲשִֽׁיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A slack hand causes poverty: but the hand of the diligent makes rich.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The poor man makes a deceitful scale [(One who is poor in Torah renders false decisions)], and the hand of the diligent [(one who diligently seeks out the truth)] prospers [(in his judgments)].


###### Proverbs 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:5)


אֹגֵ֣ר בַּ֭קַּיִץ בֵּ֣ן מַשְׂכִּ֑יל נִרְדָּ֥ם בַּ֝קָּצִ֗יר בֵּ֣ן מֵבִֽישׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that gathers in summer is a wise son: but a son that sleeps in harvest brings shame.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise son gathers in [his grain] in the summer, but the shameful one sleeps in harvest time.


###### Proverbs 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:6)


בְּ֭רָכוֹת לְרֹ֣אשׁ צַדִּ֑יק וּפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים יְכַסֶּ֥ה חָמָֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Blessings are upon the head of the just: but violence covers the mouth of the wicked.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Blessings [will descend] upon the head of the righteous one, but [their] violence will smother the mouth of the wicked [and kill them].


###### Proverbs 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:7)


זֵ֣כֶר צַ֭דִּיק לִבְרָכָ֑ה וְשֵׁ֖ם רְשָׁעִ֣ים יִרְקָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The memory of the just is blessed: but the name of the wicked shall rot.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remembrance [i.e., mention] of the righteous one will be [accompanied by] a blessing, but the name of the wicked one will rot [(from lack of mention)].


###### Proverbs 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:8)


חֲכַם־לֵ֭ב יִקַּ֣ח מִצְוֺ֑ת וֶאֱוִ֥יל שְׂ֝פָתַ֗יִם יִלָּבֵֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wise in heart will receive commandments: but a prating fool shall be punished.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise of heart will take hold of mitzvoth [(e.g., (Exodus 13:19), While Israel busied themselves with the plunder of Egypt, Moses busied himself with taking the bones of Joseph for burial)], but the fool will weary his lips [(with talking, and do nothing)].


###### Proverbs 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:9)


הוֹלֵ֣ךְ בַּ֭תֹּם יֵ֣לֶךְ בֶּ֑טַח וּמְעַקֵּ֥שׁ דְּ֝רָכָ֗יו יִוָּדֵֽעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that walks uprightly walks surely: but he that perverts his ways shall be found out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who walks in innocence will walk securely, but he who perverts his ways will be broken.


###### Proverbs 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:10)


קֹ֣רֵֽץ עַ֭יִן יִתֵּ֣ן עַצָּ֑בֶת וֶאֱוִ֥יל שְׂ֝פָתַ֗יִם יִלָּבֵֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that winks with the eye causes sorrow: and a prating fool shall fall.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who winks his eye [(inciting to evil)] produces grief [(when the wink is acted upon)], but the fool wearies [his purpose] with his lips.


###### Proverbs 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:11)


מְק֣וֹר חַ֭יִּים פִּ֣י צַדִּ֑יק וּפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים יְכַסֶּ֥ה חָמָֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The mouth of a righteous man is a fountain of life: but violence covers the mouth of the wicked.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A well of life is the mouth of the righteous one, but the mouth of the wicked conceals [their intended] violence.


###### Proverbs 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:12)


שִׂ֭נְאָה תְּעֹרֵ֣ר מְדָנִ֑ים וְעַ֥ל כׇּל־פְּ֝שָׁעִ֗ים תְּכַסֶּ֥ה אַהֲבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hatred stirs up strifes: but love covers all sins.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Present] hatred awakens [long-forgotten] quarrels, but love conceals all offense.


###### Proverbs 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:13)


בְּשִׂפְתֵ֣י נָ֭בוֹן תִּמָּצֵ֣א חׇכְמָ֑ה וְ֝שֵׁ֗בֶט לְגֵ֣ו חֲסַר־לֵֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the lips of him that has understanding wisdom is found: but a rod is for the back of him that is void of understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the lips of the understanding one there will be found wisdom [e.g., David admitted "I have sinned," when reproved by Nathan (II Samuel 12:14)], but [only] a rod [will suffice] for the back of the one void of understanding.


###### Proverbs 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:14)


חֲכָמִ֥ים יִצְפְּנוּ־דָ֑עַת וּפִֽי־אֱ֝וִ֗יל מְחִתָּ֥ה קְרֹבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wise men lay up knowledge: but the mouth of the fool brings ruin near.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise conceal wisdom [(in their heart, to guard it)], but the mouth of the fool [brings] destruction near.


###### Proverbs 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:15)


ה֣וֹן עָ֭שִׁיר קִרְיַ֣ת עֻזּ֑וֹ מְחִתַּ֖ת דַּלִּ֣ים רֵישָֽׁם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The rich man’s wealth is his strong city: the ruin of the poor is their poverty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wealth of the rich one [(in Torah)] is the city of his strength, but the breaking of the poor is their poverty [(in Torah)].


###### Proverbs 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:16)


פְּעֻלַּ֣ת צַדִּ֣יק לְחַיִּ֑ים תְּבוּאַ֖ת רָשָׁ֣ע לְחַטָּֽאת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wage of the righteous brings life: the gain of the wicked leads to sin.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The act of the righteous one is a source of life, but the produce of the wicked one is [a source of] shortening [of life].


###### Proverbs 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:17)


אֹ֣רַח לְ֭חַיִּים שׁוֹמֵ֣ר מוּסָ֑ר וְעֹזֵ֖ב תּוֹכַ֣חַת מַתְעֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that keeps instruction is in the way of life: but he that refuses reproof leads astray.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The heeding of mussar is a path to life, but he who abandons chastisement causes [himself and others] to stray.


###### Proverbs 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:18)


מְכַסֶּ֣ה שִׂ֭נְאָה שִׂפְתֵי־שָׁ֑קֶר וּמוֹצִ֥א דִ֝בָּ֗ה ה֣וּא כְסִֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who hides hatred uses lying lips, and he that utters a slander, is a fool.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The concealer of hatred [in his heart] has lying [(flattering)] lips, and he who utters slander is a fool.


###### Proverbs 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:19)


בְּרֹ֣ב דְּ֭בָרִים לֹ֣א יֶחְדַּל־פָּ֑שַׁע וְחוֹשֵׂ֖ךְ שְׂפָתָ֣יו מַשְׂכִּֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the multitude of words sin is not lacking: but he who restrains his lips is wise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With an abundance of words, offense will not cease, and he who spares his lips is wise.


###### Proverbs 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:20)


כֶּ֣סֶף נִ֭בְחָר לְשׁ֣וֹן צַדִּ֑יק לֵ֖ב רְשָׁעִ֣ים כִּמְעָֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The tongue of the just is like choice silver: the heart of the wicked is little worth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Choice silver is the tongue [i.e., the reproof] of the righteous one, but the understanding of the wicked is minute [(and they pay no heed to his reproof)].


###### Proverbs 10:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:21)


שִׂפְתֵ֣י צַ֭דִּיק יִרְע֣וּ רַבִּ֑ים וֶ֝אֱוִילִ֗ים בַּחֲסַר־לֵ֥ב יָמֽוּתוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The lips of the righteous feed many: but fools die for want of wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lips of the righteous one [(in prayer)] will feed many, but the fools, void of understanding [(and paying him no heed)], will die.


###### Proverbs 10:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:22)


בִּרְכַּ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה הִ֣יא תַעֲשִׁ֑יר וְלֹֽא־יוֹסִ֖ף עֶ֣צֶב עִמָּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The blessing of the Lord, it makes rich, and he adds no sorrow with it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The blessing of the L-rd, by itself, enriches, and [requires] no further toil along with it.


###### Proverbs 10:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:23)


כִּשְׂח֣וֹק לִ֭כְסִיל עֲשׂ֣וֹת זִמָּ֑ה וְ֝חׇכְמָ֗ה לְאִ֣ישׁ תְּבוּנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is as sport to a fool to do mischief: and likewise is it to a man of understanding to practise wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As play to the fool is acting upon evil counsel, as [acting upon] wisdom is [play] to the man of understanding.


###### Proverbs 10:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:24)


מְגוֹרַ֣ת רָ֭שָׁע הִ֣יא תְבוֹאֶ֑נּוּ וְתַאֲוַ֖ת צַדִּיקִ֣ים יִתֵּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What the wicked dreads shall come upon him: but the desire of the righteous shall be granted.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fear of the wicked one will come upon him [e.g., (Genesis 11:4 and 8)], and He will fulfill the desire of the righteous.


###### Proverbs 10:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:25)


כַּעֲב֣וֹר ס֭וּפָה וְאֵ֣ין רָשָׁ֑ע וְ֝צַדִּ֗יק יְס֣וֹד עוֹלָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the storm wind passes, the wicked is no more: but the righteous is an everlasting foundation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the tempest passes, the wicked one is no more, but the righteous one is the foundation of the world.


###### Proverbs 10:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:26)


כַּחֹ֤מֶץ**׀** לַשִּׁנַּ֗יִם וְכֶעָשָׁ֥ן לָעֵינָ֑יִם כֵּ֥ן הֶ֝עָצֵ֗ל לְשֹׁלְחָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As vinegar to the teeth, and as smoke to the eyes, so is the sluggard to those who send him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As vinegar to the teeth and as smoke to the eyes, so is the sluggard to his senders.


###### Proverbs 10:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:27)


יִרְאַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה תּוֹסִ֣יף יָמִ֑ים וּשְׁנ֖וֹת רְשָׁעִ֣ים תִּקְצֹֽרְנָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The fear of the Lord prolongs days: but the years of the wicked shall be shortened.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fear of the L-rd will increase days, but the years of the wicked shall be shortened.


###### Proverbs 10:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:28)


תּוֹחֶ֣לֶת צַדִּיקִ֣ים שִׂמְחָ֑ה וְתִקְוַ֖ת רְשָׁעִ֣ים תֹּאבֵֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The hope of the righteous is gladness: but the expectation of the wicked shall perish.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The hope of the righteous [will culminate in] joy, but the hope of the wicked will go lost.


###### Proverbs 10:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:29)


מָע֣וֹז לַ֭תֹּם דֶּ֣רֶךְ יְהֹוָ֑ה וּ֝מְחִתָּ֗ה לְפֹ֣עֲלֵי אָֽוֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The way of the Lord is a stronghold to the upright, but destruction to the workers of iniquity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The way of the L-rd is a stronghold to the innocent, and [(their not pursuing it)], a breaking to the workers of iniquity.


###### Proverbs 10:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:30)


צַ֭דִּיק לְעוֹלָ֣ם בַּל־יִמּ֑וֹט וּ֝רְשָׁעִ֗ים לֹ֣א יִשְׁכְּנוּ־אָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The righteous shall never be removed: but the wicked shall not inhabit the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The righteous one will not stoop forever, [(but ultimately will rise)], and the wicked shall not endure in the land.


###### Proverbs 10:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:31)


פִּֽי־צַ֭דִּיק יָנ֣וּב חׇכְמָ֑ה וּלְשׁ֥וֹן תַּ֝הְפֻּכ֗וֹת תִּכָּרֵֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The mouth of the just brings forth wisdom: but the perverse tongue shall be cut off.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The mouth of the righteous one will utter wisdom, but the tongue of contraries will be cut off.


###### Proverbs 10:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/10:32)


שִׂפְתֵ֣י צַ֭דִּיק יֵדְע֣וּן רָצ֑וֹן וּפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים תַּהְפֻּכֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The lips of the righteous know what is acceptable: but the mouth of the wicked is crookedness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lips of the righteous one know [how to] conciliate [G-d and man], but the mouth of the wicked [speaks] contraries.



## Chapter 11

###### Proverbs 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:1)


מֹאזְנֵ֣י מִ֭רְמָה תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֑ה וְאֶ֖בֶן שְׁלֵמָ֣ה רְצוֹנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A false balance is abomination to the Lord: but a just weight is his delight.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Deceitful scales are the abomination of the L-rd, and a whole weight is His will.


###### Proverbs 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:2)


בָּֽא־זָ֭דוֹן וַיָּבֹ֣א קָל֑וֹן וְֽאֶת־צְנוּעִ֥ים חׇכְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When pride comes, then comes shame: but with the lowly is wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When willfulness comes there comes shame, and with the humble [there comes] wisdom.


###### Proverbs 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:3)


תֻּמַּ֣ת יְשָׁרִ֣ים תַּנְחֵ֑ם וְסֶ֖לֶף בֹּגְדִ֣ים (ושדם) [יְשׇׁדֵּֽם]׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The integrity of the upright shall guide them: but the perverseness of the faithless shall destroy them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The innocence of the just shall lead them, and the perversion of the faithless shall despoil them.


###### Proverbs 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:4)


לֹא־יוֹעִ֣יל ה֭וֹן בְּי֣וֹם עֶבְרָ֑ה וּ֝צְדָקָ֗ה תַּצִּ֥יל מִמָּֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Riches profit not in the day of wrath: but righteousness delivers from death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Wealth will not avail in the day of wrath, but charity will rescue from death.


###### Proverbs 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:5)


צִדְקַ֣ת תָּ֭מִים תְּיַשֵּׁ֣ר דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וּ֝בְרִשְׁעָת֗וֹ יִפֹּ֥ל רָשָֽׁע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The righteousness of the innocent shall direct his way: but the wicked shall fall by his own wickedness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The righteousness of the innocent one straightens his path, and the wicked one will fall in his [own] wickedness.


###### Proverbs 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:6)


צִדְקַ֣ת יְ֭שָׁרִים תַּצִּילֵ֑ם וּ֝בְהַוַּ֗ת בֹּגְדִ֥ים יִלָּכֵֽדוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The righteousness of the upright shall deliver them: but the faithless shall be caught in their own malice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The righteousness of the just shall rescue them, and the faithless will be trapped in the destruction [they have plotted].


###### Proverbs 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:7)


בְּמ֤וֹת אָדָ֣ם רָ֭שָׁע תֹּאבַ֣ד תִּקְוָ֑ה וְתוֹחֶ֖לֶת אוֹנִ֣ים אָבָֽדָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When a wicked man dies, his expectation shall perish: and the hope of unjust men perishes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When a wicked man dies, the hope [of his hangers-on] is lost, and the hope of his sons perishes [(his merit not "standing" for them, [as opposed to the lot of the sons of the righteous])].


###### Proverbs 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:8)


צַ֭דִּיק מִצָּרָ֣ה נֶחֱלָ֑ץ וַיָּבֹ֖א רָשָׁ֣ע תַּחְתָּֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The righteous is delivered out of trouble, and the wicked comes in his stead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The righteous one is rescued from affliction [("when the wicked man dies")], and the wicked one comes in his place.


###### Proverbs 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:9)


בְּפֶ֗ה חָ֭נֵף יַשְׁחִ֣ת רֵעֵ֑הוּ וּ֝בְדַ֗עַת צַדִּיקִ֥ים יֵחָלֵֽצוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A hypocrite destroys his neighbour with his mouth: but the just shall be delivered through knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With [his] mouth, the flatterer destroys [his friend], but with knowledge [of the Torah, which exhorts against succumbing to the flatterer's enticements (viz. Deuteronomy 13:9)], the righteous are rescued.


###### Proverbs 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:10)


בְּט֣וּב צַ֭דִּיקִים תַּעֲלֹ֣ץ קִרְיָ֑ה וּבַאֲבֹ֖ד רְשָׁעִ֣ים רִנָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When it goes well with the righteous, the city rejoices: but when the wicked perish, there is jubilation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The city exults in the good of the righteous, and when the wicked perish there is rejoicing.


###### Proverbs 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:11)


בְּבִרְכַּ֣ת יְ֭שָׁרִים תָּר֣וּם קָ֑רֶת וּבְפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים תֵּהָרֵֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By the blessing of the upright a city is exalted: but it is overthrown by the mouth of the wicked.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
By the blessing of the just the ceiling is sustained [(When the kings of Judah were just, the "ceiling" of the Temple was kept from falling by their prayers)], but by the mouth of the wicked it is razed.


###### Proverbs 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:12)


בׇּז־לְרֵעֵ֥הוּ חֲסַר־לֵ֑ב וְאִ֖ישׁ תְּבוּנ֣וֹת יַחֲרִֽישׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that is void of wisdom despises his neighbour: but a man of understanding holds his peace.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who shames his friend lacks a heart, and the man of understanding will remain silent [(when he is thus shamed, viz. I Samuel 10:27)].


###### Proverbs 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:13)


הוֹלֵ֣ךְ רָ֭כִיל מְגַלֶּה־סּ֑וֹד וְנֶאֱמַן־ר֝֗וּחַ מְכַסֶּ֥ה דָבָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A talebearer reveals secrets: but he that is of a faithful spirit conceals the matter.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who reveals a secret is a tale-bearer, and the faithful of spirit conceals a thing.


###### Proverbs 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:14)


בְּאֵ֣ין תַּ֭חְבֻּלוֹת יִפׇּל־עָ֑ם וּ֝תְשׁוּעָ֗ה בְּרֹ֣ב יוֹעֵֽץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Where there is no counsel, a people falls: but in the multitude of counsellors there is safety.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When [(in a time of affliction)], there are no stratagems [(e.g., taking stock of one's acts, fasting, repentance, etc)], the people shall fall, and there is salvation in much counsel.


###### Proverbs 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:15)


רַע־יֵ֭רוֹעַ כִּי־עָ֣רַב זָ֑ר וְשֹׂנֵ֖א תֹקְעִ֣ים בּוֹטֵֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that is surety for a stranger shall smart for it: but he that hates suretyship is safe.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One will be utterly broken for having gone security for a stranger, and the hater of hand-shaking [(in pledge)] shall be secure.


###### Proverbs 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:16)


אֵֽשֶׁת־חֵ֭ן תִּתְמֹ֣ךְ כָּב֑וֹד וְ֝עָרִיצִ֗ים יִתְמְכוּ־עֹֽשֶׁר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A woman of charm obtains honour: and powerful men obtain wealth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The woman of favor [(the congregation of Israel)] courts honor [(the honor of the L-rd and His Torah)], and tyrants court wealth.


###### Proverbs 11:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:17)


גֹּמֵ֣ל נַ֭פְשׁוֹ אִ֣ישׁ חָ֑סֶד וְעֹכֵ֥ר שְׁ֝אֵר֗וֹ אַכְזָרִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The merciful man does good to his own soul: but he that is cruel troubles his own flesh.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who is kind to his kin is a saintly man, and the cruel man [is he who] vexes his kin.


###### Proverbs 11:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:18)


רָשָׁ֗ע עֹשֶׂ֥ה פְעֻלַּת־שָׁ֑קֶר וְזֹרֵ֥עַ צְ֝דָקָ֗ה שֶׂ֣כֶר אֱמֶֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wicked man earns wages of deceit: but he that sows righteousness has a sure reward.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The deed of the wicked one betrays him [(i.e., it does not fulfill his intent)], but one who sows charity — [it (the charity) is] a true weir [i.e., it "traps" what is intended as a weir traps fish].


###### Proverbs 11:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:19)


כֵּן־צְדָקָ֥ה לְחַיִּ֑ים וּמְרַדֵּ֖ף רָעָ֣ה לְמוֹתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that is firm in righteousness achieves life; but he that pursues evil comes to his own death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The truth [i.e., the end result] of charity is life, and one who pursues evil [does so] for his death.


###### Proverbs 11:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:20)


תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה עִקְּשֵׁי־לֵ֑ב וּ֝רְצוֹנ֗וֹ תְּמִ֣ימֵי דָֽרֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They that are of a crooked heart are an abomination to the Lord: but such as are upright in their way are his delight.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The abomination of the L-rd are the perverse of heart, and those who walk in innocence are His desire.


###### Proverbs 11:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:21)


יָ֣ד לְ֭יָד לֹא־יִנָּ֣קֶה רָּ֑ע וְזֶ֖רַע צַדִּיקִ֣ים נִמְלָֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They who join hands for wicked ends shall not go unpunished: but the seed of the righteous shall be delivered.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Punishment will come from G-d's] hand to [the] hand [of the sinner]. He will not be cleansed [i.e., absolved] of the evil [he has done]; but the righteous [(sometimes do)] escape [(in the merit of their fathers)].


###### Proverbs 11:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:22)


נֶ֣זֶם זָ֭הָב בְּאַ֣ף חֲזִ֑יר אִשָּׁ֥ה יָ֝פָ֗ה וְסָ֣רַת טָֽעַם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Like a jewel of gold in a swine’s snout, so is a fair woman without discretion.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As a golden ring [(which becomes besmirched)] in the snout of a pig, so is a beautiful woman [(a Torah scholar)] departing from wisdom [(Torah)].


###### Proverbs 11:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:23)


תַּאֲוַ֣ת צַדִּיקִ֣ים אַךְ־ט֑וֹב תִּקְוַ֖ת רְשָׁעִ֣ים עֶבְרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The desire of the righteous is only good: but the expectation of the wicked is wrath.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The desire of the righteous is good alone; the hope [i.e., the expectation] of the wicked is wrath [(Gehinnom)].


###### Proverbs 11:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:24)


יֵ֣שׁ מְ֭פַזֵּר וְנוֹסָ֥ף ע֑וֹד וְחֹשֵׂ֥ךְ מִ֝יֹּ֗שֶׁר אַךְ־לְמַחְסֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is one who gives freely, and yet increases, another spares unjustly, but comes to want.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is scattering [of one's wealth (in charity)] with even more increase, and holding back from just [(giving)] for want alone.


###### Proverbs 11:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:25)


נֶֽפֶשׁ־בְּרָכָ֥ה תְדֻשָּׁ֑ן וּ֝מַרְוֶ֗ה גַּם־ה֥וּא יוֹרֶֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The liberal soul shall be made rich: and he who waters shall be nourished also himself.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The magnanimous soul will [itself] grow fat, and the sater [of the poor] will himself be sated [with good].


###### Proverbs 11:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:26)


מֹ֣נֵֽעַ בָּ֭ר יִקְּבֻ֣הוּ לְא֑וֹם וּ֝בְרָכָ֗ה לְרֹ֣אשׁ מַשְׁבִּֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who holds back corn, the people shall curse him: but blessing shall be upon the head of him who sells freely.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who withholds produce [(Torah teaching)] will be cursed by the nations, but blessing [will descend] upon the head of the provider.


###### Proverbs 11:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:27)


שֹׁ֣חֵֽר ט֭וֹב יְבַקֵּ֣שׁ רָצ֑וֹן וְדֹרֵ֖שׁ רָעָ֣ה תְבוֹאֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who diligently seeks good procures favour: but he who seeks mischief, it shall come to him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who desires good [(for others and chastises them to that end)] seeks conciliation [i.e., that the L-rd be reconciled to them], but he who seeks out evil [for them] — it will come to him.


###### Proverbs 11:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:28)


בּוֹטֵ֣חַ בְּ֭עׇשְׁרוֹ ה֣וּא יִפּ֑וֹל וְ֝כֶעָלֶ֗ה צַדִּיקִ֥ים יִפְרָֽחוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who trusts in his riches shall fall: but the righteous shall flourish like foliage.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who trusts in his wealth shall fall, but the righteous shall sprout as leaves.


###### Proverbs 11:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:29)


עֹכֵ֣ר בֵּ֭יתוֹ יִנְחַל־ר֑וּחַ וְעֶ֥בֶד אֱ֝וִ֗יל לַֽחֲכַם־לֵֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who troubles his own house shall inherit wind: and the fool shall be servant to the wise of heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who inherits the wind [i.e., the lazy man, who neither studies Torah nor works] vexes his household [(leaving them without sustenance)], and [this] fool will [ultimately] become a servant to the wise of heart.


###### Proverbs 11:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:30)


פְּֽרִי־צַ֭דִּיק עֵ֣ץ חַיִּ֑ים וְלֹקֵ֖חַ נְפָשׁ֣וֹת חָכָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The fruit of the righteous is a tree of life; and he that wins souls is wise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fruits of [the deeds of] the righteous one are a tree of life [for the world], and he who takes [i.e., acquires] souls [(whom he teaches the way of good [viz. Genesis 12:5])] is wise.


###### Proverbs 11:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/11:31)


הֵ֣ן צַ֭דִּיק בָּאָ֣רֶץ יְשֻׁלָּ֑ם אַ֝֗ף כִּֽי־רָשָׁ֥ע וְחוֹטֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, the just man shall be recompensed on earth: how much more the wicked and the sinner!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If the righteous one is paid [i.e., punished for his evil deeds] upon this earth, how much more so [will retribution come (in this world or the next) to] the wicked one (who persists in) sinning.



## Chapter 12

###### Proverbs 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:1)


אֹהֵ֣ב מ֭וּסָר אֹ֣הֵֽב דָּ֑עַת וְשׂוֹנֵ֖א תוֹכַ֣חַת בָּֽעַר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whoever loves knowledge loves discipline: but he who hates reproof is stupid.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who loves chastisement loves wisdom, but he who hates rebuke is a boor.


###### Proverbs 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:2)


ט֗וֹב יָפִ֣יק רָ֭צוֹן מֵיְהֹוָ֑ה וְאִ֖ישׁ מְזִמּ֣וֹת יַרְשִֽׁיעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A good man obtains favour of the Lord: but a man of wicked devices will he condemn.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The good man draws forth favor from the L-rd [(for the world)], but the scheming one [draws forth] evil.


###### Proverbs 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:3)


לֹא־יִכּ֣וֹן אָדָ֣ם בְּרֶ֑שַׁע וְשֹׁ֥רֶשׁ צַ֝דִּיקִ֗ים בַּל־יִמּֽוֹט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man shall not be established by wickedness: but the root of the righteous shall not be moved.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man will not establish himself with wickedness, but the root of the righteous will not founder.


###### Proverbs 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:4)


אֵֽשֶׁת־חַ֭יִל עֲטֶ֣רֶת בַּעְלָ֑הּ וּכְרָקָ֖ב בְּעַצְמוֹתָ֣יו מְבִישָֽׁה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A virtuous woman is a crown to her husband: but she that acts shamefully is as rottenness in his bones.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A woman of valor is her husband's crown, but a shameful one is like rot in his bones.


###### Proverbs 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:5)


מַחְשְׁב֣וֹת צַדִּיקִ֣ים מִשְׁפָּ֑ט תַּחְבֻּל֖וֹת רְשָׁעִ֣ים מִרְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The thoughts of the righteous are right: but the counsels of the wicked are deceit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The thoughts of the righteous are [to do] justice; the stratagems of the wicked are [to work] deceit.


###### Proverbs 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:6)


דִּבְרֵ֣י רְשָׁעִ֣ים אֱרׇב־דָּ֑ם וּפִ֥י יְ֝שָׁרִ֗ים יַצִּילֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The words of the wicked are to lie in wait for blood: but the mouth of the upright shall deliver them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The words of the wicked are a siege upon blood [i.e., they seek to kill], but the mouth of the just will rescue them [(their intended victims)].


###### Proverbs 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:7)


הָפ֣וֹךְ רְשָׁעִ֣ים וְאֵינָ֑ם וּבֵ֖ית צַדִּיקִ֣ים יַעֲמֹֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wicked are overthrown, and are no more: but the house of the righteous shall stand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the wicked are overthrown, they are no more, but the house of the righteous will stand.


###### Proverbs 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:8)


לְֽפִי־שִׂ֭כְלוֹ יְהֻלַּל־אִ֑ישׁ וְנַעֲוֵה־לֵ֝֗ב יִהְיֶ֥ה לָבֽוּז׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man shall be commended according to his intelligence: but he that is of perverse mind shall be despised.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
According to a man's wisdom [(whether great or small)] will he be praised [i.e., will he receive his reward], but the perverse of heart will be put to shame.


###### Proverbs 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:9)


ט֣וֹב נִ֭קְלֶה וְעֶ֣בֶד ל֑וֹ מִ֝מִּתְכַּבֵּ֗ד וַחֲסַר־לָֽחֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Better is one lightly esteemed who owns a servant, than one who pranks himself but lacks bread.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better one who esteems himself lightly and is a servant unto himself, than one who holds himself honored and lacks bread [(not condescending to serve himself!)]


###### Proverbs 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:10)


יוֹדֵ֣עַ צַ֭דִּיק נֶ֣פֶשׁ בְּהֶמְתּ֑וֹ וְֽרַחֲמֵ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים אַכְזָרִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A righteous man regards the life of his beast: but the heart of the wicked is cruel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The righteous one knows the needs of his beast, but the mercies of the wicked are cruel.


###### Proverbs 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:11)


עֹבֵ֣ד אַ֭דְמָתוֹ יִֽשְׂבַּֽע־לָ֑חֶם וּמְרַדֵּ֖ף רֵיקִ֣ים חֲסַר־לֵֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that tills his land shall have plenty of bread: but he that follows vain persons is void of understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who works his earth will be sated with bread, but he who pursues vanities is lacking a heart.


###### Proverbs 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:12)


חָמַ֣ד רָ֭שָׁע מְצ֣וֹד רָעִ֑ים וְשֹׁ֖רֶשׁ צַדִּיקִ֣ים יִתֵּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wicked man craves – he is snared by mischief: but the righteous strike root.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wicked one desires the hunting of the evil ones [i.e., what thieves wrest from their victims], but the root of the righteous will give [(its own fruit)].


###### Proverbs 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:13)


בְּפֶ֣שַׁע שְׂ֭פָתַיִם מוֹקֵ֣שׁ רָ֑ע וַיֵּצֵ֖א מִצָּרָ֣ה צַדִּֽיק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wicked is snared by the transgression of his lips: but the just shall come out of trouble.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
By the sin of the lips [(of the generation of the flood, who said [Job 21:15]: "What is G-d that we should serve Him?")], an evil snare [(the flood, consumed them)], but the righteous one [(Noah)] escaped calamity.


###### Proverbs 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:14)


מִפְּרִ֣י פִי־אִ֭ישׁ יִשְׂבַּע־ט֑וֹב וּגְמ֥וּל יְדֵי־אָ֝דָ֗ם (ישוב) [יָשִׁ֥יב] לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man shall be satisfied with good by the fruit of his mouth: and the recompense of a man’s hands shall be rendered to him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From the fruits of a man's mouth, he will be sated with good [i.e., As reward for their discourse, Torah scholars enjoy good in this world], and the reward of a man's hand, He returns to him [i.e., and the principal remains for them for the world to come].


###### Proverbs 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:15)


דֶּ֣רֶךְ אֱ֭וִיל יָשָׁ֣ר בְּעֵינָ֑יו וְשֹׁמֵ֖עַ לְעֵצָ֣ה חָכָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
