The way of a fool is right in his own eyes: but he that hearkens unto counsel is wise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The way of the fool is just in his eyes — but he who heeds counsel is wise.


###### Proverbs 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:16)


אֱוִ֗יל בַּ֭יּוֹם יִוָּדַ֣ע כַּעְס֑וֹ וְכֹסֶ֖ה קָל֣וֹן עָרֽוּם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A fool’s wrath is presently known: but a prudent man conceals disgrace.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fool, on the [very] day [that he is angered] makes known his anger [(by insulting his antagonist in public)], but the wise one conceals shame.


###### Proverbs 12:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:17)


יָפִ֣יחַ אֱ֭מוּנָה יַגִּ֣יד צֶ֑דֶק וְעֵ֖ד שְׁקָרִ֣ים מִרְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who utters truth gives just evidence: but a false witness utters deceit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The [(habitual)] speaker of truth will testify justly, but the witness who [(habitually)] lies [will testify] deceitfully.


###### Proverbs 12:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:18)


יֵ֣שׁ בּ֭וֹטֶה כְּמַדְקְר֣וֹת חָ֑רֶב וּלְשׁ֖וֹן חֲכָמִ֣ים מַרְפֵּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is one who speaks like the piercings of a sword: but the tongue of the wise is health.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There are those whose speaking pierces like the sword [i.e., incites to strife], but the tongue of the wise [(who pursue peace)] "heals" [(that "speaking")].


###### Proverbs 12:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:19)


שְֽׂפַת־אֱ֭מֶת תִּכּ֣וֹן לָעַ֑ד וְעַד־אַ֝רְגִּ֗יעָה לְשׁ֣וֹן שָֽׁקֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The lip of truth shall be established for ever: but a lying tongue is but for a moment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
True speech is established forever, but the false tongue for a moment only.


###### Proverbs 12:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:20)


מִ֭רְמָה בְּלֶב־חֹ֣רְשֵׁי רָ֑ע וּֽלְיֹעֲצֵ֖י שָׁל֣וֹם שִׂמְחָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Deceit is in the heart of those who imagine evil: but the counsellors of peace have joy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Thoughts of] deceit [(which banish joy)] are in the heart of the plotters of evil, but in [the heart of] the counselors of peace [there is] joy.


###### Proverbs 12:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:21)


לֹא־יְאֻנֶּ֣ה לַצַּדִּ֣יק כׇּל־אָ֑וֶן וּ֝רְשָׁעִ֗ים מָ֣לְאוּ רָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
No evil shall happen to the just: but the wicked are filled with mischief.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
No wrong [i.e., no unwitting transgression] will befall the righteous one, but the wicked are [unceasingly] filled with evil.


###### Proverbs 12:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:22)


תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה שִׂפְתֵי־שָׁ֑קֶר וְעֹשֵׂ֖י אֱמוּנָ֣ה רְצוֹנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lying lips are abomination to the Lord: but they who deal truly are his delight.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
False lips are an abomination to the L-rd, and those who act in faith are His desire.


###### Proverbs 12:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:23)


אָדָ֣ם עָ֭רוּם כֹּ֣סֶה דָּ֑עַת וְלֵ֥ב כְּ֝סִילִ֗ים יִקְרָ֥א אִוֶּֽלֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A prudent man conceals knowledge: but the heart of fools proclaims his foolishness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise man conceals [even his] wisdom, and the heart of the fools calls out foolishness.


###### Proverbs 12:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:24)


יַד־חָרוּצִ֥ים תִּמְשׁ֑וֹל וּ֝רְמִיָּ֗ה תִּהְיֶ֥ה לָמַֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The hand of the diligent shall bear rule: but the slothful shall be under tribute.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The hand of the just shall prosper, but [that of] deceivers shall melt.


###### Proverbs 12:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:25)


דְּאָגָ֣ה בְלֶב־אִ֣ישׁ יַשְׁחֶ֑נָּה וְדָבָ֖ר ט֣וֹב יְשַׂמְּחֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Anxiety in a man’s heart dejects it: but a good word gladdens it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[If there is] sorrow in a man's heart, let him banish it [(from his mind)], and a good thing [i.e., Torah] will convert it to joy.


###### Proverbs 12:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:26)


יָתֵ֣ר מֵרֵעֵ֣הוּ צַדִּ֑יק וְדֶ֖רֶךְ רְשָׁעִ֣ים תַּתְעֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The righteous guides his neighbour aright: but the way of the wicked leads them astray.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
More yielding than his neighbor is the righteous one, and the way of the wicked leads them astray, [and, as a result],


###### Proverbs 12:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:27)


לֹא־יַחֲרֹ֣ךְ רְמִיָּ֣ה צֵיד֑וֹ וְהוֹן־אָדָ֖ם יָקָ֣ר חָרֽוּץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The slothful man does not roast his catch: but the industrious man arrives at precious wealth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
he [(the wicked one)] will not [get to] roast his game [caught by his] deceit; but the wealth of an honest man is precious [and enduring].


###### Proverbs 12:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/12:28)


בְּאֹֽרַח־צְדָקָ֥ה חַיִּ֑ים וְדֶ֖רֶךְ נְתִיבָ֣הֿ אַל־מָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the way of righteousness is life; and in its path there is no death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
On the road of righteousness there is life, and in its pathway, no death.



## Chapter 13

###### Proverbs 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:1)


בֵּ֣ן חָ֭כָם מ֣וּסַר אָ֑ב וְ֝לֵ֗ץ לֹא־שָׁמַ֥ע גְּעָרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wise son hears his father’s instruction: but a scorner does not accept rebuke.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A wise son [loves] the mussar of [his] father, but a scoffer does not hear [i.e., accept] rebuke.


###### Proverbs 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:2)


מִפְּרִ֣י פִי־אִ֭ישׁ יֹ֣אכַל ט֑וֹב וְנֶ֖פֶשׁ בֹּגְדִ֣ים חָמָֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man shall eat good from the fruit of his mouth: but the desire of the faithless is for violence.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From the fruit of a man's mouth [i.e., as the reward of his Torah], he will eat good [(in this world, with the principal remaining for the world to come)], but the desire of the faithless is violence.


###### Proverbs 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:3)


נֹצֵ֣ר פִּ֭יו שֹׁמֵ֣ר נַפְשׁ֑וֹ פֹּשֵׂ֥ק שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו מְחִתָּה־לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who guards his mouth keeps his life: but he who opens wide his lips shall have destruction.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who guards his mouth guards his soul; he who opens wide his lips [(to speak his fill, always)], it is breaking to him.


###### Proverbs 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:4)


מִתְאַוָּ֣ה וָ֭אַיִן נַפְשׁ֣וֹ עָצֵ֑ל וְנֶ֖פֶשׁ חָרֻצִ֣ים תְּדֻשָּֽׁן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The soul of the sluggard desires, and has nothing: but the soul of the diligent shall be richly supplied.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The soul of the sluggard lusts [for everything] and has nothing, but the soul of the just [(who eat of the toil of their hands)] is fatted.


###### Proverbs 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:5)


דְּבַר־שֶׁ֭קֶר יִשְׂנָ֣א צַדִּ֑יק וְ֝רָשָׁ֗ע יַבְאִ֥ישׁ וְיַחְפִּֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A righteous man hates lying: but a wicked man is vile, and comes to shame.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The righteous one hates a false report, but the wicked one shames and sullies [(men with it)].


###### Proverbs 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:6)


צְ֭דָקָה תִּצֹּ֣ר תׇּם־דָּ֑רֶךְ וְ֝רִשְׁעָ֗ה תְּסַלֵּ֥ף חַטָּֽאת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Righteousness keeps him that is upright in the way: but wickedness overthrows the sinner.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Charity will guard him who walks in innocence, but [his] wickedness will pervert the sinner.


###### Proverbs 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:7)


יֵ֣שׁ מִ֭תְעַשֵּׁר וְאֵ֣ין כֹּ֑ל מִ֝תְרוֹשֵׁ֗שׁ וְה֣וֹן רָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is one who pretends to be rich, yet has nothing: there is another who pretends to be poor, yet has great riches.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Some flaunt wealth, having nothing, and others [flaunt] poverty, having great wealth.


###### Proverbs 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:8)


כֹּ֣פֶר נֶפֶשׁ־אִ֣ישׁ עׇשְׁר֑וֹ וְ֝רָ֗שׁ לֹא־שָׁמַ֥ע גְּעָרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The ransom of a man’s life are his riches: but the poor hears no threat.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man's wealth [(when giving charity)] may redeem his soul — [but only] when the poor man hears no rebuke [in receiving it].


###### Proverbs 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:9)


אוֹר־צַדִּיקִ֥ים יִשְׂמָ֑ח וְנֵ֖ר רְשָׁעִ֣ים יִדְעָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The light of the righteous rejoices: but the lamp of the wicked shall be put out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The light [i.e., the soul] of the righteous will rejoice, but the lamp of the wicked will flicker out.


###### Proverbs 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:10)


רַק־בְּ֭זָדוֹן יִתֵּ֣ן מַצָּ֑ה וְאֶת־נ֖וֹעָצִ֣ים חׇכְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Only by pride comes contention: but with the well advised is wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only by the willful is strife fomented, but among those who take counsel, there is wisdom [(and peace)].


###### Proverbs 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:11)


ה֭וֹן מֵהֶ֣בֶל יִמְעָ֑ט וְקֹבֵ֖ץ עַל־יָ֣ד יַרְבֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wealth gotten by vanity shall be diminished: but he that gathers by labour shall increase.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Wealth [i.e., Torah] [accumulated] in "bundles" will diminish, but that gathered by the hand [i.e., by patient study and review] will increase.


###### Proverbs 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:12)


תּוֹחֶ֣לֶת מְ֭מֻשָּׁכָה מַחֲלָה־לֵ֑ב וְעֵ֥ץ חַ֝יִּ֗ים תַּאֲוָ֥ה בָאָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hope deferred makes the heart sick: but desire fulfilled is a tree of life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A drawn out hope [i.e., trusting in vain to one's friend)] sickens the heart, but a tree of life is desire attained.


###### Proverbs 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:13)


בָּ֣ז לְ֭דָבָר יֵחָ֣בֶל ל֑וֹ וִירֵ֥א מִ֝צְוָ֗ה ה֣וּא יְשֻׁלָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who despises the word shall be punished; but he who fears the commandment shall be rewarded.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who cheapens a thing [(of Torah)] will be taken in pledge for it, but he who fears a mitzvah will be paid [i.e., rewarded].


###### Proverbs 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:14)


תּוֹרַ֣ת חָ֭כָם מְק֣וֹר חַיִּ֑ים לָ֝ס֗וּר מִמֹּ֥קְשֵׁי מָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Tora of the wise is a fountain of life, to depart from the snares of death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The Torah of the wise man is a well of life, [(teaching him)] to turn away from the snares of death [i.e., sins].


###### Proverbs 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:15)


שֵֽׂכֶל־ט֭וֹב יִתֶּן־חֵ֑ן וְדֶ֖רֶךְ בֹּגְדִ֣ים אֵיתָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Good understanding gives grace: but the way of the faithless is rough.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Right understanding invests one with grace, but the way of the faithless is strong [i.e., severe to him and to others].


###### Proverbs 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:16)


כׇּל־עָ֭רוּם יַעֲשֶׂ֣ה בְדָ֑עַת וּ֝כְסִ֗יל יִפְרֹ֥שׂ אִוֶּֽלֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In everything a prudent man acts with knowledge: but a fool lays bare his folly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every wise man proceeds with discretion, but the fool broadcasts [his] folly.


###### Proverbs 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:17)


מַלְאָ֣ךְ רָ֭שָׁע יִפֹּ֣ל בְּרָ֑ע וְצִ֖יר אֱמוּנִ֣ים מַרְפֵּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wicked messenger falls into mischief: but a faithful ambassador is health.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A wicked messenger will fall into evil [(e.g., Bilam [Numbers 22:35])], but the faithful emissary [(Moses)] enhances [(his commission)].


###### Proverbs 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:18)


רֵ֣ישׁ וְ֭קָלוֹן פּוֹרֵ֣עַ מוּסָ֑ר וְשֹׁמֵ֖ר תּוֹכַ֣חַת יְכֻבָּֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Poverty and shame come to him who refuses instruction: but he who heeds reproof shall be honoured.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Poverty and shame [come to him] who casts aside mussar, but he who heeds rebuke will be honored.


###### Proverbs 13:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:19)


תַּאֲוָ֣ה נִ֭הְיָה תֶּעֱרַ֣ב לְנָ֑פֶשׁ וְתוֹעֲבַ֥ת כְּ֝סִילִ֗ים ס֣וּר מֵרָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A desire subdued is sweet to the soul: but it is abomination to fools to depart from evil.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lust realized is sweet to the soul — [wherefore] it is the abomination of fools to turn away from evil.


###### Proverbs 13:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:20)


(הלוך) [הוֹלֵ֣ךְ] אֶת־חֲכָמִ֣ים (וחכם) [יֶחְכָּ֑ם] וְרֹעֶ֖ה כְסִילִ֣ים יֵרֽוֹעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that walks with wise men shall be wise: but a companion of fools shall suffer harm.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who walks with the wise will grow wise, but the befriender of fools will be broken.


###### Proverbs 13:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:21)


חַ֭טָּאִים תְּרַדֵּ֣ף רָעָ֑ה וְאֶת־צַ֝דִּיקִ֗ים יְשַׁלֶּם־טֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Evil pursues sinners: but to the righteous good shall be repaid.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Their] evil will pursue the sinners [(until they are destroyed by it)], and the righteous will be paid [i.e., rewarded] by [their] good.


###### Proverbs 13:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:22)


ט֗וֹב יַנְחִ֥יל בְּנֵֽי־בָנִ֑ים וְצָפ֥וּן לַ֝צַּדִּ֗יק חֵ֣יל חוֹטֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A good man leaves an inheritance to his children’s children: but the wealth of the sinner is laid up for the just.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The good [man] bequeaths [(his merits and his wealth)] to [his] grandchildren, but the wealth of the sinner is secreted for the righteous one.


###### Proverbs 13:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:23)


רׇב־אֹ֭כֶל נִ֣יר רָאשִׁ֑ים וְיֵ֥שׁ נִ֝סְפֶּ֗ה בְּלֹ֣א מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Much food is in the well tilled acre of the poor: but sometimes ruin comes for want of judgment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Much food [comes to the world through] the plowing of the poor [i.e., Much Torah is produced by the disciples ("the poor"), whose teachers ("the rich") "plow" them with pilpul (dialectic)], but there are [those of them] who perish for lack of judgment [i.e., for improper deportment].


###### Proverbs 13:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:24)


חוֹשֵׂ֣ךְ שִׁ֭בְטוֹ שׂוֹנֵ֣א בְנ֑וֹ וְ֝אֹהֲב֗וֹ שִׁחֲר֥וֹ מוּסָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that spares his rod hates his son: but he that loves him chastises him early.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who spares his rod [will come] to hate his child [(for improper deportment)], but he who loves him chastises him betimes.


###### Proverbs 13:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/13:25)


צַדִּ֗יק אֹ֭כֵֽל לְשֹׂ֣בַע נַפְשׁ֑וֹ וּבֶ֖טֶן רְשָׁעִ֣ים תֶּחְסָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The righteous eats to satisfy his soul: but the belly of the wicked shall feel want.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The righteous one eats to [(what he perceives as)] the satiety of his soul, but the stomach of the wicked [(in their mind)] is [always] lacking.



## Chapter 14

###### Proverbs 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:1)


חַכְמ֣וֹת נָ֭שִׁים בָּנְתָ֣ה בֵיתָ֑הּ וְ֝אִוֶּ֗לֶת בְּיָדֶ֥יהָ תֶהֶרְסֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wisdom of women builds her house: but Folly plucks it down with her hands.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wisest of the women builds her house, and the foolish one destroys it with her own hands.


###### Proverbs 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:2)


הוֹלֵ֣ךְ בְּ֭יׇשְׁרוֹ יְרֵ֣א יְהֹוָ֑ה וּנְל֖וֹז דְּרָכָ֣יו בּוֹזֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who walks in his uprightness fears the Lord: but he who is perverse in his ways despises him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who fears the L-rd walks in uprightness, and he who is perverse in his ways shames Him.


###### Proverbs 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:3)


בְּֽפִי־אֱ֭וִיל חֹ֣טֶר גַּאֲוָ֑ה וְשִׂפְתֵ֥י חֲ֝כָמִ֗ים תִּשְׁמוּרֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the mouth of the foolish is a rod of pride: but the lips of the wise shall preserve them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the fool is the stick of pride, but the lips of the wise will guard them.


###### Proverbs 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:4)


בְּאֵ֣ין אֲ֭לָפִים אֵב֣וּס בָּ֑ר וְרׇב־תְּ֝בוּא֗וֹת בְּכֹ֣חַ שֽׁוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Where no oxen are, the crib is clean: but much increase comes by the strength of the ox.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Where there are no oxen, the manger is clean — but there is much produce in the strength of the ox. [(Where there are no Torah scholars, the teaching is not in accordance with the halachah)].


###### Proverbs 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:5)


עֵ֣ד אֱ֭מוּנִים לֹ֣א יְכַזֵּ֑ב וְיָפִ֥יחַ כְּ֝זָבִ֗ים עֵ֣ד שָֽׁקֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A trusty witness is he that does not lie: but he that utters lies is a false witness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The honest witness will not deceive, but the false witness spouts deceptions.


###### Proverbs 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:6)


בִּקֶּשׁ־לֵ֣ץ חׇכְמָ֣ה וָאָ֑יִן וְדַ֖עַת לְנָב֣וֹן נָקָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A scorner seeks wisdom, and does not find it: but knowledge is easy to him who understands.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The scoffer will seek wisdom [(when he requires it)] and not find it [in his heart], but wisdom will come easily to the understanding one.


###### Proverbs 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:7)


לֵ֣ךְ מִ֭נֶּגֶד לְאִ֣ישׁ כְּסִ֑יל וּבַל־יָ֝דַ֗עְתָּ שִׂפְתֵי־דָֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Go from the presence of a foolish man, for thou perceivest not in him the lips of knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Go far away from the foolish man [i.e., do not befriend him], or you will not know lips of wisdom.


###### Proverbs 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:8)


חׇכְמַ֣ת עָ֭רוּם הָבִ֣ין דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וְאִוֶּ֖לֶת כְּסִילִ֣ים מִרְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wisdom of the prudent is to understand his way: but the folly of fools is deceit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wisdom of the wise one is understanding his way [i.e., weighing his decisions]; the folly of the fools is [the] deceit [in their hearts (which prompts them to folly)].


###### Proverbs 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:9)


אֱ֭וִלִים יָלִ֣יץ אָשָׁ֑ם וּבֵ֖ין יְשָׁרִ֣ים רָצֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Guilt mocks fools: but the favour of God is among the upright.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Monetary] reparations [(to those they have offended)] will plead for the fools, [(viz. Samuel 6:4)], but among the just [(G-d and His people)] good will [(will suffice)].


###### Proverbs 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:10)


לֵ֗ב י֭וֹדֵעַ מׇרַּ֣ת נַפְשׁ֑וֹ וּ֝בְשִׂמְחָת֗וֹ לֹא־יִתְעָ֥רַב זָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The heart knows its own bitterness; and no stranger shares its joy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The heart knows its own toil [(in the study of Torah)], and [(therefore)] in its joy [i.e., its reward in the future] no stranger will share.


###### Proverbs 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:11)


בֵּ֣ית רְ֭שָׁעִים יִשָּׁמֵ֑ד וְאֹ֖הֶל יְשָׁרִ֣ים יַפְרִֽיחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The house of the wicked shall be overthrown: but the tent of the upright shall flourish.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The house of the wicked will be destroyed, and the tent of the just will blossom.


###### Proverbs 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:12)


יֵ֤שׁ דֶּ֣רֶךְ יָ֭שָׁר לִפְנֵי־אִ֑ישׁ וְ֝אַחֲרִיתָ֗הּ דַּרְכֵי־מָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is a way which seems right to a man, but at its end are the ways of death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is a way [(of transgression)] which is just in a man's eyes, but whose end is the ways of death.


###### Proverbs 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:13)


גַּם־בִּשְׂחֹ֥ק יִכְאַב־לֵ֑ב וְאַחֲרִיתָ֖הּ שִׂמְחָ֣ה תוּגָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Even in laughter the heart aches: and the end of that mirth is grief.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even with play [(the "play" of the L-rd with the idolators in this world)], the heart [(of the idolators)] will grieve [(in the world to come [viz. Isaiah 65:14])] so that its end will be joy [turned to] sorrow.


###### Proverbs 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:14)


מִדְּרָכָ֣יו יִ֭שְׂבַּע ס֣וּג לֵ֑ב וּ֝מֵעָלָ֗יו אִ֣ישׁ טֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The dissembler shall have enough of his own ways: but a good man shall find satisfaction in himself.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The impure of heart [(Esav)] shall be sated [i.e., punished] with his ways, and the good man [(Yaakov)] [will depart] from him [(not to share in his punishment)].


###### Proverbs 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:15)


פֶּ֭תִי יַאֲמִ֣ין לְכׇל־דָּבָ֑ר וְ֝עָר֗וּם יָבִ֥ין לַאֲשֻׁרֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The simple man believes everything: but the prudent man looks well to his step.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fool believes everything [(reported by talebearers)], but the wise man looks to his steps [(i.e., he ascertains the truth before acting on what he hears)].


###### Proverbs 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:16)


חָכָ֣ם יָ֭רֵא וְסָ֣ר מֵרָ֑ע וּ֝כְסִ֗יל מִתְעַבֵּ֥ר וּבוֹטֵֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wise man fears, and departs from evil: but the fool rages, and is confident.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise man fears [punishment] and turns away from the evil, but the fool forges on and falls.


###### Proverbs 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:17)


קְֽצַר־אַ֭פַּיִם יַעֲשֶׂ֣ה אִוֶּ֑לֶת וְאִ֥ישׁ מְ֝זִמּ֗וֹת יִשָּׂנֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that is soon angry acts foolishly: but a man of wicked devices is hated.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who is quick to vent his wrath is rendered foolish thereby, and the man who plots evil is hated.


###### Proverbs 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:18)


נָחֲל֣וּ פְתָאיִ֣ם אִוֶּ֑לֶת וַ֝עֲרוּמִ֗ים יַכְתִּ֥רוּ דָֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The simple inherit folly: but the prudent are crowned with knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fools have inherited folly, and the wise will crown [themselves with] wisdom, [and, in the end,]


###### Proverbs 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:19)


שַׁח֣וּ רָ֭עִים לִפְנֵ֣י טוֹבִ֑ים וּ֝רְשָׁעִ֗ים עַֽל־שַׁעֲרֵ֥י צַדִּֽיק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The evil bow before the good; and the wicked at the gates of the righteous.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
the evildoers will grovel before the good, and the wicked, at the gates of the righteous one.


###### Proverbs 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:20)


גַּם־לְ֭רֵעֵהוּ יִשָּׂ֣נֵא רָ֑שׁ וְאֹהֲבֵ֖י עָשִׁ֣יר רַבִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The poor man is hated even by his own neighbour: but the rich has many friends.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even by his comrade is the ignorant [(coarse)] man hated, and the lovers of the rich are many.


###### Proverbs 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:21)


בׇּז־לְרֵעֵ֥הוּ חוֹטֵ֑א וּמְחוֹנֵ֖ן (עניים) [עֲנָוִ֣ים] אַשְׁרָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that despises his neighbour sins: but he that is kind to the poor, happy is he.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who shames his friend is a sinner, and he who is gracious to the poor, fortunate is he.


###### Proverbs 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:22)


הֲֽלוֹא־יִ֭תְעוּ חֹ֣רְשֵׁי רָ֑ע וְחֶ֥סֶד וֶ֝אֱמֶ֗ת חֹ֣רְשֵׁי טֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not they who devise evil go astray? and do not they who devise good enjoy loyalty and truth?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will not the devisers of evil stray, and lovingkindness and truth [greet] the devisers of good?


###### Proverbs 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:23)


בְּכׇל־עֶ֭צֶב יִהְיֶ֣ה מוֹתָ֑ר וּדְבַר־שְׂ֝פָתַ֗יִם אַךְ־לְמַחְסֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In all labour there is profit: but the talk of the lips tends only to penury.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In all toil there is gain, but the word of the lips [i.e., vain talk] is for loss alone.


###### Proverbs 14:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:24)


עֲטֶ֣רֶת חֲכָמִ֣ים עׇשְׁרָ֑ם אִוֶּ֖לֶת כְּסִילִ֣ים אִוֶּֽלֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The crown of the wise is their riches: but the foolishness of fools is folly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The crown of the wise is their wealth [(in Torah)]; the undoing of the fools is their folly [(their laxity in learning)].


###### Proverbs 14:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:25)


מַצִּ֣יל נְ֭פָשׁוֹת עֵ֣ד אֱמֶ֑ת וְיָפִ֖חַ כְּזָבִ֣ים מִרְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A true witness saves lives: but a deceitful witness utters lies.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A true witness is the saver of souls, and the spouter of lies [saves the man of] deceit.


###### Proverbs 14:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:26)


בְּיִרְאַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה מִבְטַח־עֹ֑ז וּ֝לְבָנָ֗יו יִהְיֶ֥ה מַחְסֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the fear of the Lord a man has strong confidence: and his children shall have a place of refuge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fear of the L-rd is a bastion of strength [viz. Genesis 22:12 and 17], and he [(the G-d-fearing man)] will be shelter for his sons.


###### Proverbs 14:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:27)


יִרְאַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה מְק֣וֹר חַיִּ֑ים לָ֝ס֗וּר מִמֹּ֥קְשֵׁי מָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The fear of the Lord is a fountain of life, to depart from the snares of death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fear of the L-rd is a source of life to turn [one away] from the snares of death.


###### Proverbs 14:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:28)


בְּרׇב־עָ֥ם הַדְרַת־מֶ֑לֶךְ וּבְאֶ֥פֶס לְ֝אֹ֗ם מְחִתַּ֥ת רָזֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the multitude of people is the king’s glory: but in the lack of people is the downfall of the prince.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the multitude of the people [(when they are meritorious)] is the glory of the King, and in the want of a [(virtuous)] populace is the waning of [His] princehood [i.e., majesty].


###### Proverbs 14:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:29)


אֶ֣רֶךְ אַ֭פַּיִם רַב־תְּבוּנָ֑ה וּקְצַר־ר֝֗וּחַ מֵרִ֥ים אִוֶּֽלֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that is slow to anger is of great understanding: but he that is hasty of spirit exalts folly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who is slow to wrath [gains] much understanding, but he who is short-tempered garners folly.


###### Proverbs 14:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:30)


חַיֵּ֣י בְ֭שָׂרִים לֵ֣ב מַרְפֵּ֑א וּרְקַ֖ב עֲצָמ֣וֹת קִנְאָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A sound heart is the life of the flesh: but envy is the rottenness of the bones.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The life of [creatures of] flesh is a healing heart [(a heart of flesh, which "heals" the evil by yielding and forgiving)], but a rotting of the bones [(of mankind)] is a man of scorn.


###### Proverbs 14:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:31)


עֹ֣שֵֽׁק דָּ֭ל חֵרֵ֣ף עֹשֵׂ֑הוּ וּ֝מְכַבְּד֗וֹ חֹנֵ֥ן אֶבְיֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that oppresses the poor blasphemes his maker: but he that honours him is gracious to the poor.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who robs the poor man shames his Maker, but he who is gracious to the needy one honors Him.


###### Proverbs 14:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:32)


בְּֽ֭רָעָתוֹ יִדָּחֶ֣ה רָשָׁ֑ע וְחֹסֶ֖ה בְמוֹת֣וֹ צַדִּֽיק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wicked is thrust down in his calamity: but the righteous has hope in his death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wicked man is cast out by his evil, and the righteous one takes shelter [(in the L-rd)] in his death [(knowing that he will come to Eden)].


###### Proverbs 14:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:33)


בְּלֵ֣ב נָ֭בוֹן תָּנ֣וּחַ חׇכְמָ֑ה וּבְקֶ֥רֶב כְּ֝סִילִ֗ים תִּוָּדֵֽעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wisdom rests quietly in the heart of the judicious: but the folly in the breast of fools shall easily be known.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the understanding heart, wisdom will repose, and in the midst of the fools, it [(the modicum of wisdom that they may possess)] will clamor.


###### Proverbs 14:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:34)


צְדָקָ֥ה תְרוֹמֵֽם־גּ֑וֹי וְחֶ֖סֶד לְאֻמִּ֣ים חַטָּֽאת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Righteousness exalts a nation: but sin is a reproach to any people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Charity will uplift the nation [(Israel)], and the lovingkindness of the peoples, [(who steal from one and give to another)] is a sin.


###### Proverbs 14:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/14:35)


רְֽצוֹן־מֶ֭לֶךְ לְעֶ֣בֶד מַשְׂכִּ֑יל וְ֝עֶבְרָת֗וֹ תִּהְיֶ֥ה מֵבִֽישׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The king’s favour is turned towards a wise servant: but his anger falls on one who acts shamefully.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The favor of the king [will be] upon the wise servant, and his wrath, upon the shameful one.



## Chapter 15

###### Proverbs 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:1)


מַֽעֲנֶה־רַּ֭ךְ יָשִׁ֣יב חֵמָ֑ה וּדְבַר־עֶ֝֗צֶב יַעֲלֶה־אָֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A soft answer turns away wrath: but grievous words stir up anger.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A soft answer turns away wrath, but a galling response raises wrath.


###### Proverbs 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:2)


לְשׁ֣וֹן חֲ֭כָמִים תֵּיטִ֣יב דָּ֑עַת וּפִ֥י כְ֝סִילִ֗ים יַבִּ֥יעַ אִוֶּֽלֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The tongue of the wise uses knowledge aright: but the mouth of fools pours out foolishness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The tongue of the wise will perfect wisdom, but the mouth of fools will spout folly.


###### Proverbs 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:3)


בְּֽכׇל־מָ֭קוֹם עֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה צֹ֝פ֗וֹת רָעִ֥ים וְטוֹבִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The eyes of the Lord are in every place, watching the evil and the good.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In every place, the eyes of the L-rd see the evil and the good.


###### Proverbs 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:4)


מַרְפֵּ֣א לָ֭שׁוֹן עֵ֣ץ חַיִּ֑ים וְסֶ֥לֶף בָּ֝֗הּ שֶׁ֣בֶר בְּרֽוּחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wholesome tongue is a tree of life: but perverseness therein is a wound to the spirit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A soft tongue is a tree of life, but perverseness with it [leads to] breaking by the [(east)] wind.


###### Proverbs 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:5)


אֱוִ֗יל יִ֭נְאַץ מוּסַ֣ר אָבִ֑יו וְשֹׁמֵ֖ר תּוֹכַ֣חַת יַעְרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A fool despises his father’s correction: but he that regards reproof is prudent.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fool despises the mussar of his father, but he who guards chastisement will grow wise.


###### Proverbs 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:6)


בֵּ֣ית צַ֭דִּיק חֹ֣סֶן רָ֑ב וּבִתְבוּאַ֖ת רָשָׁ֣ע נֶעְכָּֽרֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the house of the righteous is much treasure: but in the revenues of the wicked is trouble.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The house of the righteous one [i.e., the Temple, built by David] is a tower of strength [(to Israel)], but with the entrance of wickedness [(the idolatry brought there by Menasheh)] it is polluted.


###### Proverbs 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:7)


שִׂפְתֵ֣י חֲ֭כָמִים יְזָ֣רוּ דָ֑עַת וְלֵ֖ב כְּסִילִ֣ים לֹא־כֵֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The lips of the wise disperse knowledge: but the heart of the foolish is vanity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lips of the wise crown wisdom; not so, the heart of fools.


###### Proverbs 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:8)


זֶ֣בַח רְ֭שָׁעִים תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֑ה וּתְפִלַּ֖ת יְשָׁרִ֣ים רְצוֹנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sacrifice of the wicked is an abomination to the Lord: but the prayer of the upright is his delight.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The offering of the wicked [(Balak and Bilam)] is an abomination to the L-rd, and the prayer of the just [(Moses)] is His desire.


###### Proverbs 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:9)


תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה דֶּ֣רֶךְ רָשָׁ֑ע וּמְרַדֵּ֖ף צְדָקָ֣ה יֶאֱהָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The way of the wicked is an abomination unto the Lord: but he who follows after righteousness He will love.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The path of the wicked is an abomination to the L-rd, and he who pursues righteousness is beloved by Him.


###### Proverbs 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:10)


מוּסָ֣ר רָ֭ע לְעֹזֵ֣ב אֹ֑רַח שׂוֹנֵ֖א תוֹכַ֣חַת יָמֽוּת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is a severe correction for him who forsakes the way: he who hates re- proof shall die.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Severe afflictions await the forsaker of the path [of the L-rd]; the hater of chastisement will die.


###### Proverbs 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:11)


שְׁא֣וֹל וַ֭אֲבַדּוֹן נֶ֣גֶד יְהֹוָ֑ה אַ֝֗ף כִּֽי־לִבּ֥וֹת בְּֽנֵי־אָדָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She᾽ol and destruction are before the Lord: how much more then the hearts of the children of men?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The grave and perdition [i.e., Gehinnom] are revealed to the L-rd; how much more so the hearts of men.


###### Proverbs 15:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:12)


לֹ֣א יֶאֱהַב־לֵ֭ץ הוֹכֵ֣חַֽ ל֑וֹ אֶל־חֲ֝כָמִ֗ים לֹ֣א יֵלֵֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A scorner does not like to be reproved; nor will he go to the wise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fool does not like being chastised; he will not go to the wise.


###### Proverbs 15:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:13)


לֵ֣ב שָׂ֭מֵחַ יֵיטִ֣ב פָּנִ֑ים וּבְעַצְּבַת־לֵ֝֗ב ר֣וּחַ נְכֵאָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A merry heart makes a cheerful countenance: but by sorrow of the heart the spirit is broken.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A happy heart brightens the Countenance [i.e., If one gladdens the L-rd's heart (by walking in His ways), He will look with favor upon him and do His will)], but saddening of [His] heart, [rouses] a spirit of anger.


###### Proverbs 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:14)


לֵ֣ב נָ֭בוֹן יְבַקֶּשׁ־דָּ֑עַת (ופני) [וּפִ֥י] כְ֝סִילִ֗ים יִרְעֶ֥ה אִוֶּֽלֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The heart of the judicious seeks knowledge: but the mouth of fools feeds on foolishness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
An understanding heart will pursue wisdom, but the mouth of fools will befriend folly.


###### Proverbs 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:15)


כׇּל־יְמֵ֣י עָנִ֣י רָעִ֑ים וְטֽוֹב־לֵ֝֗ב מִשְׁתֶּ֥ה תָמִֽיד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All the days of the poor are evil: but he that is of a merry heart has a continual feast.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All the days of the poor man [(even Sabbaths and festivals)] are evil [("A change of regimen brings on intestinal disorder")]; but the good-hearted [i.e., one who is happy with what he possesses] (enjoys) a perpetual feast.


###### Proverbs 15:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:16)


טוֹב־מְ֭עַט בְּיִרְאַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֑ה מֵאוֹצָ֥ר רָ֝֗ב וּמְה֥וּמָה בֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Better is little with the fear of the Lord, than great treasure and trouble with it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better a little gained through fear of the L-rd than a great treasure amassed over [the] cries [of the despoiled].


###### Proverbs 15:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:17)


ט֤וֹב אֲרֻחַ֣ת יָ֭רָק וְאַהֲבָה־שָׁ֑ם מִשּׁ֥וֹר אָ֝ב֗וּס וְשִׂנְאָה־בֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Better is a dinner of herbs where love is, than a fatted ox and hatred with it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better a meal of greens [(given to the poor)] where there is love than a fattened ox where there is hatred.


###### Proverbs 15:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:18)


אִ֣ישׁ חֵ֭מָה יְגָרֶ֣ה מָד֑וֹן וְאֶ֥רֶךְ אַ֝פַּ֗יִם יַשְׁקִ֥יט רִֽיב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wrathful man stirs up strife: but he that is slow to anger appeases strife.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The man of wrath incites strife, and the patient of spirit quiets a quarrel [(in the bud)].


###### Proverbs 15:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:19)


דֶּ֣רֶךְ עָ֭צֵל כִּמְשֻׂ֣כַת חָ֑דֶק וְאֹ֖רַח יְשָׁרִ֣ים סְלֻלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The way of a sluggard is as hedged in with thorns: but the path of the righteous is level.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The way of the sluggard is fenced with thorns [(in his eyes)], but the path of the just is paved.


###### Proverbs 15:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:20)


בֵּ֣ן חָ֭כָם יְשַׂמַּח־אָ֑ב וּכְסִ֥יל אָ֝דָ֗ם בּוֹזֶ֥ה אִמּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A Wise son makes a glad father: but a foolish man despises his mother.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise son rejoices his father, and the fool among men brings shame upon his mother.


###### Proverbs 15:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:21)


אִ֭וֶּלֶת שִׂמְחָ֣ה לַחֲסַר־לֵ֑ב וְאִ֥ישׁ תְּ֝בוּנָ֗ה יְיַשֶּׁר־לָֽכֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Folly is joy to him who is destitute of wisdom: but a man of understanding walks straight forward.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Folly is joy to him who lacks a heart, but the man of understanding [will rejoice] when he walks justly.


###### Proverbs 15:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:22)


הָפֵ֣ר מַ֭חֲשָׁבוֹת בְּאֵ֣ין ס֑וֹד וּבְרֹ֖ב יוֹעֲצִ֣ים תָּקֽוּם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Without counsel purposes are frustrated: but in the multitude of counsellors they are established.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Thoughts are voided for lack of counsel, and in an abundance of counselors they are established.


###### Proverbs 15:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:23)


שִׂמְחָ֣ה לָ֭אִישׁ בְּמַעֲנֵה־פִ֑יו וְדָבָ֖ר בְּעִתּ֣וֹ מַה־טּֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man has joy in the answer of his mouth: and a word in due season, how good it is!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man rejoices in the answer of his mouth [i.e., a soft reply causes men to love him], and a word in its time [i.e., apt responses to inquiries on festival halachoth] — how good it is!


###### Proverbs 15:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:24)


אֹ֣רַח חַ֭יִּים לְמַ֣עְלָה לְמַשְׂכִּ֑יל לְמַ֥עַן ס֝֗וּר מִשְּׁא֥וֹל מָֽטָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The way of life for the wise leads upward, that he may depart from She᾽ol beneath.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A path of life [is readied] for the wise man above, so that his soul turn away from Sheol beneath.


###### Proverbs 15:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:25)


בֵּ֣ית גֵּ֭אִים יִסַּ֥ח׀יְהֹוָ֑ה וְ֝יַצֵּ֗ב גְּב֣וּל אַלְמָנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord will pluck up the house of the proud: but he will establish the border of the widow.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd will uproot the dwelling of the proud and establish [in its place] the boundary of the widow.


###### Proverbs 15:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:26)


תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה מַחְשְׁב֣וֹת רָ֑ע וּ֝טְהֹרִ֗ים אִמְרֵי־נֹֽעַם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The thoughts of the wicked are an abomination to the Lord: but pleasant words are pure.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The abomination of the L-rd is [soft speech with] wicked thoughts; words of pleasantness [should be entirely] pure.


###### Proverbs 15:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:27)


עֹכֵ֣ר בֵּ֭יתוֹ בּוֹצֵ֣עַ בָּ֑צַע וְשׂוֹנֵ֖א מַתָּנֹ֣ת יִחְיֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that is greedy of gain troubles his own house; but he that hates gifts shall live.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who steals sullies his home, but the hater [even] of gifts [(and not only of theft)] will live.


###### Proverbs 15:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:28)


לֵ֣ב צַ֭דִּיק יֶהְגֶּ֣ה לַעֲנ֑וֹת וּפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים יַבִּ֥יעַ רָעֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The heart of the righteous ponders how to answer: but the mouth of the wicked pours out evil things.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The heart of the righteous one will consider [what] to answer, but the mouth of the wicked will spout evils.


###### Proverbs 15:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:29)


רָח֣וֹק יְ֭הֹוָה מֵרְשָׁעִ֑ים וּתְפִלַּ֖ת צַדִּיקִ֣ים יִשְׁמָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord is far from the wicked: but he hears the prayer of the righteous.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd is far from the wicked, but He will hear the prayer of the righteous.


###### Proverbs 15:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:30)


מְֽאוֹר־עֵ֭ינַיִם יְשַׂמַּֽח־לֵ֑ב שְׁמוּעָ֥ה ט֝וֹבָ֗ה תְּדַשֶּׁן־עָֽצֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The light of the eyes rejoices the heart: and a good report makes the bones fat.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The [de-] light of the eyes [(such as green gardens and flowing rivers)] rejoices the heart [(and dispels sadness)]; a good report fattens the bone.


###### Proverbs 15:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:31)


אֹ֗זֶן שֹׁ֭מַעַת תּוֹכַ֣חַת חַיִּ֑ים בְּקֶ֖רֶב חֲכָמִ֣ים תָּלִֽין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The ear that listens to the reproof of life abides among the wise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The ear that attends to the reproof [that brings] life will come to abide in the midst of the wise.


###### Proverbs 15:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:32)


פּוֹרֵ֣עַ מ֭וּסָר מוֹאֵ֣ס נַפְשׁ֑וֹ וְשׁוֹמֵ֥עַ תּ֝וֹכַ֗חַת ק֣וֹנֶה לֵּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that refuses correction despises his own soul: but he that listens to reproof gets understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who rejects mussar despises his soul, and he who listens to reproof acquires understanding.


###### Proverbs 15:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/15:33)


יִרְאַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה מוּסַ֣ר חׇכְמָ֑ה וְלִפְנֵ֖י כָב֣וֹד עֲנָוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The fear of the Lord is instruction in wisdom; and humility goes before honour.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fear of the L-rd is chastisement [(towards the acquisition)] of wisdom, and humility leads to honor.



## Chapter 16

###### Proverbs 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:1)


לְאָדָ֥ם מַעַרְכֵי־לֵ֑ב וּ֝מֵיְהֹוָ֗ה מַעֲנֵ֥ה לָשֽׁוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The thoughts of the heart are man’s, but from the Lord comes the utterance of the tongue.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man arranges [his brief in his] heart, but from the L-rd is the utterance of his tongue [(in expressing it, whether for smoothness or for stumbling)].


###### Proverbs 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:2)


כׇּֽל־דַּרְכֵי־אִ֭ישׁ זַ֣ךְ בְּעֵינָ֑יו וְתֹכֵ֖ן רוּח֣וֹת יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All the ways of a man are clean in his own eyes; but the Lord weighs the spirits.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All of a man's ways are pure in his eyes, but the L-rd counts the spirits [(of men and evaluates them)].


###### Proverbs 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:3)


גֹּ֣ל אֶל־יְהֹוָ֣ה מַעֲשֶׂ֑יךָ וְ֝יִכֹּ֗נוּ מַחְשְׁבֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Commit thy works to the Lord, and thy plans shall be established.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cast your deeds [i.e., the fulfillment of your needs] upon the L-rd, and all your thoughts will be set aright.


###### Proverbs 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:4)


כֹּ֤ל פָּעַ֣ל יְ֭הֹוָה לַֽמַּעֲנֵ֑הוּ וְגַם־רָ֝שָׁ֗ע לְי֣וֹם רָעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord has made every thing for his own purpose: yea, even the wicked for the day of evil.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd created all for His praise — even the evildoer for the day of retribution.


###### Proverbs 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:5)


תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה כׇּל־גְּבַהּ־לֵ֑ב יָ֥ד לְ֝יָ֗ד לֹ֣א יִנָּקֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Everyone that is proud in heart is an abomination to the Lord: those who join hands in an evil cause shall not go unpunished.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The abomination of the L-rd is every one who is haughty of heart; [punishment will come from G-d's] hand to [his] hand. He will not go clean.


###### Proverbs 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:6)


בְּחֶ֣סֶד וֶ֭אֱמֶת יְכֻפַּ֣ר עָוֺ֑ן וּבְיִרְאַ֥ת יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה ס֣וּר מֵרָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By loyalty and truth iniquity is purged: and by the fear of the Lord men depart from evil.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Through lovingkindness and truth sin is forgiven, and through fear of the L-rd [there is] turning from evil.


###### Proverbs 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:7)


בִּרְצ֣וֹת יְ֭הֹוָה דַּרְכֵי־אִ֑ישׁ גַּם־א֝וֹיְבָ֗יו יַשְׁלִ֥ם אִתּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When a man’s ways please the Lord, he makes even his enemies to be at peace with him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the L-rd desires a man's ways, his foes, too, He will conciliate unto him.


###### Proverbs 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:8)


טוֹב־מְ֭עַט בִּצְדָקָ֑ה מֵרֹ֥ב תְּ֝בוּא֗וֹת בְּלֹ֣א מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Better is a little with righteousness than great revenues without right.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better a little [gained] through righteousness than much produce without justice.


###### Proverbs 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:9)


לֵ֣ב אָ֭דָם יְחַשֵּׁ֣ב דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וַ֝יהֹוָ֗ה יָכִ֥ין צַעֲדֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man’s heart devises his way: but the Lord directs his steps.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man's heart will consider his way [(to walk the right path)], and the L-rd will set his steps aright.


###### Proverbs 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:10)


קֶ֤סֶם**׀** עַֽל־שִׂפְתֵי־מֶ֑לֶךְ בְּ֝מִשְׁפָּ֗ט לֹ֣א יִמְעַל־פִּֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A divine sentence is in the lips of the king: his mouth does not transgress in judgment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is a charm on the lips of a king [(a Torah sage sitting in judgment)]; his mouth will not deceive in judgment.


###### Proverbs 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:11)


פֶּ֤לֶס**׀** וּמֹאזְנֵ֣י מִ֭שְׁפָּט לַיהֹוָ֑ה מַ֝עֲשֵׂ֗הוּ כׇּל־אַבְנֵי־כִֽיס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A just weight and balance are the Lord’s: all the weights in the bag are his work.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A scale and just balances are the L-rd's [(to recompense a man according to his deeds)]. His deeds [(of requital are performed as with)] all pocket stones [(weights), commensurate with the deeds of men.]


###### Proverbs 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:12)


תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת מְ֭לָכִים עֲשׂ֣וֹת רֶ֑שַׁע כִּ֥י בִ֝צְדָקָ֗ה יִכּ֥וֹן כִּסֵּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is an abomination to kings to commit wickedness: for a throne is established by righteousness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is an abomination for kings [i.e., judges] to do evil, for it is through righteousness that a throne is established.


###### Proverbs 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:13)


רְצ֣וֹן מְ֭לָכִים שִׂפְתֵי־צֶ֑דֶק וְדֹבֵ֖ר יְשָׁרִ֣ים יֶאֱהָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Righteous lips are the delight of kings; and they love him who speaks what is right.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The desire of kings is righteous words, and just speech is beloved [of them].


###### Proverbs 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:14)


חֲמַת־מֶ֥לֶךְ מַלְאֲכֵי־מָ֑וֶת וְאִ֖ישׁ חָכָ֣ם יְכַפְּרֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wrath of a king is as messengers of death: but a wise man will pacify it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wrath of kings [is like] angels of death, and the wise man will appease it.


###### Proverbs 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:15)


בְּאוֹר־פְּנֵי־מֶ֥לֶךְ חַיִּ֑ים וּ֝רְצוֹנ֗וֹ כְּעָ֣ב מַלְקֽוֹשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the light of the king’s countenance is life; and his favour is as a cloud bringing the spring rain.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the light [i.e., the favor] of the King's countenance, there is life [(so that it behooves one to straighten his paths before Him)]; and His favor is as the cloud [bringing] the latter rains.


###### Proverbs 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:16)


קְֽנֹה־חׇכְמָ֗ה מַה־טּ֥וֹב מֵחָר֑וּץ וּקְנ֥וֹת בִּ֝ינָ֗ה נִבְחָ֥ר מִכָּֽסֶף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How much better is it to get wisdom than gold! and to get understanding is preferable to silver!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How much better is the acquisition of silver than fine gold, and the acquisition of understanding, [how much] more select than silver.


###### Proverbs 16:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:17)


מְסִלַּ֣ת יְ֭שָׁרִים ס֣וּר מֵרָ֑ע שֹׁמֵ֥ר נַ֝פְשׁ֗וֹ נֹצֵ֥ר דַּרְכּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The highway of the upright is to depart from evil: he that guards his path preserves his soul.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The path of the just is departing from evil. The keeper of his soul guards his way.


###### Proverbs 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:18)


לִפְנֵי־שֶׁ֥בֶר גָּא֑וֹן וְלִפְנֵ֥י כִ֝שָּׁל֗וֹן גֹּ֣בַהּ רֽוּחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Pride goes before destruction, and haughtiness of spirit before a fall.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Pride comes before the fall, and haughtiness of spirit before failure.


###### Proverbs 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:19)


ט֣וֹב שְׁפַל־ר֭וּחַ אֶת־[עֲנָוִ֑ים] (עניים) מֵחַלֵּ֥ק שָׁ֝לָ֗ל אֶת־גֵּאִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Better it is to be of a humble spirit with the lowly, than to divide the spoil with the proud.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better [to keep company] with the humble [in] lowliness of spirit than to share spoil with the proud.


###### Proverbs 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:20)


מַשְׂכִּ֣יל עַל־דָּ֭בָר יִמְצָא־ט֑וֹב וּבוֹטֵ֖חַ בַּיהֹוָ֣ה אַשְׁרָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who considers his words shall find good: and he who trusts in the Lord shall be happy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who deliberates over a thing [i.e., he who gives heart to his ways] will find good; and trusting to the L-rd [(where there is risk of loss in pursuing a mitzvah)] is his praise.


###### Proverbs 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:21)


לַֽחֲכַם־לֵ֭ב יִקָּרֵ֣א נָב֑וֹן וּמֶ֥תֶק שְׂ֝פָתַ֗יִם יֹסִ֥יף לֶֽקַח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wise in heart shall be called prudent: and the sweetness of the lips increases learning.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise of heart [(he who has acquired wisdom from his teacher)] will be called "understanding" [(when he generates new ideas from this wisdom)], and he who "sweetens" [his learning, making it cogent in imparting it] with his lips, will [himself] gain in knowledge.


###### Proverbs 16:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:22)


מְק֣וֹר חַ֭יִּים שֵׂ֣כֶל בְּעָלָ֑יו וּמוּסַ֖ר אֱוִלִ֣ים אִוֶּֽלֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Intelligence is a wellspring of life to him who has it: but folly is a chastisement to fools.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A well of life is wisdom to its possessor, and folly is chastisement to the fools.


###### Proverbs 16:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:23)


לֵ֣ב חָ֭כָם יַשְׂכִּ֣יל פִּ֑יהוּ וְעַל־שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו יֹסִ֥יף לֶֽקַח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The heart of the wise teaches his mouth, and adds learning to his lips.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The sage's heart makes his mouth wise, and from his lips he grows in knowledge.


###### Proverbs 16:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:24)


צוּף־דְּ֭בַשׁ אִמְרֵי־נֹ֑עַם מָת֥וֹק לַ֝נֶּ֗פֶשׁ וּמַרְפֵּ֥א לָעָֽצֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Pleasant words are like a honeycomb, sweet to the soul, and health to the bones.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Words of pleasantness [i.e., words of Torah] are a honeycomb; sweet to the soul and healing to the body.


###### Proverbs 16:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:25)


יֵ֤שׁ דֶּ֣רֶךְ יָ֭שָׁר לִפְנֵי־אִ֑ישׁ וְ֝אַחֲרִיתָ֗הּ דַּרְכֵי־מָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is a way which seems right to a man, but its end are the ways of death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is a way that is just in the eyes of man, but whose end is the ways of death.


###### Proverbs 16:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:26)


נֶ֣פֶשׁ עָ֭מֵל עָ֣מְלָה לּ֑וֹ כִּֽי־אָכַ֖ף עָלָ֣יו פִּֽיהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The hunger of the labourer labours for him; for his mouth presses upon him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The toiling spirit will have toiled for itself [i.e., for its own benefit] when its mouth makes demands upon it.


###### Proverbs 16:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:27)


אִ֣ישׁ בְּ֭לִיַּעַל כֹּרֶ֣ה רָעָ֑ה וְעַל־[שְׂ֝פָת֗וֹ] (שפתיו) כְּאֵ֣שׁ צָרָֽבֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
An ungodly man digs up evil: and in his lips there is a kind of burning fire.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wicked man plots evil, and it burns like a fire on his lips [(until he expresses it [i.e., until he brings it to fruition])].


###### Proverbs 16:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:28)


אִ֣ישׁ תַּ֭הְפֻּכוֹת יְשַׁלַּ֣ח מָד֑וֹן וְ֝נִרְגָּ֗ן מַפְרִ֥יד אַלּֽוּף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A perverse man sows strife: and a whisperer separates close friends.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The man of contraries incites strife, and the complainer [(through his complaints)] estranges the Regent.


###### Proverbs 16:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:29)


אִ֣ישׁ חָ֭מָס יְפַתֶּ֣ה רֵעֵ֑הוּ וְ֝הוֹלִיכ֗וֹ בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ לֹא־טֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A violent man entices his neighbour, and leads him into a way that is not good:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The man of violence entices his companion and leads him in a way that is not good.


###### Proverbs 16:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:30)


עֹצֶ֣ה עֵ֭ינָיו לַחְשֹׁ֣ב תַּהְפֻּכ֑וֹת קֹרֵ֥ץ שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו כִּלָּ֥ה רָעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he winks his eyes to devise crooked things: pursing his lips he brings evil to pass.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who blinks with his eyes to plot perverseness, one who "blinks" with his lips, has consummated evil.


###### Proverbs 16:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:31)


עֲטֶ֣רֶת תִּפְאֶ֣רֶת שֵׂיבָ֑ה בְּדֶ֥רֶךְ צְ֝דָקָ֗ה תִּמָּצֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The hoary head is a crown of glory; it is found in the way of righteousness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A crown of glory is the hoary head; in the way of righteousness it will be found.


###### Proverbs 16:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:32)


ט֤וֹב אֶ֣רֶךְ אַ֭פַּיִם מִגִּבּ֑וֹר וּמֹשֵׁ֥ל בְּ֝רוּח֗וֹ מִלֹּכֵ֥ד עִֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that is slow to anger is better than the mighty; and he that rules his spirit than one who captures a city.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Greater is the withholder of [his] wrath than the hero, and the ruler of his spirit [i.e., the conqueror of his evil inclination] than the conqueror of a city.


###### Proverbs 16:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/16:33)


בַּ֭חֵיק יוּטַ֣ל אֶת־הַגּוֹרָ֑ל וּ֝מֵיְהֹוָ֗ה כׇּל־מִשְׁפָּטֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The lot is cast into the lap; but the whole of its decision is from the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In [his] lap one casts the lot [(deeming himself the shaper of his fate)] — when all of his judgment [has been "cast"] by the L-rd.



## Chapter 17

###### Proverbs 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:1)


ט֤וֹב פַּ֣ת חֲ֭רֵבָה וְשַׁלְוָה־בָ֑הּ מִ֝בַּ֗יִת מָלֵ֥א זִבְחֵי־רִֽיב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Better is a dry morsel, and quietness with it, than a house full of feasting with strife.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better a dry loaf and peace with it than a house full of slaughtered [meat in the midst of] contention [i.e., It was better for the L-rd to destroy His Temple and His city and be at peace from the transgressions of Israel than to have them sacrifice offerings therein in the midst of strife].


###### Proverbs 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:2)


עֶֽבֶד־מַשְׂכִּ֗יל יִ֭מְשֹׁל בְּבֵ֣ן מֵבִ֑ישׁ וּבְת֥וֹךְ אַ֝חִ֗ים יַחֲלֹ֥ק נַחֲלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wise servant shall control a son who acts shamefully, and shall share the inheritance among the brethren.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise servant [(Nevuchadnezzar)] will rule over the shameful son [(Israel)] and in the midst of brothers will apportion the inheritance [(to himself)].


###### Proverbs 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:3)


מַצְרֵ֣ף לַ֭כֶּסֶף וְכ֣וּר לַזָּהָ֑ב וּבֹחֵ֖ן לִבּ֣וֹת יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The refining pot is for silver, and the furnace for gold: but the Lord tries the hearts.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The refining pot for [assaying and refining] silver, and the crucible for gold, but the L-rd assays hearts.


###### Proverbs 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:4)


מֵ֭רַע מַקְשִׁ֣יב עַל־שְׂפַת־אָ֑וֶן שֶׁ֥קֶר מֵ֝זִ֗ין עַל־לְשׁ֥וֹן הַוֺּֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wicked doer gives heed to false lips; and a liar gives ear to a mischievous tongue.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wicked man listens to iniquitous speech [(slander and false testimony)]; the false man listens to the destroying tongue.


###### Proverbs 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:5)


לֹעֵ֣ג לָ֭רָשׁ חֵרֵ֣ף עֹשֵׂ֑הוּ שָׂמֵ֥חַ לְ֝אֵ֗יד לֹ֣א יִנָּקֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
One who mocks the poor insults his maker: and one who is glad at calamity shall not go unpunished.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who mocks the poor man insults his Maker; he who rejoices in [another's] misfortune will not be clean [of it himself].


###### Proverbs 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:6)


עֲטֶ֣רֶת זְ֭קֵנִים בְּנֵ֣י בָנִ֑ים וְתִפְאֶ֖רֶת בָּנִ֣ים אֲבוֹתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Children’s children are the crown of old men; and the glory of children are their fathers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The crown of the old are grandchildren [(who walk in the way of good)], and the glory of children are their fathers [(who are righteous)].


###### Proverbs 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:7)


לֹא־נָאוָ֣ה לְנָבָ֣ל שְׂפַת־יֶ֑תֶר אַ֝֗ף כִּֽי־לְנָדִ֥יב שְׂפַת־שָֽׁקֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Haughty speech does not befit a churl: much less do lying lips a prince.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lofty speech does not become the churl; how much more so, lying speech the noble one.


###### Proverbs 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:8)


אֶבֶן־חֵ֣ן הַ֭שֹּׁחַד בְּעֵינֵ֣י בְעָלָ֑יו אֶֽל־כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֖ר יִפְנֶ֣ה יַשְׂכִּֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A bribe is a fair jewel to the eyes of him who dispenses it: wherever it turns it brings success.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A precious stone is appeasement [(of the L-rd)] in the eyes of his [(the appeaser's, the penitent's,)] Master. Wherever he turns [i.e., in whatever he asks of Him], He will prosper [him].


###### Proverbs 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:9)


מְֽכַסֶּה־פֶּ֭שַׁע מְבַקֵּ֣שׁ אַהֲבָ֑ה וְשֹׁנֶ֥ה בְ֝דָבָ֗ר מַפְרִ֥יד אַלּֽוּף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that covers a transgression seeks love; but he that repeats a matter separates close friends.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who conceals an offense [done against him (by not mentioning it)] seeks love [i.e., causes the offender to love him], and one who repeats a thing [i.e., one who always brings up the offense to his face] divorces the Ruler [from himself].


###### Proverbs 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:10)


תֵּ֣חַת גְּעָרָ֣ה בְמֵבִ֑ין מֵהַכּ֖וֹת כְּסִ֣יל מֵאָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A reproof enters more into a wise man than a hundred blows into a fool.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The humbling [effect] of a rebuke in an understanding person [is more evident] than [the effect of] a hundred blows to the fool.


###### Proverbs 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:11)


אַךְ־מְרִ֥י יְבַקֶּשׁ־רָ֑ע וּמַלְאָ֥ךְ אַ֝כְזָרִ֗י יְשֻׁלַּח־בּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
An evil man seeks only to be insolent: but a stern messenger shall be sent against him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The rebellious one seeks only evil, and a cruel angel will be sent against him.


###### Proverbs 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:12)


פָּג֬וֹשׁ דֹּ֣ב שַׁכּ֣וּל בְּאִ֑ישׁ וְאַל־כְּ֝סִ֗יל בְּאִוַּלְתּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let a bear robbed of her whelps meet a man, rather than a fool in his folly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better to be met by a bear bereaved [(of its cubs)] than by a fool [(an idolator)] in his folly [(enticing to idolatry)].


###### Proverbs 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:13)


מֵשִׁ֣יב רָ֭עָה תַּ֣חַת טוֹבָ֑ה לֹא־[תָמ֥וּשׁ] (תמיש) רָ֝עָ֗ה מִבֵּיתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whoever rewards evil for good, evil shall not depart from his house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who returns evil for good — evil will not depart from his house.


###### Proverbs 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:14)


פּ֣וֹטֵֽר מַ֭יִם רֵאשִׁ֣ית מָד֑וֹן וְלִפְנֵ֥י הִ֝תְגַּלַּ֗ע הָרִ֥יב נְטֽוֹשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The beginning of strife is like letting out water: therefore leave off contention, before it breaks out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As opening [the way for] water [to escape] is beginning a quarrel; before it [(your shame)] is revealed, abandon it [(the quarrel)].


###### Proverbs 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:15)


מַצְדִּ֣יק רָ֭שָׁע וּמַרְשִׁ֣יעַ צַדִּ֑יק תּוֹעֲבַ֥ת יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה גַּם־שְׁנֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who justifies the wicked, and he who condemns the just, both of them are abomination to the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Justifying an evildoer and condemning the righteous one — both of these are the abomination of the L-rd.


###### Proverbs 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:16)


לָמָּה־זֶּ֣ה מְחִ֣יר בְּיַד־כְּסִ֑יל לִקְנ֖וֹת חׇכְמָ֣ה וְלֶב־אָֽיִן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why is the purchase price of wisdom in the hand of a fool, seeing he has no sense?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Of what use is payment in the hand of a fool to acquire wisdom [(Torah)] if the heart [(to fulfill it)] is lacking [(and he learns it only to acquire a name)]?


###### Proverbs 17:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:17)


בְּכׇל־עֵ֭ת אֹהֵ֣ב הָרֵ֑עַ וְאָ֥ח לְ֝צָרָ֗ה יִוָּלֵֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A friend loves at all times, and a brother is born for adversity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
At all times love friends [(to acquire them)], and a brother in [a time of] affliction will he be born.


###### Proverbs 17:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:18)


אָדָ֣ם חֲסַר־לֵ֭ב תּוֹקֵ֣עַ כָּ֑ף עֹרֵ֥ב עֲ֝רֻבָּ֗ה לִפְנֵ֥י רֵעֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man who strikes the palm is void of understanding, in becoming surety in the presence of his friend.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The man lacking a heart clasps hands, going security for his friend.


###### Proverbs 17:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:19)


אֹ֣הֵֽב פֶּ֭שַׁע אֹהֵ֣ב מַצָּ֑ה מַגְבִּ֥יהַּ פִּ֝תְח֗וֹ מְבַקֶּשׁ־שָֽׁבֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He loves transgression that loves strife: and he that makes high his door seeks destruction.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who loves to offend [(his neighbor)] loves to quarrel; one who lifts his voice [in pride] seeks breaking.


###### Proverbs 17:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:20)


עִקֶּשׁ־לֵ֭ב לֹ֣א יִמְצָא־ט֑וֹב וְנֶהְפָּ֥ךְ בִּ֝לְשׁוֹנ֗וֹ יִפּ֥וֹל בְּרָעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that has a crooked heart finds no good: and he that has a perverse tongue falls into mischief.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The perverse of heart will not find good, and he who turns away [his true intent] with his tongue will fall into the evil [he plotted against the other].


###### Proverbs 17:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:21)


יֹלֵ֣ד כְּ֭סִיל לְת֣וּגָה ל֑וֹ וְלֹא־יִ֝שְׂמַ֗ח אֲבִ֣י נָבָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that begets a fool does it to his sorrow: and the father of a churl has no joy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who has begotten a fool has done so to his sorrow, and the father of a churl will not rejoice.


###### Proverbs 17:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:22)


לֵ֣ב שָׂ֭מֵחַ יֵיטִ֣יב גֵּהָ֑ה וְר֥וּחַ נְ֝כֵאָ֗ה תְּיַבֶּשׁ־גָּֽרֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A merry heart is a good medicine: but a broken spirit dries the bones.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A happy heart [(in contentment with one's lot)] heightens shining [(of countenance)], but a broken spirit dries the bone.


###### Proverbs 17:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:23)


שֹׁ֣חַד מֵ֭חֵק רָשָׁ֣ע יִקָּ֑ח לְ֝הַטּ֗וֹת אׇרְח֥וֹת מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wicked man takes a bribe out of the bosom to pervert the ways of justice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He [(the L-rd)] accepts appeasement from the recesses of the wicked one [(i.e., in secret)] to turn the paths of judgment [(from severity to lenity)].


###### Proverbs 17:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:24)


אֶת־פְּנֵ֣י מֵבִ֣ין חׇכְמָ֑ה וְעֵינֵ֥י כְ֝סִ֗יל בִּקְצֵה־אָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wisdom is before him that has understanding; but the eyes of a fool are in the ends of the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Wisdom lies before the understanding one [i.e., he perceives it as easily accessible to him]; but the eyes of the fool [vis-à-vis] wisdom are in the ends of the earth.


###### Proverbs 17:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:25)


כַּ֣עַס לְ֭אָבִיו בֵּ֣ן כְּסִ֑יל וּ֝מֶ֗מֶר לְיֽוֹלַדְתּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A foolish son is a grief to his father, and bitterness to her that bore him.
