**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A foolish son is ire to his father [(e.g., Yeravam to the L-rd)] and bitterness to her who bore him [(i.e., to the congregation of Israel, whom he caused to sin)].


###### Proverbs 17:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:26)


גַּ֤ם עֲנ֣וֹשׁ לַצַּדִּ֣יק לֹא־ט֑וֹב לְהַכּ֖וֹת נְדִיבִ֣ים עַל־יֹֽשֶׁר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Neither is it good to punish an innocent man, nor to give blows to noble men for their integrity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To punish the righteous, too, [(along with the wicked)] is not good [viz. I Kings 19:18], to smite the noble in [their] justness.


###### Proverbs 17:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:27)


חוֹשֵׂ֣ךְ אֲ֭מָרָיו יוֹדֵ֣עַ דָּ֑עַת (וקר) [יְקַר־]ר֝֗וּחַ אִ֣ישׁ תְּבוּנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that has knowledge spares his words: and a man of understanding is slow to anger.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The knower of wisdom spares his words; the man of understanding is chary of breath [i.e., speech].


###### Proverbs 17:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/17:28)


גַּ֤ם אֱוִ֣יל מַ֭חֲרִישׁ חָכָ֣ם יֵחָשֵׁ֑ב אֹטֵ֖ם שְׂפָתָ֣יו נָבֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Even a fool, when he holds his peace, is counted wise: and he that shuts his lips is esteemed a man of understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even one who silences a fool is accounted wise; one who stops up his [the fool's] lips [is accounted] understanding.



## Chapter 18

###### Proverbs 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:1)


לְֽ֭תַאֲוָה יְבַקֵּ֣שׁ נִפְרָ֑ד בְּכׇל־תּ֝וּשִׁיָּ֗ה יִתְגַּלָּֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who keeps himself apart seeks to satisfy his own vanity: he breaks out against all sound policy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who departs [from the L-rd] to court lust will be exposed in every Torah conclave.


###### Proverbs 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:2)


לֹא־יַחְפֹּ֣ץ כְּ֭סִיל בִּתְבוּנָ֑ה כִּ֝֗י אִֽם־בְּהִתְגַּלּ֥וֹת לִבּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A fool has no delight in understanding, but that his heart may lay itself bare.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fool does not desire understanding, but to "ventilate" his heart.


###### Proverbs 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:3)


בְּֽבוֹא־רָ֭שָׁע בָּ֣א גַם־בּ֑וּז וְֽעִם־קָל֥וֹן חֶרְפָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the wicked comes, then comes also contempt, and with ignominy reproach.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With the coming of the wicked one, there also comes contempt, and with profligacy, shaming.


###### Proverbs 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:4)


מַ֣יִם עֲ֭מֻקִּים דִּבְרֵ֣י פִי־אִ֑ישׁ נַ֥חַל נֹ֝בֵ֗עַ מְק֣וֹר חׇכְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The words of a man’s mouth are like deep waters, a flowing wadi, a fountain of wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Deep waters are the words of a hero, a flowing stream, the wellspring of wisdom.


###### Proverbs 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:5)


שְׂאֵ֣ת פְּנֵי־רָשָׁ֣ע לֹא־ט֑וֹב לְהַטּ֥וֹת צַ֝דִּ֗יק בַּמִּשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is not good to favour the person of the wicked, so as to turn aside justice from the righteous.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is not good for the wicked that favor is shown [them in this world and punishment exacted in the next.] [It is good] for the righteous that [the scales of] judgment are turned [against them in this world, so that they merit life in the world to come].


###### Proverbs 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:6)


שִׂפְתֵ֣י כְ֭סִיל יָבֹ֣אוּ בְרִ֑יב וּ֝פִ֗יו לְֽמַהֲלֻמ֥וֹת יִקְרָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A fool’s lips enter into contention, and his mouth calls for strokes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lips of the fool [i.e., all of his words] come in [terms of] quarrel, and his mouth invites blows.


###### Proverbs 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:7)


פִּֽי־כְ֭סִיל מְחִתָּה־ל֑וֹ וּ֝שְׂפָתָ֗יו מוֹקֵ֥שׁ נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A fool’s mouth is his destruction, and his lips are the snare of his soul.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The mouth of a fool brings destruction to him, and his lips are a stumbling-block to his soul.


###### Proverbs 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:8)


דִּבְרֵ֣י נִ֭רְגָּן כְּמִֽתְלַהֲמִ֑ים וְ֝הֵ֗ם יָרְד֥וּ חַדְרֵי־בָֽטֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The words of a talebearer are like dainties, and they go down into the innermost parts of the body.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The words of the complainer are like blows and they enter one's innards.


###### Proverbs 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:9)


גַּ֭ם מִתְרַפֶּ֣ה בִמְלַאכְתּ֑וֹ אָ֥ח ה֝֗וּא לְבַ֣עַל מַשְׁחִֽית׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He also that is slothful in his work is brother to him who destroys.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even he [(a Torah scholar)] who weakens in his work [(i.e., who departs from the Torah)] is a brother to the destroyer [(Satan)].


###### Proverbs 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:10)


מִגְדַּל־עֹ֭ז שֵׁ֣ם יְהֹוָ֑ה בּֽוֹ־יָר֖וּץ צַדִּ֣יק וְנִשְׂגָּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The name of the Lord is a strong tower: the righteous runs into it, and is safe.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A tower of strength is the name of the L-rd. Through it the righteous one will race [whithersoever he wishes], and he will be strengthened [therein].


###### Proverbs 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:11)


ה֣וֹן עָ֭שִׁיר קִרְיַ֣ת עֻזּ֑וֹ וּכְחוֹמָ֥ה נִ֝שְׂגָּבָ֗ה בְּמַשְׂכִּתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The rich man’s wealth is his strong city, and as a high wall in his own conceit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The treasure of the wealthy one is the city of his strength; as a fortified wall [is it] in his chamber.


###### Proverbs 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:12)


לִפְנֵי־שֶׁ֭בֶר יִגְבַּ֣הּ לֶב־אִ֑ישׁ וְלִפְנֵ֖י כָב֣וֹד עֲנָוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Before destruction the heart of man is haughty, but before honour goes humility.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Before destruction a man's heart grows haughty, and before honor there is humility.


###### Proverbs 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:13)


מֵשִׁ֣יב דָּ֭בָר בְּטֶ֣רֶם יִשְׁמָ֑ע אִוֶּ֥לֶת הִיא־ל֝֗וֹ וּכְלִמָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that answers a matter before he hears it, it is folly and shame to him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If one answers before he has heard, it will be accounted foolishness to him, and shame.


###### Proverbs 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:14)


רֽוּחַ־אִ֭ישׁ יְכַלְכֵּ֣ל מַחֲלֵ֑הוּ וְר֥וּחַ נְ֝כֵאָ֗ה מִ֣י יִשָּׂאֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The spirit of a man will sustain his infirmity; but a wounded spirit who can bear?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A hero's spirit will sustain his sickness [(and it will not prevail over him)]; but a broken spirit, who will bear it?


###### Proverbs 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:15)


לֵ֣ב נָ֭בוֹן יִקְנֶה־דָּ֑עַת וְאֹ֥זֶן חֲ֝כָמִ֗ים תְּבַקֶּשׁ־דָּֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The heart of the prudent acquires knowledge; and the ear of the wise seeks knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
An understanding heart will acquire wisdom [of itself], but the ear of the wise will seek wisdom.


###### Proverbs 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:16)


מַתָּ֣ן אָ֭דָם יַרְחִ֣יב ל֑וֹ וְלִפְנֵ֖י גְדֹלִ֣ים יַנְחֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man’s gift makes room for him, and brings him before great men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man's gift will broaden [the path] for him and will lead him before the great.


###### Proverbs 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:17)


צַדִּ֣יק הָרִאשׁ֣וֹן בְּרִיב֑וֹ (יבא) [וּבָֽא־]רֵ֝עֵ֗הוּ וַחֲקָרֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The one who pleads first seems to be in the right: then his neighbour comes forward, and sifts his case.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The first [to state his case] in an argument seems righteous; but when his fellow comes, he is interrogated [in distrust].


###### Proverbs 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:18)


מִ֭דְיָנִים יַשְׁבִּ֣ית הַגּוֹרָ֑ל וּבֵ֖ין עֲצוּמִ֣ים יַפְרִֽיד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The lot causes contentions to cease, and it decides between the mighty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A lottery can quell a quarrel and part the litigants [in their dispute].


###### Proverbs 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:19)


אָ֗ח נִפְשָׁ֥ע מִקִּרְיַת־עֹ֑ז (ומדונים) [וּ֝מִדְיָנִ֗ים] כִּבְרִ֥יחַ אַרְמֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A brother offended is harder to be won than a strong city: and their contentions are like the bars of a castle.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[There is] a brother who, by his rebellion [(against his brother)], [is deprived] of a bastion of strength [(e.g., Lot against Abraham, Esav against Jacob)], and [whose] quarrel [separates them forever] as the bolt of palace [doors forbids entry].


###### Proverbs 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:20)


מִפְּרִ֣י פִי־אִ֭ישׁ תִּשְׂבַּ֣ע בִּטְנ֑וֹ תְּבוּאַ֖ת שְׂפָתָ֣יו יִשְׂבָּֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man’s belly shall be satisfied with the fruit of his mouth; and with the increase of his lips shall he be filled.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man's stomach will be sated with the fruits of his mouth; he will be sated with the produce of his lips.


###### Proverbs 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:21)


מָ֣וֶת וְ֭חַיִּים בְּיַד־לָשׁ֑וֹן וְ֝אֹהֲבֶ֗יהָ יֹאכַ֥ל פִּרְיָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Death and life are in the power of the tongue: and they that love it shall eat its fruit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Life and death are in the power of the tongue; those who love [to use] it [in the study of Torah] will eat its fruit [in the world to come].


###### Proverbs 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:22)


מָצָ֣א אִ֭שָּׁה מָ֣צָא ט֑וֹב וַיָּ֥פֶק רָ֝צ֗וֹן מֵיְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who finds a wife finds a good thing, and obtains favour of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who has found a wife has found good and has brought forth favor from the L-rd.


###### Proverbs 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:23)


תַּחֲנוּנִ֥ים יְדַבֶּר־רָ֑שׁ וְ֝עָשִׁ֗יר יַעֲנֶ֥ה עַזּֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The poor uses entreaties; but the rich answers with impudence.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The poor man speaks imploringly [though he knows that] the rich man will answer brazenly.


###### Proverbs 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/18:24)


אִ֣ישׁ רֵ֭עִים לְהִתְרֹעֵ֑עַ וְיֵ֥שׁ אֹ֝הֵ֗ב דָּבֵ֥ק מֵאָֽח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There are friends who merely pretend friendship: and there is a true friend who is closer than a brother.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man [acquires] friends to befriend [him in time of need], for sometimes a lover [i.e., a friend] will cleave [to him] more than a brother.



## Chapter 19

###### Proverbs 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:1)


טֽוֹב־רָ֭שׁ הוֹלֵ֣ךְ בְּתֻמּ֑וֹ מֵעִקֵּ֥שׁ שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו וְה֣וּא כְסִֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Better is the poor man who walks in his integrity, than he that is perverse in his lips, and is a fool.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better a poor man who walks in his innocence than one who perverts his lips and is a fool.


###### Proverbs 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:2)


גַּ֤ם בְּלֹא־דַ֣עַת נֶ֣פֶשׁ לֹא־ט֑וֹב וְאָ֖ץ בְּרַגְלַ֣יִם חוֹטֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also, that the soul be without knowledge is not good; and he that hastens with his feet sins.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Also, without wisdom [i.e., Torah] it is not good [for] the soul; and the sinner treads [transgressions] underfoot [(saying: "This is only a slight sin and I will do it.")]


###### Proverbs 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:3)


אִוֶּ֣לֶת אָ֭דָם תְּסַלֵּ֣ף דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וְעַל־יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה יִזְעַ֥ף לִבּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The foolishness of a man perverts his way: and his heart frets against the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man's folly perverts his way and his heart rants against the L-rd! [viz., Joseph's brothers (Genesis 42:28)]


###### Proverbs 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:4)


ה֗וֹן יֹ֭סִיף רֵעִ֣ים רַבִּ֑ים וְ֝דָ֗ל מֵרֵעֵ֥הוּ יִפָּרֵֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wealth makes many friends; but as for the poor, his neighbour keeps himself apart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Wealth adds many friends, but a poor man is parted from his fellow [by his poverty].


###### Proverbs 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:5)


עֵ֣ד שְׁ֭קָרִים לֹ֣א יִנָּקֶ֑ה וְיָפִ֥יחַ כְּ֝זָבִ֗ים לֹ֣א יִמָּלֵֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A false witness shall not go unpunished, and he that utters lies shall not escape.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The false witness will not be clean [(of Heavenly judgment)], and the spouter of lies will not escape.


###### Proverbs 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:6)


רַ֭בִּים יְחַלּ֣וּ פְנֵֽי־נָדִ֑יב וְכׇל־הָ֝רֵ֗עַ לְאִ֣ישׁ מַתָּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Many will entreat the favour of the generous man: and every man is a friend to him that gives gifts.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Many court the presence of the patron, and all befriend the man of gifts.


###### Proverbs 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:7)


כׇּ֥ל־אֲחֵי־רָ֨שׁ**׀** שְֽׂנֵאֻ֗הוּ אַ֤ף כִּ֣י מְ֭רֵעֵהוּ רָחֲק֣וּ מִמֶּ֑נּוּ מְרַדֵּ֖ף אֲמָרִ֣ים (לא) [לוֹ־]הֵֽמָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All the brethren of the poor do hate him: how much more do his friends go far from him? Their word for him is, Pursuer.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All the kin of the poor man hate him — How much more so do his friends withdraw from him! He pursues words which are naught [i.e., vain, saying: "So and so is my relative; so and so is my friend."]


###### Proverbs 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:8)


קֹֽנֶה־לֵּ֭ב אֹהֵ֣ב נַפְשׁ֑וֹ שֹׁמֵ֥ר תְּ֝בוּנָ֗ה לִמְצֹא־טֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that gets wisdom loves his own soul: he that keeps understanding shall find good.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who acquires understanding loves his soul; the guarder of understanding will find good.


###### Proverbs 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:9)


עֵ֣ד שְׁ֭קָרִים לֹ֣א יִנָּקֶ֑ה וְיָפִ֖יחַ כְּזָבִ֣ים יֹאבֵֽד׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A false witness shall not go unpunished, and he that utters lies shall perish.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The false witness will not be clean [(of Heavenly judgment)], and the spouter of lies will go lost.


###### Proverbs 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:10)


לֹא־נָאוֶ֣ה לִכְסִ֣יל תַּעֲנ֑וּג אַ֝֗ף כִּֽי־לְעֶ֤בֶד**׀** מְשֹׁ֬ל בְּשָׂרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Luxury is not seemly for a fool; much less for a servant to have rule over princes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Pleasure does not befit a fool; how much more so [does it not befit] a servant to lord it over dignitaries.


###### Proverbs 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:11)


שֵׂ֣כֶל אָ֭דָם הֶאֱרִ֣יךְ אַפּ֑וֹ וְ֝תִפְאַרְתּ֗וֹ עֲבֹ֣ר עַל־פָּֽשַׁע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The discretion of a man makes him slow to anger; and it is his glory to pass over a transgression.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is wisdom in a man to withhold his wrath, and his glory is overlooking offense.


###### Proverbs 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:12)


נַ֣הַם כַּ֭כְּפִיר זַ֣עַף מֶ֑לֶךְ וּכְטַ֖ל עַל־עֵ֣שֶׂב רְצוֹנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The king’s wrath is like the roaring of a lion; and his favour is like dew upon the grass.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As the roar of a young lion is the wrath of the King, and as dew upon grass is His [good] will.


###### Proverbs 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:13)


הַוֺּ֣ת לְ֭אָבִיו בֵּ֣ן כְּסִ֑יל וְדֶ֥לֶף טֹ֝רֵ֗ד מִדְיְנֵ֥י אִשָּֽׁה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A foolish son is the calamity of his father: and the contentions of a wife are a continual drip.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A father's heartbreak is a foolish son, and [like] a harrowing drip is a woman's contentions.


###### Proverbs 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:14)


בַּ֣יִת וָ֭הוֹן נַחֲלַ֣ת אָב֑וֹת וּ֝מֵיְהֹוָ֗ה אִשָּׁ֥ה מַשְׂכָּֽלֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
House and riches are the inheritance of fathers: but a prudent wife is from the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A house and wealth is an inheritance from fathers, but a wise woman comes from the L-rd.


###### Proverbs 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:15)


עַ֭צְלָה תַּפִּ֣יל תַּרְדֵּמָ֑ה וְנֶ֖פֶשׁ רְמִיָּ֣ה תִרְעָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Slothfulness casts into a deep sleep; and the idle soul shall suffer hunger.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Laziness casts a slumber, and the deceiving soul will go hungry.


###### Proverbs 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:16)


שֹׁמֵ֣ר מִ֭צְוָה שֹׁמֵ֣ר נַפְשׁ֑וֹ בּוֹזֵ֖ה דְרָכָ֣יו (יומת) [יָמֽוּת]׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that keeps the commandment keeps his own soul; but he that despises his ways shall die.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who keeps a mitzvah keeps his soul. One who is heedless of his ways [(not taking care to weigh them)] will die.


###### Proverbs 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:17)


מַלְוֵ֣ה יְ֭הֹוָה ח֣וֹנֵֽן דָּ֑ל וּ֝גְמֻל֗וֹ יְשַׁלֶּם־לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that gives graciously to the poor makes a loan to the Lord; and that which he has given he will pay him back.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who is gracious to the poor has lent to the L-rd, and He will pay him his reward.


###### Proverbs 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:18)


יַסֵּ֣ר בִּ֭נְךָ כִּֽי־יֵ֣שׁ תִּקְוָ֑ה וְאֶל־הֲ֝מִית֗וֹ אַל־תִּשָּׂ֥א נַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Chastise thy son while there is hope, and let not thy soul spare for his crying.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Chastise your son, for there is hope, but do not turn your soul to killing him.


###### Proverbs 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:19)


(גרל) [גְּֽדׇל־]חֵ֭מָה נֹ֣שֵׂא עֹ֑נֶשׁ כִּ֥י אִם־תַּ֝צִּ֗יל וְע֣וֹד תּוֹסִֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man of great anger shall suffer the penalty; for if thou interpose, that shall only add to it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[If you] suppress [your] wrath [against your foe and] rescue [him when you see him] coming to harm, you will grow yet more [in years and in good].


###### Proverbs 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:20)


שְׁמַ֣ע עֵ֭צָה וְקַבֵּ֣ל מוּסָ֑ר לְ֝מַ֗עַן תֶּחְכַּ֥ם בְּאַחֲרִיתֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear counsel, and receive instruction, that thou mayst be wise in thy latter end.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear counsel and accept chastisement in order to grow wise in your later days.


###### Proverbs 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:21)


רַבּ֣וֹת מַחֲשָׁב֣וֹת בְּלֶב־אִ֑ישׁ וַעֲצַ֥ת יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה הִ֣יא תָקֽוּם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There are many devices in a man’s heart; but the counsel of the Lord, that shall stand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There are many thoughts in a man's heart, but the counsel of the L-rd, it will prevail.


###### Proverbs 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:22)


תַּאֲוַ֣ת אָדָ֣ם חַסְדּ֑וֹ וְטֽוֹב־רָ֝֗שׁ מֵאִ֥ישׁ כָּזָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The attraction of a man is his kindness: but a poor man is better than a liar.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man is desired because of his [expected] lovingkindness, but a poor man [who does not promise at all] is better than a [rich] man who deceives.


###### Proverbs 19:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:23)


יִרְאַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֣ה לְחַיִּ֑ים וְשָׂבֵ֥עַ יָ֝לִ֗ין בַּל־יִפָּ֥קֶד רָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The fear of the Lord tends to life: and he that has it shall abide satisfied; he shall not be visited with evil.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fear of the L-rd is [(a cause)] for life, and he [(its possessor)] will rest sated and not be visited with [any] evil.


###### Proverbs 19:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:24)


טָ֘מַ֤ן עָצֵ֣ל יָ֭דוֹ בַּצַּלָּ֑חַת גַּם־אֶל־פִּ֝֗יהוּ לֹ֣א יְשִׁיבֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A lazy man hides his hand in the dish, and will not so much as bring it back to his mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lazy man hides his hand in the [hot] kettle [(to warm it)]; not even to his mouth does he return it.


###### Proverbs 19:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:25)


לֵ֣ץ תַּ֭כֶּה וּפֶ֣תִי יַעְרִ֑ם וְהוֹכִ֥יחַ לְ֝נָב֗וֹן יָבִ֥ין דָּֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Smite a scorner, and the simple will beware: but rebuke one that has understanding, and he will gain knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You smite the scoffer and the understanding one grows wise [(Through the plagues against Pharaoh and the war with Amalek Yithro grew wise and became a proselyte)]. Chastise the understanding one and he will understand [(even more)] knowledge.


###### Proverbs 19:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:26)


מְֽשַׁדֶּד־אָ֭ב יַבְרִ֣יחַ אֵ֑ם בֵּ֝֗ן מֵבִ֥ישׁ וּמַחְפִּֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A son of scandalous and shameful ways shall ruin his father, and drive his mother away.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He despoils [his] father and drives away [his] mother — a son who shames and confounds [his ways]. [(Because Sarah saw Yishmael corrupting his ways, she drove him out together with his mother, Hagar, grieving Abraham thereby)].


###### Proverbs 19:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:27)


חֲֽדַל־בְּ֭נִי לִשְׁמֹ֣עַ מוּסָ֑ר לִ֝שְׁג֗וֹת מֵאִמְרֵי־דָֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cease, my son, to hear the instruction only to stray from the words of knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Forbear, my son, from straying from words of wisdom, in order to hear mussar.


###### Proverbs 19:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:28)


עֵ֣ד בְּ֭לִיַּעַל יָלִ֣יץ מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים יְבַלַּע־אָֽוֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
An ungodly witness scorns justice: and the mouth of the wicked devours iniquity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The perverse witness spouts justice, but blight will devour the mouth of the wicked.


###### Proverbs 19:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/19:29)


נָכ֣וֹנוּ לַלֵּצִ֣ים שְׁפָטִ֑ים וּ֝מַהֲלֻמ֗וֹת לְגֵ֣ו כְּסִילִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Judgments are prepared for scorners, and stripes for the back of fools.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Judgments are readied for the scoffers [(The L-rd has readied judgments of leprosy for the scoffing slanderers)], and blows for the backs of fools.



## Chapter 20

###### Proverbs 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:1)


לֵ֣ץ הַ֭יַּיִן הֹמֶ֣ה שֵׁכָ֑ר וְכׇל־שֹׁ֥גֶה בּ֝֗וֹ לֹ֣א יֶחְכָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wine is a mocker, strong drink is riotous, and whoever is misled thereby is not wise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[The drinker of] wine is a scoffer; [one who drinks] strong drink will cry out, and all who err therein [thinking that much wine will produce sound judgment] will not grow wise.


###### Proverbs 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:2)


נַ֣הַם כַּ֭כְּפִיר אֵ֣ימַת מֶ֑לֶךְ מִ֝תְעַבְּר֗וֹ חוֹטֵ֥א נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The terror of a king is as the roaring of a lion: he who provokes him to anger forfeits his life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As the roar of a young lion, so is the fear of the king [to his subject]; one who angers him diminishes [i.e., endangers] his soul.


###### Proverbs 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:3)


כָּב֣וֹד לָ֭אִישׁ שֶׁ֣בֶת מֵרִ֑יב וְכׇל־אֱ֝וִ֗יל יִתְגַּלָּֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is an honour for a man to cease from strife: but every fool will burst out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man's honor is to abstain from a quarrel, and [the shame of] every man [who does not abstain from it] will be revealed [through it].


###### Proverbs 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:4)


מֵ֭חֹרֶף עָצֵ֣ל לֹא־יַחֲרֹ֑שׁ (ישאל) [וְשָׁאַ֖ל] בַּקָּצִ֣יר וָאָֽיִן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sluggard will not plough by reason of the cold; therefore he shall beg in harvest, and have nothing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Because of the [cold of] winter the sluggard will not plow [i.e., he will not engage in the study of Torah]. He will desire it at harvest time, but it will not be there.


###### Proverbs 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:5)


מַ֣יִם עֲ֭מֻקִּים עֵצָ֣ה בְלֶב־אִ֑ישׁ וְאִ֖ישׁ תְּבוּנָ֣ה יִדְלֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Counsel in the heart of man is like deep water; but a man of understanding will draw it out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Like] deep waters is counsel in the heart of a man [i.e., the halachah is concealed in the heart of the sage], and the man of understanding [(a knowing disciple)] will draw them forth.


###### Proverbs 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:6)


רׇב־אָדָ֗ם יִ֭קְרָא אִ֣ישׁ חַסְדּ֑וֹ וְאִ֥ישׁ אֱ֝מוּנִ֗ים מִ֣י יִמְצָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Most men will proclaim everyone his own goodness: but a faithful man who can find?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Many men call upon the man [in whom they trust for] lovingkindness, but who will find a man of trust?


###### Proverbs 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:7)


מִתְהַלֵּ֣ךְ בְּתֻמּ֣וֹ צַדִּ֑יק אַשְׁרֵ֖י בָנָ֣יו אַחֲרָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The just man walks in his integrity: happy are his children after him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who walks in his innocence is a righteous man; fortunate are his sons after him.


###### Proverbs 20:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:8)


מֶ֗לֶךְ יוֹשֵׁ֥ב עַל־כִּסֵּא־דִ֑ין מְזָרֶ֖ה בְעֵינָ֣יו כׇּל־רָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A king that sits on the throne of judgment scatters away all evil with his eyes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the King sits on the seat of judgment, all evil is spread out before His eyes.


###### Proverbs 20:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:9)


מִֽי־יֹ֭אמַר זִכִּ֣יתִי לִבִּ֑י טָ֝הַ֗רְתִּי מֵחַטָּאתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who can say, I have made my heart clean, I am pure from my sin?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who can say: "My heart is clean"? "I have purified myself from my sin"?


###### Proverbs 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:10)


אֶ֣בֶן וָ֭אֶבֶן אֵיפָ֣ה וְאֵיפָ֑ה תּוֹעֲבַ֥ת יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה גַּם־שְׁנֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Divers weights, and divers measures, both of them are alike an abomination to the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A [large] stone and a [small] stone; a [large] measure and a [small] measure — both of them are the abomination of the L-rd.


###### Proverbs 20:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:11)


גַּ֣ם בְּ֭מַעֲלָלָיו יִתְנַכֶּר־נָ֑עַר אִם־זַ֖ךְ וְאִם־יָשָׁ֣ר פׇּעֳלֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Even a child is known by his doings, whether his work be pure, and whether it be right.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even the youth in his youthful deeds can cause wonder as to whether his acts be pure and just.


###### Proverbs 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:12)


אֹ֣זֶן שֹׁ֭מַעַת וְעַ֣יִן רֹאָ֑ה יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה עָשָׂ֥ה גַם־שְׁנֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The hearing ear, and the seeing eye: the Lord has made them both.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The hearing ear and the seeing eye — G-d made both of these [(and He desires an ear that hears mussar and an eye that sees the future)].


###### Proverbs 20:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:13)


אַל־תֶּאֱהַ֣ב שֵׁ֭נָה פֶּן־תִּוָּרֵ֑שׁ פְּקַ֖ח עֵינֶ֣יךָ שְֽׂבַֽע־לָֽחֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not love sleep, lest thou come to poverty; open thy eyes, and thou shalt be satisfied with bread.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not love sleep lest you grow poor. Open your eyes and be sated with bread.


###### Proverbs 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:14)


רַ֣ע רַ֭ע יֹאמַ֣ר הַקּוֹנֶ֑ה וְאֹזֵ֥ל ל֝֗וֹ אָ֣ז יִתְהַלָּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is bad, it is bad, says the buyer: but when he is gone his way, then he boasts.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"Bad, bad," says the [prospective] buyer, but when he walks away [with purchase in hand], he vaunts himself [for his acumen]. [(One who acquires Torah under duress bemoans his hard lot, but when he emerges full of wisdom, his past suffering is a source of gratification to him.)]


###### Proverbs 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:15)


יֵ֣שׁ זָ֭הָב וְרׇב־פְּנִינִ֑ים וּכְלִ֥י יְ֝קָ֗ר שִׂפְתֵי־דָֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is gold, and a multitude of rubies: but the lips of knowledge are a precious jewel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is [much] gold and many pearls, but lips of wisdom are a precious vessel.


###### Proverbs 20:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:16)


לְֽקַח־בִּ֭גְדוֹ כִּי־עָ֣רַב זָ֑ר וּבְעַ֖ד (נכרים) [נׇכְרִיָּ֣ה] חַבְלֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take his garment when he has become surety for a stranger: and take his pledge on behalf of an alien woman.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take [(without compunction)] the garment of him who goes security for a stranger, and take his security for an alien woman.


###### Proverbs 20:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:17)


עָרֵ֣ב לָ֭אִישׁ לֶ֣חֶם שָׁ֑קֶר וְ֝אַחַ֗ר יִמָּֽלֵא־פִ֥יהוּ חָצָֽץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Bread of deceit is sweet to a man; but afterwards his mouth shall be filled with gravel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The "bread of falsehood" [i.e., adultery] is sweet to a man; but afterwards his mouth will be filled with gravel.


###### Proverbs 20:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:18)


מַ֭חֲשָׁבוֹת בְּעֵצָ֣ה תִכּ֑וֹן וּ֝בְתַחְבֻּל֗וֹת עֲשֵׂ֣ה מִלְחָמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Every purpose is established by counsel: and with good advice conduct war.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Thoughts [conceived] in counsel will endure, and wage war [against Satan] with stratagems [(of penitence, prayer, and fasting)].


###### Proverbs 20:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:19)


גּֽוֹלֶה־סּ֭וֹד הוֹלֵ֣ךְ רָכִ֑יל וּלְפֹתֶ֥ה שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו לֹ֣א תִתְעָרָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that goes about as a talebearer reveals secrets: therefore meddle not with him who prates with his lips.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The revealer of secrets, the bearer of tales, and the "smooth talker" — do not befriend them.


###### Proverbs 20:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:20)


מְ֭קַלֵּל אָבִ֣יו וְאִמּ֑וֹ יִֽדְעַ֥ךְ נֵ֝ר֗וֹ (באישון) [בֶּאֱשׁ֥וּן] חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who curses his father or his mother, his lamp shall be put out in utter darkness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who curses his father and his mother, his light [i.e., his soul] will flicker out in the pith of darkness [i.e., when evil befalls him].


###### Proverbs 20:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:21)


נַ֭חֲלָה (מבחלת) [מְבֹהֶ֣לֶת] בָּרִאשׁוֹנָ֑ה וְ֝אַחֲרִיתָ֗הּ לֹ֣א תְבֹרָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
An estate may be gotten hastily at the beginning; but its end shall not be blessed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
An inheritance pursued in haste in the beginning [e.g., that of the sons of Gad and the sons of Reuven (viz. Numbers 32:16)] will not be blessed in the end. [(They were exiled several years before the other tribes.)]


###### Proverbs 20:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:22)


אַל־תֹּאמַ֥ר אֲשַׁלְּמָה־רָ֑ע קַוֵּ֥ה לַ֝יהֹוָ֗ה וְיֹ֣שַֽׁע לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not say, I will repay evil; but wait on the Lord, and he will save thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not say: "I will repay evil [after its kind]." Hope in the L-rd and He will help you.


###### Proverbs 20:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:23)


תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הֹוָה אֶ֣בֶן וָאָ֑בֶן וּמֹאזְנֵ֖י מִרְמָ֣ה לֹא־טֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Divers weights are an abomination to the Lord; and a false balance is not good.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The abomination of the L-rd is a [large] stone and a [small] stone, and scales of deceit are not good [in His eyes].


###### Proverbs 20:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:24)


מֵיְהֹוָ֥ה מִצְעֲדֵי־גָ֑בֶר וְ֝אָדָ֗ם מַה־יָּבִ֥ין דַּרְכּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Man’s goings are of the Lord; how can a man then understand his own way?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man's steps are from the L-rd, but what does a man know of his way?


###### Proverbs 20:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:25)


מוֹקֵ֣שׁ אָ֭דָם יָ֣לַע קֹ֑דֶשׁ וְאַחַ֖ר נְדָרִ֣ים לְבַקֵּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is a snare to a man rashly to declare, It is holy; and afterwards to probe his vows.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When a man stumbles [in sin], he undermines [his] holiness, and he must seek out vows [i.e., offerings through which to find atonement].


###### Proverbs 20:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:26)


מְזָרֶ֣ה רְ֭שָׁעִים מֶ֣לֶךְ חָכָ֑ם וַיָּ֖שֶׁב עֲלֵיהֶ֣ם אוֹפָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wise king scatters the wicked, and drives the wheel over them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise King scatters the wicked [(Pharaoh and his hosts)], and he turns the wheel [back] upon them [(recompensing them measure for measure)].


###### Proverbs 20:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:27)


נֵ֣ר יְ֭הֹוָה נִשְׁמַ֣ת אָדָ֑ם חֹ֝פֵ֗שׂ כׇּל־חַדְרֵי־בָֽטֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The spirit of man is the candle of the Lord, searching all the inward parts of the belly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lamp of the L-rd is a man's soul [(it testifies concerning him in judgment)]; it seeks out all [his] hidden recesses.


###### Proverbs 20:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:28)


חֶ֣סֶד וֶ֭אֱמֶת יִצְּרוּ־מֶ֑לֶךְ וְסָעַ֖ד בַּחֶ֣סֶד כִּסְאֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Love and truth preserve the king: and his throne is upheld by loyalty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lovingkindness and truth guard a king, and he sustains his throne in lovingkindness.


###### Proverbs 20:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:29)


תִּפְאֶ֣רֶת בַּחוּרִ֣ים כֹּחָ֑ם וַהֲדַ֖ר זְקֵנִ֣ים שֵׂיבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The glory of young men is their strength: and the beauty of old men is the grey head.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The beauty of youths is their strength, and the glory of the old is [their] hoariness.


###### Proverbs 20:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/20:30)


חַבֻּר֣וֹת פֶּ֭צַע (תמריק) [תַּמְר֣וּק] בְּרָ֑ע וּ֝מַכּ֗וֹת חַדְרֵי־בָֽטֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Bruises and wounds purge away evil: so do stripes which reach the inward parts.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Bruises [and] wounds [come upon] one who consecrates himself to evil, and "blows [in his] innards" [i.e., dropsy.]



## Chapter 21

###### Proverbs 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:1)


פַּלְגֵי־מַ֣יִם לֶב־מֶ֭לֶךְ בְּיַד־יְהֹוָ֑ה עַֽל־כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֖ר יַחְפֹּ֣ץ יַטֶּֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Like water courses is the king’s heart in the hand of the Lord: he directs it wherever he wishes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As water courses [in the hands of a man], so is the heart of a king in the hand of the L-rd. Whithersoever He wishes, He inclines it.


###### Proverbs 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:2)


כׇּֽל־דֶּרֶךְ־אִ֭ישׁ יָשָׁ֣ר בְּעֵינָ֑יו וְתֹכֵ֖ן לִבּ֣וֹת יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Every way of a man is right in his own eyes: but the Lord weighs the heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man's every way is just in his eyes, but the L-rd counts the hearts.


###### Proverbs 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:3)


עֲ֭שֹׂה צְדָקָ֣ה וּמִשְׁפָּ֑ט נִבְחָ֖ר לַיהֹוָ֣ה מִזָּֽבַח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
To do justice and judgment is more acceptable to the Lord than sacrifice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The doing of righteousness and justice is choicer to the L-rd than a sacrifice.


###### Proverbs 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:4)


רוּם־עֵ֭ינַיִם וּרְחַב־לֵ֑ב נִ֖ר רְשָׁעִ֣ים חַטָּֽאת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A haughty look, and a proud heart are the sinful growth of the wicked.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Haughtiness of eye and swelling of heart [i.e., desire to satisfy lust] is the plotting of the wicked, [and it is their] sin.


###### Proverbs 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:5)


מַחְשְׁב֣וֹת חָ֭רוּץ אַךְ־לְמוֹתָ֑ר וְכׇל־אָ֝֗ץ אַךְ־לְמַחְסֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The thoughts of the diligent tend only to plenteousness; but everyone that is hasty comes to want.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The thoughts of the upright are for gain alone, and all who are precipitate reap only loss.


###### Proverbs 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:6)


פֹּ֣עַל אֹ֭צָרוֹת בִּלְשׁ֣וֹן שָׁ֑קֶר הֶ֥בֶל נִ֝דָּ֗ף מְבַקְשֵׁי־מָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The getting of treasures by a lying tongue is a fleeting vapour: they lead to death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The amassing of treasures with a false tongue is a smitten vapor; [they (the treasures) are] snares of death.


###### Proverbs 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:7)


שֹׁד־רְשָׁעִ֥ים יְגוֹרֵ֑ם כִּ֥י מֵ֝אֲנ֗וּ לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The robbery of the wicked shall destroy them; because they refuse to do justice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The theft of the wicked affrights them, for they refuse to deal justly [(by returning what they have stolen)].


###### Proverbs 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:8)


הֲפַכְפַּ֬ךְ דֶּ֣רֶךְ אִ֣ישׁ וָזָ֑ר וְ֝זַ֗ךְ יָשָׁ֥ר פׇּעֳלֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The way of some men is crooked and strange; but as for the pure, his work is right.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Crooked is the way of a man who is strange [to mitzvoth]; but the pure one, his work is just.


###### Proverbs 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:9)


ט֗וֹב לָשֶׁ֥בֶת עַל־פִּנַּת־גָּ֑ג מֵאֵ֥שֶׁת מִ֝דְיָנִ֗ים וּבֵ֥ית חָֽבֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is better to dwell in a corner of the housetop, than with a brawling woman in a roomy house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better to dwell on a rooftop corner than [to have] a contentious wife and [one who enters his] friend's house [to converse with his wife].


###### Proverbs 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:10)


נֶ֣פֶשׁ רָ֭שָׁע אִוְּתָה־רָ֑ע לֹֽא־יֻחַ֖ן בְּעֵינָ֣יו רֵעֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The soul of the wicked desires evil: his neighbour finds no favour in his eyes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The soul of the evildoer desires evil [knowing that it is evil, and therefore,] his companion [in evil] will not find favor in his eyes.


###### Proverbs 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:11)


בַּֽעֲנׇשׁ־לֵ֭ץ יֶחְכַּם־פֶּ֑תִי וּבְהַשְׂכִּ֥יל לְ֝חָכָ֗ם יִקַּח־דָּֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the scorner is punished, the simple man is made wise: and when the wise is instructed, he receives knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the scoffer is punished, the simple one gains wisdom [and repents]; and when the wise one receives instruction, he gains wisdom.


###### Proverbs 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:12)


מַשְׂכִּ֣יל צַ֭דִּיק לְבֵ֣ית רָשָׁ֑ע מְסַלֵּ֖ף רְשָׁעִ֣ים לָרָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The righteous man who considers the house of the wicked, leads the wicked astray to greater evil.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The Righteous One eyes the house of the wicked [(e.g., Amalek) to destroy it)]; He relegates the wicked to ruin.


###### Proverbs 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:13)


אֹטֵ֣ם אׇ֭זְנוֹ מִזַּעֲקַת־דָּ֑ל גַּֽם־ה֥וּא יִ֝קְרָ֗א וְלֹ֣א יֵעָנֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whoever stops his ears at the cry of the poor, he also shall cry himself, but shall not be heard.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If one closes his ear to the pauper's cry, he, too, will call out and not be answered.


###### Proverbs 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:14)


מַתָּ֣ן בַּ֭סֵּתֶר יִכְפֶּה־אָ֑ף וְשֹׁ֥חַד בַּ֝חֵ֗ק חֵמָ֥ה עַזָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A gift in secret pacifies anger: and a reward in the bosom strong wrath.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The hidden gift [(charity)] will cover up wrath, and a bribe in the bosom [(likewise, charity)] [will cover up] strong fury.


###### Proverbs 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:15)


שִׂמְחָ֣ה לַ֭צַּדִּיק עֲשׂ֣וֹת מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּ֝מְחִתָּ֗ה לְפֹ֣עֲלֵי אָֽוֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is joy to the just to do judgment: but it is the downfall of the workers of iniquity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The doing of justice [(i.e., G-d's afflicting them)] is joy to the righteous [(in that it purges them for the world to come)], but it is breaking to the workers of iniquity [(in that they do not take heart to repent, so that the afflictions are "wasted" upon them)].


###### Proverbs 21:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:16)


אָדָ֗ם תּ֭וֹעֶה מִדֶּ֣רֶךְ הַשְׂכֵּ֑ל בִּקְהַ֖ל רְפָאִ֣ים יָנֽוּחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The man that wanders out of the way of understanding shall remain in the congregation of the shades.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The man who wanders from the path of wisdom [(Torah)] will rest in the congregation of the shades [(Gehinnom)].


###### Proverbs 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:17)


אִ֣ישׁ מַ֭חְסוֹר אֹהֵ֣ב שִׂמְחָ֑ה אֹהֵ֥ב יַֽיִן־וָ֝שֶׁ֗מֶן לֹ֣א יַעֲשִֽׁיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that loves pleasure shall be a poor man: he that loves wine and oil shall not be rich.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lover of joy will be a man of want; the lover of wine and oil will not grow rich.


###### Proverbs 21:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:18)


כֹּ֣פֶר לַצַּדִּ֣יק רָשָׁ֑ע וְתַ֖חַת יְשָׁרִ֣ים בּוֹגֵֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wicked is a ransom for the righteous, and the transgressor for the upright.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wicked one will be ransom for the righteous one [e.g., Haman for Mordecai], and the faithless one for the just.


###### Proverbs 21:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:19)


ט֗וֹב שֶׁ֥בֶת בְּאֶֽרֶץ־מִדְבָּ֑ר מֵאֵ֖שֶׁת (מדונים) [מִדְיָנִ֣ים] וָכָֽעַס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is better to live in the wilderness, than with a contentious and shrewish woman.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better to dwell in a wasteland than with a contentious woman in the midst of anger.


###### Proverbs 21:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:20)


אוֹצָ֤ר**׀** נֶחְמָ֣ד וָ֭שֶׁמֶן בִּנְוֵ֣ה חָכָ֑ם וּכְסִ֖יל אָדָ֣ם יְבַלְּעֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Costly things and oil are treasured up in the wise man’s dwelling: but a fool devours them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A goodly treasure and oil [endure] in the abode of the wise, and the folly of man swallows it up [at once].


###### Proverbs 21:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:21)


רֹ֭דֵף צְדָקָ֣ה וָחָ֑סֶד יִמְצָ֥א חַ֝יִּ֗ים צְדָקָ֥ה וְכָבֽוֹד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that follows righteousness and loyalty finds life, righteousness, and honour.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who pursues righteousness and lovingkindness will find life, righteousness, and honor.


###### Proverbs 21:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:22)


עִ֣יר גִּ֭בֹּרִים עָלָ֣ה חָכָ֑ם וַ֝יֹּ֗רֶד עֹ֣ז מִבְטֶחָֽהֿ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wise man scales the city of the mighty, and casts down the stronghold in which it trusts.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise one [(Moses)] went up to the city of the strong [(the angels)] and brought down the "strength of its trust" [i.e., the Torah].


###### Proverbs 21:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:23)


שֹׁמֵ֣ר פִּ֭יו וּלְשׁוֹנ֑וֹ שֹׁמֵ֖ר מִצָּר֣וֹת נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who guards his mouth and his tongue keeps his soul from troubles.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who guards his mouth and tongue [(from improper speech)] guards his soul from evil.


###### Proverbs 21:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:24)


זֵ֣ד יָ֭הִיר לֵ֣ץ שְׁמ֑וֹ ע֝וֹשֶׂ֗ה בְּעֶבְרַ֥ת זָדֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The proud and haughty one, scorner is his name, acts in arrogant wrath.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wanton boaster, his name is "scoffer" [i.e., he is destined to become a scoffer.] He acts in the wrath of wantonness.


###### Proverbs 21:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:25)


תַּאֲוַ֣ת עָצֵ֣ל תְּמִיתֶ֑נּוּ כִּֽי־מֵאֲנ֖וּ יָדָ֣יו לַעֲשֽׂוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The desire of the lazy man kills him; for his hands refuse to labour;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lust of the sluggard [(to vegetate)] will kill him, for his hands will refuse to do.


###### Proverbs 21:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:26)


כׇּל־הַ֭יּוֹם הִתְאַוָּ֣ה תַאֲוָ֑ה וְצַדִּ֥יק יִ֝תֵּ֗ן וְלֹ֣א יַחְשֹֽׂךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he covets greedily all the day long: but the righteous gives and spares not.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The whole day he will court lust, though the Righteous One give him [all of his necessities] and not spare.


###### Proverbs 21:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:27)


זֶ֣בַח רְ֭שָׁעִים תּוֹעֵבָ֑ה אַ֝֗ף כִּֽי־בְזִמָּ֥ה יְבִיאֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sacrifice of the wicked is an abomination: how much more, when he brings it with evil intent?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The offering of the wicked is an abomination; how much more so when they bring it with evil intent [e.g., the offerings of Balak and Bilam].


###### Proverbs 21:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:28)


עֵד־כְּזָבִ֥ים יֹאבֵ֑ד וְאִ֥ישׁ שׁ֝וֹמֵ֗עַ לָנֶ֥צַח יְדַבֵּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A false witness shall perish: but the word of a man who truly attends shall endure.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The deceitful witness will go lost, but the man who heeds ["You shall not testify falsely against your neighbor" (Exodus 20:13)] will speak forever.


###### Proverbs 21:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:29)


הֵעֵ֬ז אִ֣ישׁ רָשָׁ֣ע בְּפָנָ֑יו וְ֝יָשָׁ֗ר ה֤וּא**׀** (יכין דרכיו) [יָבִ֬ין דַּרְכּֽוֹ]׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wicked man hardens his face: but as for the upright, he considers his way.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wicked man shows his brazenness in his wrath, but the upright one sets his paths aright.


###### Proverbs 21:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:30)


אֵ֣ין חׇ֭כְמָה וְאֵ֣ין תְּבוּנָ֑ה וְאֵ֥ין עֵ֝צָ֗ה לְנֶ֣גֶד יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is no wisdom or understanding or counsel against the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is no wisdom or understanding or counsel against the L-rd [(No honor is accorded the sage at the expense of desecration of the Name.)]


###### Proverbs 21:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/21:31)


ס֗וּס מ֭וּכָן לְי֣וֹם מִלְחָמָ֑ה וְ֝לַיהֹוָ֗ה הַתְּשׁוּעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The horse is prepared for the day of battle: but safety comes from the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Though] the horse be set for the day of war, the salvation is the L-rd's.



## Chapter 22

###### Proverbs 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:1)


נִבְחָ֣ר שֵׁ֭ם מֵעֹ֣שֶׁר רָ֑ב מִכֶּ֥סֶף וּ֝מִזָּהָ֗ב חֵ֣ן טֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A good name is rather to be chosen than great riches, and amiable grace rather than silver or gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A [good] name is choicer than much wealth, and goodly favor than silver and gold.


###### Proverbs 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:2)


עָשִׁ֣יר וָרָ֣שׁ נִפְגָּ֑שׁוּ עֹשֵׂ֖ה כֻלָּ֣ם יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The rich and poor meet together: the Lord is the maker of them all.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The rich man and the poor man meet. [If the poor man asks for food and the rich man answers him harshly,] the L-rd makes all [anew. i.e., He makes the rich man poor and the poor man rich].


###### Proverbs 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:3)


עָר֤וּם**׀** רָאָ֣ה רָעָ֣ה (ויסתר) [וְנִסְתָּ֑ר] וּ֝פְתָיִ֗ים עָבְר֥וּ וְֽנֶעֱנָֽשׁוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A prudent man foresees evil, and hides himself: but the simple pass on, and are punished.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise man sees the evil [(the punishment for a transgression)] and hides [from it]; but the fools pass on [i.e., they transgress] and are punished.


###### Proverbs 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:4)


עֵ֣קֶב עֲ֭נָוָה יִרְאַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֑ה עֹ֖שֶׁר וְכָב֣וֹד וְחַיִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The reward of humility and the fear of the Lord are riches, and honour, and life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the path of humility comes fear of the L-rd, wealth, and honor, and life.


###### Proverbs 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:5)


צִנִּ֣ים פַּ֭חִים בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ עִקֵּ֑שׁ שׁוֹמֵ֥ר נַ֝פְשׁ֗וֹ יִרְחַ֥ק מֵהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thorns and snares are in the way of the crooked man: he that guards his soul shall be far from them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Thorns and snares [i.e., afflictions] are in the way of the perverse [i.e., they are readied for them]; the keeper of his soul will distance himself from them.


###### Proverbs 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:6)


חֲנֹ֣ךְ לַ֭נַּעַר עַל־פִּ֣י דַרְכּ֑וֹ גַּ֥ם כִּי־יַ֝זְקִ֗ין לֹא־יָס֥וּר מִמֶּֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Train up a child in the way he should go: and when he is old, he will not depart from it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[If you] rear the youth in his way [(either good or evil)], even when he grows old, he will not depart from it.


###### Proverbs 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:7)


עָ֭שִׁיר בְּרָשִׁ֣ים יִמְשׁ֑וֹל וְעֶ֥בֶד לֹ֝וֶ֗ה לְאִ֣ישׁ מַלְוֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The rich rules over the poor, and the borrower is servant to the lender.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The rich rule the poor [i.e., the ignorant are always dependent upon the Torah scholars], just as the debtor is a servant to the creditor.


###### Proverbs 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:8)


זוֹרֵ֣עַ עַ֭וְלָה (יקצור) [יִקְצׇר־]אָ֑וֶן וְשֵׁ֖בֶט עֶבְרָת֣וֹ יִכְלֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that sows iniquity shall reap vanity: and the rod of his arrogance shall fail.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The sower of wrong will reap wrong, and his grain-stalk [i.e., his strength] will wither.


###### Proverbs 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:9)


טֽוֹב־עַ֭יִן ה֣וּא יְבֹרָ֑ךְ כִּֽי־נָתַ֖ן מִלַּחְמ֣וֹ לַדָּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that has a generous eye shall be blessed; for he gives of his bread to the poor.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The generous one will be blessed, for he has given of his bread to the poor.


###### Proverbs 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:10)


גָּ֣רֵֽשׁ לֵ֭ץ וְיֵצֵ֣א מָד֑וֹן וְ֝יִשְׁבֹּ֗ת דִּ֣ין וְקָלֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cast out the scorner, and contention will go out; yea, strife and disgrace will cease.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Drive away the scoffer [(the evil inclination)] and strife will depart, and punishment and shame will cease.


###### Proverbs 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:11)


אֹהֵ֥ב (טהור) [טְהׇר־]לֵ֑ב חֵ֥ן שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו רֵעֵ֥הוּ מֶֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that loves pureness of heart, and grace is on his lips, the king shall be his friend.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He [the L-rd] loves the pure of heart; [one who has] favor [on] his lips, the King befriends him.


###### Proverbs 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:12)


עֵינֵ֣י יְ֭הֹוָה נָ֣צְרוּ דָ֑עַת וַ֝יְסַלֵּ֗ף דִּבְרֵ֥י בֹגֵֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The eyes of the Lord preserve knowledge, and he overthrows the words of the transgressor.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The eyes of the L-rd guard wisdom, and He twists the words of the faithless.


###### Proverbs 22:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:13)


אָמַ֣ר עָ֭צֵל אֲרִ֣י בַח֑וּץ בְּת֥וֹךְ רְ֝חֹב֗וֹת אֵרָצֵֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The lazy man says, There is a lion outside, I shall be slain in the streets.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lazy one, [(shirking Torah study)], says: "There is a lion outside; in the midst of the streets I will be slain!" [(How can I go out to learn?)]


###### Proverbs 22:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:14)


שׁוּחָ֣ה עֲ֭מֻקָּה פִּ֣י זָר֑וֹת זְע֥וּם יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה (יפול) [יִפׇּל־]שָֽׁם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The mouth of strange woman is a deep pit: he who incurs the Lord’s indignation shall fall therein.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A deep pit is the mouth of the strange woman [(the idolatress)]; the scorned of the L-rd will fall therein.


###### Proverbs 22:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:15)


אִ֭וֶּלֶת קְשׁוּרָ֣ה בְלֶב־נָ֑עַר שֵׁ֥בֶט מ֝וּסָ֗ר יַרְחִיקֶ֥נָּה מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Foolishness is bound in the heart of a child; but the rod of correction shall drive it far from him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Foolishness is rooted in the heart of a youth; the rod of chastisement will distance it from him.


###### Proverbs 22:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:16)


עֹ֣שֵֽׁק דָּ֭ל לְהַרְבּ֣וֹת ל֑וֹ נֹתֵ֥ן לְ֝עָשִׁ֗יר אַךְ־לְמַחְסֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that oppresses the poor may well bring him gain, and he that gives to the rich, may nevertheless come to want.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who robs the poor to increase [his fortune] will [ultimately] give [his wealth] to the rich [pagan kings, so that his greed will redound] to [his own] want.


###### Proverbs 22:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:17)


הַ֥ט אׇזְנְךָ֗ וּ֭שְׁמַע דִּבְרֵ֣י חֲכָמִ֑ים וְ֝לִבְּךָ֗ תָּשִׁ֥ית לְדַעְתִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Incline thy ear, and hear the words of the wise, and apply thy heart to my knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Incline your ear and hear the words of the wise [(though they be only moderately wise)], but put your heart to My mind [(i.e., If your teacher is wicked, do not learn from him.)]


###### Proverbs 22:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:18)


כִּֽי־נָ֭עִים כִּֽי־תִשְׁמְרֵ֣ם בְּבִטְנֶ֑ךָ יִכֹּ֥נוּ יַ֝חְדָּ֗ו עַל־שְׂפָתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For it is a pleasant thing if thou keep them within thee; let them be firmly attached together to thy lips.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For [it will be] pleasant [for you] if you keep them in your innards. [And when will you keep them so, (not to forget them)?] When they are established together upon your lips [i.e., when you utter them with your mouth.]


###### Proverbs 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:19)


לִֽהְי֣וֹת בַּ֭יהֹוָה מִבְטַחֶ֑ךָ הוֹדַעְתִּ֖יךָ הַיּ֣וֹם אַף־אָֽתָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
That thy trust may be in the Lord, I have made known to thee this day, even to thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have counseled you to put your trust in the L-rd [and to study Torah, without worrying how you will support yourself], today, even you!


###### Proverbs 22:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:20)


הֲלֹ֤א כָתַ֣בְתִּֽי לְ֭ךָ (שלשום) [שָׁלִישִׁ֑ים] בְּמֹ֖עֵצ֣וֹת וָדָֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Have I not written to thee excellent things in counsels and knowledge,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Have I not written for you three-fold [(Torah, Prophets, Writings)] with counsel and wisdom?


###### Proverbs 22:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:21)


לְהֽוֹדִיעֲךָ֗ קֹ֭שְׁטְ אִמְרֵ֣י אֱמֶ֑ת לְהָשִׁ֥יב אֲמָרִ֥ים אֱ֝מֶ֗ת לְשֹׁלְחֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that I might make thee know the certainty of the words of truth; that thou mightest answer the words of truth to those who send thee?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To apprise you of the truth of words of truth, to return words of truth to those who send [to] you [for instruction].


###### Proverbs 22:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:22)


אַֽל־תִּגְזׇל־דָּ֭ל כִּ֣י דַל־ה֑וּא וְאַל־תְּדַכֵּ֖א עָנִ֣י בַשָּֽׁעַר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not rob the poor, because he is poor: nor oppress the afflicted in the gate:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not rob a poor man because he is poor [i.e., because you see him to be defenseless]; and do not crush a poor man in the gate [of judgment].


###### Proverbs 22:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:23)


כִּֽי־יְ֭הֹוָה יָרִ֣יב רִיבָ֑ם וְקָבַ֖ע אֶת־קֹבְעֵיהֶ֣ם נָֽפֶשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for the Lord will plead their cause, and rob life those who rob them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the L-rd will fight their battle, and He will steal the soul of those who would steal from them.


###### Proverbs 22:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:24)


אַל־תִּ֭תְרַע אֶת־בַּ֣עַל אָ֑ף וְאֶת־אִ֥ישׁ חֵ֝מ֗וֹת לֹ֣א תָבֽוֹא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Make no friendship with an angry man; and with a furious man thou shalt not go:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not befriend the angry man, and do not come together with the man of wrath.


###### Proverbs 22:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:25)


פֶּן־תֶּאֱלַ֥ף אֹרְחֹתָ֑ו וְלָקַחְתָּ֖ מוֹקֵ֣שׁ לְנַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
lest thou learn his ways, and get a snare to thy soul.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lest you learn his ways and take a snare for your soul.


###### Proverbs 22:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:26)


אַל־תְּהִ֥י בְתֹֽקְעֵי־כָ֑ף בַּ֝עֹרְבִ֗ים מַשָּׁאֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Be not thou one of those who strike the palm, or of those who are sureties for debts.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not be among those who shake hands [(to pledge themselves)], who stand security for loans.


###### Proverbs 22:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:27)


אִם־אֵֽין־לְךָ֥ לְשַׁלֵּ֑ם לָ֥מָּה יִקַּ֥ח מִ֝שְׁכָּבְךָ֗ מִתַּחְתֶּֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou hast nothing with which to pay, why should he take away thy bed from under thee?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you have not what to pay, why should he [(the creditor)] take your bedding from beneath you?


###### Proverbs 22:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:28)


אַל־תַּ֭סֵּג גְּב֣וּל עוֹלָ֑ם אֲשֶׁ֖ר עָשׂ֣וּ אֲבוֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Remove not the ancient landmark, which thy fathers have set.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not move back the long-standing boundary-marker, which your forefathers have established. [(Do not depart from the customs of your forefathers.)]


###### Proverbs 22:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/22:29)


חָזִ֡יתָ אִ֤ישׁ**׀** מָ֘הִ֤יר בִּמְלַאכְתּ֗וֹ לִֽפְנֵי־מְלָכִ֥ים יִתְיַצָּ֑ב בַּל־יִ֝תְיַצֵּ֗ב לִפְנֵ֥י חֲשֻׁכִּֽים׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Dost thou see a man diligent in his business? he shall stand before kings; he shall not stand before obscure men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Have you seen a man quick in his work? He will stand before kings. He will not stand before low-life.



## Chapter 23

###### Proverbs 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:1)


כִּֽי־תֵ֭שֵׁב לִלְח֣וֹם אֶת־מוֹשֵׁ֑ל בִּ֥ין תָּ֝בִ֗ין אֶת־אֲשֶׁ֥ר לְפָנֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou sittest to eat with a ruler consider diligently what is before thee:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you sit down to dine with a ruler, know well what is before you [(i.e., whether he is parsimonious or generous)].


###### Proverbs 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:2)


וְשַׂמְתָּ֣ שַׂכִּ֣ין בְּלֹעֶ֑ךָ אִם־בַּ֖עַל נֶ֣פֶשׁ אָֽתָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and put a knife to thy throat, if thou be a man given to appetite.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And "put a knife to your cheek" if you hunger [i.e., If you see that he is niggardly, restrain yourself and do not eat with him].


###### Proverbs 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:3)


אַל־תִּ֭תְאָו לְמַטְעַמּוֹתָ֑יו וְ֝ה֗וּא לֶ֣חֶם כְּזָבִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Be not desirous of his dainties: for they are deceitful food.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not lust for his delicacies, for it is "deceitful" bread [i.e., not freely given.]


###### Proverbs 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:4)


אַל־תִּיגַ֥ע לְֽהַעֲשִׁ֑יר מִֽבִּינָתְךָ֥ חֲדָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not toil to be rich: because thou hast understanding, forbear.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not weary yourself to grow rich [i.e., to amass "bundles" of learning]; forbear from your own understanding.


###### Proverbs 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:5)


(התעוף) [הֲתָ֤עִיף]־עֵינֶ֥יךָ בּ֗וֹ וְֽאֵ֫ינֶ֥נּוּ כִּ֤י עָשֹׂ֣ה יַעֲשֶׂה־לּ֣וֹ כְנָפַ֑יִם כְּ֝נֶ֗שֶׁר (ועיף) [יָע֥וּף] הַשָּׁמָֽיִם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wilt thou set thy eyes upon it? it is already gone: for riches suddenly make themselves wings; they fly away like an eagle towards the sky.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Should you blink your eyes upon it [(such "undigested" learning)], it is no longer there. For it makes wings for itself like an eagle and flies heavenwards.


###### Proverbs 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:6)


אַל־תִּלְחַ֗ם אֶת־לֶ֭חֶם רַ֣ע עָ֑יִן וְאַל־תִּ֝תְאָ֗ו לְמַטְעַמֹּתָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not eat the bread of him who has an evil eye, nor desire his dainties.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not eat bread of the miserly, and do not lust for his delicacies.


###### Proverbs 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:7)


כִּ֤י**׀** כְּמ֥וֹ שָׁעַ֥ר בְּנַפְשׁ֗וֹ כֶּ֫ן־ה֥וּא אֱכ֣וֹל וּ֭שְׁתֵה יֹ֣אמַר לָ֑ךְ וְ֝לִבּ֗וֹ בַּל־עִמָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For as one that calculates in his heart, so is he: Eat and drink, says he to thee; but his heart is not with thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For like gall poured into his soul, so is this [(your eating of his bread)]. "Eat and drink," he will tell you, and his heart will not be with you.


###### Proverbs 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:8)


פִּֽתְּךָ־אָכַ֥לְתָּ תְקִיאֶ֑נָּה וְ֝שִׁחַ֗תָּ דְּבָרֶ֥יךָ הַנְּעִימִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The morsel which thou hast eaten shalt thou vomit up, and lose thy sweet words.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The loaf that you have eaten you will vomit up [(out of shame)], and you will have wasted your words of ingratiation.


###### Proverbs 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:9)


בְּאׇזְנֵ֣י כְ֭סִיל אַל־תְּדַבֵּ֑ר כִּי־יָ֝ב֗וּז לְשֵׂ֣כֶל מִלֶּֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not speak in the ears of a fool: for he will despise the wisdom of thy words.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak not in the ears of a fool, for he will shame the wisdom of your words.


###### Proverbs 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:10)


אַל־תַּ֭סֵּג גְּב֣וּל עוֹלָ֑ם וּבִשְׂדֵ֥י יְ֝תוֹמִ֗ים אַל־תָּבֹֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not remove the old landmark; and do not enter the fields of the fatherless:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not move back the long-standing boundary marker, and do not enter the orphans' fields [i.e., do not take their allotted portion of the grain].


###### Proverbs 23:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:11)


כִּֽי־גֹאֲלָ֥ם חָזָ֑ק הֽוּא־יָרִ֖יב אֶת־רִיבָ֣ם אִתָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for their redeemer is mighty; he shall plead their cause with thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For their Redeemer is strong, and He will quarrel their quarrel with you.


###### Proverbs 23:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:12)


הָבִ֣יאָה לַמּוּסָ֣ר לִבֶּ֑ךָ וְ֝אׇזְנֶ֗ךָ לְאִמְרֵי־דָֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Apply thy heart to instruction, and thy ears to the words of knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Render up your heart for chastisement, and your ears for words of wisdom.


###### Proverbs 23:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:13)


אַל־תִּמְנַ֣ע מִנַּ֣עַר מוּסָ֑ר כִּֽי־תַכֶּ֥נּוּ בַ֝שֵּׁ֗בֶט לֹ֣א יָמֽוּת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not withhold correction from the child: for if thou beatest him with the rod, he will not die.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not withhold chastisement from the youth. If you strike him with the rod, he will not die.


###### Proverbs 23:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:14)


אַ֭תָּה בַּשֵּׁ֣בֶט תַּכֶּ֑נּוּ וְ֝נַפְשׁ֗וֹ מִשְּׁא֥וֹל תַּצִּֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt beat him with the rod, and shalt deliver his soul from She᾽ol.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You will strike him with the rod, and you will rescue his soul from Sheol.


###### Proverbs 23:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:15)


בְּ֭נִי אִם־חָכַ֣ם לִבֶּ֑ךָ יִשְׂמַ֖ח לִבִּ֣י גַם־אָֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, if thy heart is wise, my heart too shall rejoice:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My son, when your heart becomes wise, then my heart, too, will rejoice.


###### Proverbs 23:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:16)


וְתַעְלֹ֥זְנָה כִלְיוֹתָ֑י בְּדַבֵּ֥ר שְׂ֝פָתֶ֗יךָ מֵישָׁרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and my reins shall rejoice, when thy lips speak right things.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And my reins will rejoice when your lips speak righteous words [(in consequence of your heart's growing wise)].


###### Proverbs 23:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:17)


אַל־יְקַנֵּ֣א לִ֭בְּךָ בַּחַטָּאִ֑ים כִּ֥י אִם־בְּיִרְאַת־יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה כׇּל־הַיּֽוֹם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let not thy heart envy sinners: but be in the fear of the Lord all the day long.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let your heart not envy [the prosperity of] sinners [and incline you to emulate their ways], but [preoccupy yourself] with fear of the L-rd all the day.


###### Proverbs 23:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:18)


כִּ֭י אִם־יֵ֣שׁ אַחֲרִ֑ית וְ֝תִקְוָתְךָ֗ לֹ֣א תִכָּרֵֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For surely there is a future; and thy expectation shall not be cut off.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For thereby it shall endure and your hope will not be cut off.


###### Proverbs 23:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:19)


שְׁמַע־אַתָּ֣ה בְנִ֣י וַחֲכָ֑ם וְאַשֵּׁ֖ר בַּדֶּ֣רֶךְ לִבֶּֽךָ׃
