**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear, my son, and be wise, and guide thy heart in the way.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear, my son, and you will grow wise, and [then] you may walk in the way of your heart [without injury].


###### Proverbs 23:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:20)


אַל־תְּהִ֥י בְסֹֽבְאֵי־יָ֑יִן בְּזֹלְלֵ֖י בָשָׂ֣ר לָֽמוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Be not among winebibbers; among gluttonous eaters of meat:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not be among the guzzlers of wine, among the gorgers of meat for themselves.


###### Proverbs 23:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:21)


כִּֽי־סֹבֵ֣א וְ֭זוֹלֵל יִוָּרֵ֑שׁ וּ֝קְרָעִ֗ים תַּלְבִּ֥ישׁ נוּמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for the drunkard and the glutton shall come to poverty: and drowsiness shall clothe a man with rags.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the guzzler and the gorger will be poor, and slumber will clothe [you] in tatters.


###### Proverbs 23:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:22)


שְׁמַ֣ע לְ֭אָבִיךָ זֶ֣ה יְלָדֶ֑ךָ וְאַל־תָּ֝ב֗וּז כִּֽי־זָקְנָ֥ה אִמֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hearken to thy father who begot thee, and despise not thy mother when she is old.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Give ear to your father, who begot you, and do not scorn your mother's [words], though she be old.


###### Proverbs 23:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:23)


אֱמֶ֣ת קְ֭נֵה וְאַל־תִּמְכֹּ֑ר חׇכְמָ֖ה וּמוּסָ֣ר וּבִינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Buy the truth, and sell it not; also wisdom, and instruction, and understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Purchase truth [if you must pay for it], but [even if you have paid for it] do not sell it, [(but teach it gratis)] — wisdom, reproof, and understanding.


###### Proverbs 23:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:24)


(גול) [גִּ֣יל] יָ֭גִיל אֲבִ֣י צַדִּ֑יק (יולד) [וְיוֹלֵ֥ד] חָ֝כָ֗ם (וישמח) [יִשְׂמַח־]בּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The father of the righteous shall greatly rejoice: and he who begets a wise child shall have joy of him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The father of the righteous one will rejoice. He who engenders wisdom [in the heart of another] will rejoice in him.


###### Proverbs 23:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:25)


יִֽשְׂמַח־אָבִ֥יךָ וְאִמֶּ֑ךָ וְ֝תָגֵ֗ל יוֹלַדְתֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let thy father and thy mother be glad, and let her who bore thee rejoice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your father and mother will rejoice, and he who engenders [wisdom in your heart] will rejoice.


###### Proverbs 23:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:26)


תְּנָה־בְנִ֣י לִבְּךָ֣ לִ֑י וְ֝עֵינֶ֗יךָ דְּרָכַ֥י (תרצנה) [תִּצֹּֽרְנָה]׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, give me thy heart, and let thy eyes observe my ways.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Give, my son, your heart to me, and your eyes will heed my paths.


###### Proverbs 23:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:27)


כִּֽי־שׁוּחָ֣ה עֲמֻקָּ֣ה זוֹנָ֑ה וּבְאֵ֥ר צָ֝רָ֗ה נׇכְרִיָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For a harlot is a deep ditch; and an alien woman is a narrow pit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the harlot is a deep pit, and the strange woman is a strait well.


###### Proverbs 23:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:28)


אַף־הִ֭יא כְּחֶ֣תֶף תֶּאֱרֹ֑ב וּ֝בוֹגְדִ֗ים בְּאָדָ֥ם תּוֹסִֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She also lies in wait like a robber, and increases the transgressors among men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She, too, lurks [to snatch] suddenly and multiplies traitors among men [i.e., heretics among Israel].


###### Proverbs 23:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:29)


לְמִ֨י א֥וֹי לְמִ֪י אֲב֡וֹי לְמִ֤י (מדונים) [מִדְיָנִ֨ים**׀**] לְמִ֥י שִׂ֗יחַ לְ֭מִי פְּצָעִ֣ים חִנָּ֑ם לְ֝מִ֗י חַכְלִל֥וּת עֵינָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who cries, Woe? who cries, Alas? who has quarrels? who has complaints? who has causeless injuries? who has redness of eyes?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who cries "Oh!" Who cries "Woe!" One who is embattled [with men], one who talks [too much]. Who is wounded for naught? Whose eyes are bloodshot?


###### Proverbs 23:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:30)


לַֽמְאַחֲרִ֥ים עַל־הַיָּ֑יִן לַ֝בָּאִ֗ים לַחְקֹ֥ר מִמְסָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They who tarry long at the wine; they who go to seek mixed wine.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Those who sit late over wine, those who make inquiries over [choice] blends.


###### Proverbs 23:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:31)


אַל־תֵּ֥רֶא יַּיִן֮ כִּ֤י יִתְאַ֫דָּ֥ם כִּֽי־יִתֵּ֣ן (בכיס) [בַּכּ֣וֹס] עֵינ֑וֹ יִ֝תְהַלֵּ֗ךְ בְּמֵֽישָׁרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Look not upon the wine when it is red, when it sparkles in the cup, when it goes down smoothly:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not look at the redness of wine; for to him who fixes his eye on the goblet, all paths are just [i.e., all transgressions seem "straight" to him].


###### Proverbs 23:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:32)


אַ֭חֲרִיתוֹ כְּנָחָ֣שׁ יִשָּׁ֑ךְ וּֽכְצִפְעֹנִ֥י יַפְרִֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
at the last it bites like a snake, and stings like a viper.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His end is as that [of him] bitten by a snake, stung by a serpent.


###### Proverbs 23:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:33)


עֵ֭ינֶיךָ יִרְא֣וּ זָר֑וֹת וְ֝לִבְּךָ֗ יְדַבֵּ֥ר תַּהְפֻּכֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thy eyes shall behold strange things, and thy heart shall utter perverse words.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(If you become drunk)] your eyes will ogle strange women [i.e., harlots], and your heart will speak contraries.


###### Proverbs 23:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:34)


וְ֭הָיִיתָ כְּשֹׁכֵ֣ב בְּלֶב־יָ֑ם וּ֝כְשֹׁכֵ֗ב בְּרֹ֣אשׁ חִבֵּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yea, thou shalt be like one who lies down in the midst of the sea, or like one who lies upon the top of a mast.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will be [unhinged] like one lying in the heart of the sea, like one lying on the top of the mast.


###### Proverbs 23:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/23:35)


הִכּ֥וּנִי בַל־חָלִיתִי֮ הֲלָמ֗וּנִי בַּל־יָ֫דָ֥עְתִּי מָתַ֥י אָקִ֑יץ א֝וֹסִ֗יף אֲבַקְשֶׁ֥נּוּ עֽוֹד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They have struck me, but I was not hurt; they have beaten me, and I felt it not: when shall I awake? I will seek it yet again.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(When you start coming out of your stupor you will say:) "They smote me, but I did not sicken; they beat me, but I did not know it. When I wake up, I will ask for more and more."



## Chapter 24

###### Proverbs 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:1)


אַל־תְּ֭קַנֵּא בְּאַנְשֵׁ֣י רָעָ֑ה וְאַל־תִּ֝תְאָ֗ו לִֽהְי֥וֹת אִתָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Be not envious of evil men, nor desire to be with them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not envy men of evil and do not desire to be with them.


###### Proverbs 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:2)


כִּי־שֹׁ֭ד יֶהְגֶּ֣ה לִבָּ֑ם וְ֝עָמָ֗ל שִׂפְתֵיהֶ֥ם תְּדַבֵּֽרְנָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For their heart studies destruction, and their lips talk of mischief.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For their hearts contemplate spoil and their lips speak deceit.


###### Proverbs 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:3)


בְּ֭חׇכְמָה יִבָּ֣נֶה בָּ֑יִת וּ֝בִתְבוּנָ֗ה יִתְכּוֹנָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Through wisdom a house is built; and by understanding it is established:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let a house be built for wisdom's sake and let it be established for the sake of understanding.


###### Proverbs 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:4)


וּ֭בְדַעַת חֲדָרִ֣ים יִמָּלְא֑וּ כׇּל־ה֖וֹן יָקָ֣ר וְנָעִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and by knowledge are the chambers filled with all precious and pleasant riches.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And for the sake of knowledge let its chambers be filled with all dear and stately treasures.


###### Proverbs 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:5)


גֶּבֶר־חָכָ֥ם בַּע֑וֹז וְאִֽישׁ־דַּ֝֗עַת מְאַמֶּץ־כֹּֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wise man is strong; and a man of knowledge increases strength.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise man is [always] steadfast and the man of knowledge increases strength.


###### Proverbs 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:6)


כִּ֣י בְ֭תַחְבֻּלוֹת תַּעֲשֶׂה־לְּךָ֣ מִלְחָמָ֑ה וּ֝תְשׁוּעָ֗ה בְּרֹ֣ב יוֹעֵֽץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For by wise counsel thou shalt make thy war: and in multitude of counsellors there is victory.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For through [wisdom's] stratagems, you can wage war [against the evil inclination], and there is salvation in the increase of counsel.


###### Proverbs 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:7)


רָאמ֣וֹת לֶאֱוִ֣יל חׇכְמ֑וֹת בַּ֝שַּׁ֗עַר לֹ֣א יִפְתַּח־פִּֽיהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wisdom it too high for a fool: he does not open his mouth in the gate.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Like a precious [(unattainable)] stone is wisdom to the fool, [and, therefore,] he will not open his mouth in the gate [of Torah. But the wise man learns a little each day].


###### Proverbs 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:8)


מְחַשֵּׁ֥ב לְהָרֵ֑עַ ל֝֗וֹ בַּעַל־מְזִמּ֥וֹת יִקְרָֽאוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who devises to do evil shall be called a mischievous person.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who thinks to do evil will be called a plotter of wrong.


###### Proverbs 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:9)


זִמַּ֣ת אִוֶּ֣לֶת חַטָּ֑את וְתוֹעֲבַ֖ת לְאָדָ֣ם לֵֽץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The devising of folly is sin: and the scorner is an abomination to men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Plotting wrong is sin and levity abominates a man [(to the L-rd and to his fellow men)].


###### Proverbs 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:10)


הִ֭תְרַפִּיתָ בְּי֥וֹם צָרָ֗ה צַ֣ר כֹּחֶֽכָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou faint in the day of adversity, thy strength is small:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you have weakened [in helping your friend] in a day of affliction, your power [(to help yourself at such a time)] will be straitened.


###### Proverbs 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:11)


הַ֭צֵּל לְקֻחִ֣ים לַמָּ֑וֶת וּמָטִ֥ים לַ֝הֶ֗רֶג אִם־תַּחְשֽׂוֹךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if thou forbear to rescue those who are drawn to death, and those who are ready to be slain;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[If you have weakened] from helping those who have been consigned to death, and if you desist from [helping] those who are inclined towards destruction.


###### Proverbs 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:12)


כִּֽי־תֹאמַ֗ר הֵן֮ לֹא־יָדַ֢עְנ֫וּ זֶ֥ה הֲֽלֹא־תֹ֘כֵ֤ן לִבּ֨וֹת**׀** הֽוּא־יָבִ֗ין וְנֹצֵ֣ר נַ֭פְשְׁךָ ה֣וּא יֵדָ֑ע וְהֵשִׁ֖יב לְאָדָ֣ם כְּפׇעֳלֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if thou sayst, Behold, we knew it not, does not he who ponders the heart consider it? and he who keeps thy soul, does he not know it? and shall he not render to every man according to his works?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lest you say: "We did not know this" — does He who resides in the midst of hearts not understand? and does the Protector of your soul not know? And He recompenses a man according to his deeds.


###### Proverbs 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:13)


אֱכׇל־בְּנִ֣י דְבַ֣שׁ כִּי־ט֑וֹב וְנֹ֥פֶת מָ֝ת֗וֹק עַל־חִכֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, eat honey, because it is good; and the honeycomb, which is sweet to thy taste:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Just as honey [-food] is good, my son, and just as [you put] drippings of sweet things to your palate,


###### Proverbs 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:14)


כֵּ֤ן**׀** דְּעֶ֥ה חׇכְמָ֗ה לְנַ֫פְשֶׁ֥ךָ אִם־מָ֭צָאתָ וְיֵ֣שׁ אַחֲרִ֑ית וְ֝תִקְוָתְךָ֗ לֹ֣א תִכָּרֵֽת׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
know that such is wisdom for thy soul! when hast found it, then there shall be a reward, and thy expectation shall not be cut off.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So, know wisdom for your soul. If you have found it, it has a [goodly] consummation, and your hope [of reward] will not be cut off.


###### Proverbs 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:15)


אַל־תֶּאֱרֹ֣ב רָ֭שָׁע לִנְוֵ֣ה צַדִּ֑יק אַֽל־תְּשַׁדֵּ֥ד רִבְצֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not lie in wait, O wicked man, against the dwelling of the righteous; do not plunder his resting place:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not lurk, you evildoer, near the habitation of the righteous one [(hoping to secure it when he falls upon hard times)]. Do not steal the place where he reclines.


###### Proverbs 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:16)


כִּ֤י שֶׁ֨בַע**׀** יִפּ֣וֹל צַדִּ֣יק וָקָ֑ם וּ֝רְשָׁעִ֗ים יִכָּשְׁל֥וּ בְרָעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for a just man falls seven times, and yet rises up again: but the wicked stumble into mischief.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For [though] the righteous one fall seven times, he will rise [again], and the wicked will stumble [(not to rise again)] when evil [comes upon them].


###### Proverbs 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:17)


בִּנְפֹ֣ל (אויביך) [א֭וֹיִבְךָ] אַל־תִּשְׂמָ֑ח וּ֝בִכָּשְׁל֗וֹ אַל־יָגֵ֥ל לִבֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not rejoice when thy enemy falls, and do not let thy heart be glad when he stumbles:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When your foe falls, do not rejoice, and when he stumbles, let your heart not be merry.


###### Proverbs 24:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:18)


פֶּן־יִרְאֶ֣ה יְ֭הֹוָה וְרַ֣ע בְּעֵינָ֑יו וְהֵשִׁ֖יב מֵעָלָ֣יו אַפּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
lest the Lord see it, and it displease him, and he turn away his wrath from him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lest the L-rd see, and it be evil in His eyes, and He turn His wrath from him [onto you].


###### Proverbs 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:19)


אַל־תִּתְחַ֥ר בַּמְּרֵעִ֑ים אַל־תְּ֝קַנֵּ֗א בָּֽרְשָׁעִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Fret not thyself because of evil men, nor be envious at the wicked;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not emulate evildoers; do not envy the wicked.


###### Proverbs 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:20)


כִּ֤י**׀** לֹא־תִהְיֶ֣ה אַחֲרִ֣ית לָרָ֑ע נֵ֖ר רְשָׁעִ֣ים יִדְעָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for there shall be no reward to the evil man; the candle of the wicked shall be put out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For there will be no endurance to evil; the light of the wicked will be extinguished.


###### Proverbs 24:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:21)


יְרָא־אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה בְּנִ֣י וָמֶ֑לֶךְ עִם־שׁ֝וֹנִ֗ים אַל־תִּתְעָרָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, fear thou the Lord and the king: and do not meddle with them who are given to change:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Fear the L-rd, my son, and the king [— so long as the second does not detract from the first]. Do not mingle with variegators [(those who posit a variety of deities)].


###### Proverbs 24:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:22)


כִּֽי־פִ֭תְאֹם יָק֣וּם אֵידָ֑ם וּפִ֥יד שְׁ֝נֵיהֶ֗ם מִ֣י יוֹדֵֽעַ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for their calamity shall rise suddenly; and who knows the ruin of them both?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For their destruction will suddenly arise, and who knows [the extent of] the breaking of both [the idols and the idolators]?


###### Proverbs 24:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:23)


גַּם־אֵ֥לֶּה לַחֲכָמִ֑ים הַֽכֵּר־פָּנִ֖ים בְּמִשְׁפָּ֣ט בַּל־טֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These words also belong to the wise. It is not good to have respect of persons in judgment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These things, too, [(which follow)] are intended for the wise [that sit in judgment], that they not show favoritism in judgment, [for] it is not good [to do so].


###### Proverbs 24:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:24)


אֹ֤מֵ֨ר**׀** לְרָשָׁע֮ צַדִּ֢יק אָ֥֫תָּה יִקְּבֻ֥הוּ עַמִּ֑ים יִזְעָמ֥וּהוּ לְאֻמִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who says to the wicked, Thou art in the right; him shall the people curse, nations shall abhor him:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who says to the wicked one: "You are righteous" will be cursed by the peoples, will be anathematized by the nations.


###### Proverbs 24:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:25)


וְלַמּוֹכִיחִ֥ים יִנְעָ֑ם וַ֝עֲלֵיהֶ֗ם תָּב֥וֹא בִרְכַּת־טֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but those who rebuke the wicked shall have delight, and a good blessing shall come upon them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But for the reprovers it will be accounted pleasant [(to say: "You are essentially good, but …")], and a goodly blessing will come upon them.


###### Proverbs 24:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:26)


שְׂפָתַ֥יִם יִשָּׁ֑ק מֵ֝שִׁ֗יב דְּבָרִ֥ים נְכֹחִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Like a kiss on the lips, it is when one gives a right answer.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[All] lips should kiss one who returns right answers.


###### Proverbs 24:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:27)


הָ֘כֵ֤ן בַּח֨וּץ**׀** מְלַאכְתֶּ֗ךָ וְעַתְּדָ֣הּ בַּשָּׂדֶ֣ה לָ֑ךְ אַ֝חַ֗ר וּבָנִ֥יתָ בֵיתֶֽךָ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Prepare thy work outside, and make it fit for thyself in the field; and afterwards build thy house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Prepare your work outside [(Acquire fields and vineyards)], and put livestock in your field, and then, build your house [i.e., take a wife].


###### Proverbs 24:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:28)


אַל־תְּהִ֣י עֵד־חִנָּ֣ם בְּרֵעֶ֑ךָ וַ֝הֲפִתִּ֗יתָ בִּשְׂפָתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Be not a witness against thy neighbour without cause; and deceive not with thy lips.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not offer vain testimony for your friend, that you [not] be enticed [for him] by your lips.


###### Proverbs 24:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:29)


אַל־תֹּאמַ֗ר כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר עָֽשָׂה־לִ֭י כֵּ֤ן אֶֽעֱשֶׂה־לּ֑וֹ אָשִׁ֖יב לָאִ֣ישׁ כְּפׇעֳלֽוֹ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not say, I will do so to him as he has done to me: I will render to the man according to his deeds.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not say: "As he has done for me, so will I do for him — I will reward a man according to his acts."


###### Proverbs 24:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:30)


עַל־שְׂדֵ֣ה אִישׁ־עָצֵ֣ל עָבַ֑רְתִּי וְעַל־כֶּ֝֗רֶם אָדָ֥ם חֲסַר־לֵֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I went by the field of a lazy man, and by the vineyard of a man void of understanding;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I passed by the field of a lazy man [(one who does not review his learning)], and by the vineyard of a man lacking a heart.


###### Proverbs 24:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:31)


וְהִנֵּ֨ה עָ֘לָ֤ה כֻלּ֨וֹ**׀** קִמְּשֹׂנִ֗ים כׇּסּ֣וּ פָנָ֣יו חֲרֻלִּ֑ים וְגֶ֖דֶר אֲבָנָ֣יו נֶהֱרָֽסָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and, lo, it was all grown over with thorns, and nettles had covered it over, and its stone wall was broken down.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, behold, it was all grown over with thorns; nettles had covered its face, and its stone wall was broken down. [(If one does not review his learning, first he forgets the broad outlines, then he confuses the statements of the sages, interchanging "clean" with "unclean," and vice versa, and, ultimately, "destroying the world.")]


###### Proverbs 24:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:32)


וָאֶחֱזֶ֣ה אָ֭נֹכִי אָשִׁ֣ית לִבִּ֑י רָ֝אִ֗יתִי לָקַ֥חְתִּי מוּסָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I saw, and considered it well: I looked upon it, and received instruction.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when I saw this, I set my heart [to understand it]; and when I saw, I took instruction:


###### Proverbs 24:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:33)


מְעַ֣ט שֵׁ֭נוֹת מְעַ֣ט תְּנוּמ֑וֹת מְעַ֓ט**׀** חִבֻּ֖ק יָדַ֣יִם לִשְׁכָּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A little sleep, a little slumber, a little folding of the hands to sleep


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A little sleep, a little slumber, a little folding of the hands to recline,


###### Proverbs 24:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/24:34)


וּבָֽא־מִתְהַלֵּ֥ךְ רֵישֶׁ֑ךָ וּ֝מַחְסֹרֶ֗יךָ כְּאִ֣ישׁ מָגֵֽן׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so shall thy poverty come like a marauder; and thy want like an armed man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then your impoverishments will come as a runner, and your wants, as an armed man.



## Chapter 25

###### Proverbs 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:1)


גַּם־אֵ֭לֶּה מִשְׁלֵ֣י שְׁלֹמֹ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר הֶ֝עְתִּ֗יקוּ אַנְשֵׁ֤י**׀** חִזְקִיָּ֬ה מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These also are proverbs of Shelomo, which the men of Ḥizqiyya, king of Yehuda copied out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These, too, are the analogues of King Solomon, which were set down by the men of Chizkiyah, king of Judah [when he was crowned as king].


###### Proverbs 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:2)


כְּבֹ֣ד אֱ֭לֹהִים הַסְתֵּ֣ר דָּבָ֑ר וּכְבֹ֥ד מְ֝לָכִ֗ים חֲקֹ֣ר דָּבָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is the glory of God to conceal a thing: but the honour of kings is to search out a matter.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The honor of G-d is concealing a thing [(i.e., not to make inquiries into recondite matters such as the Divine Chariot, the creation, and the statutes of the Torah)]; but the honor of kings [i.e., the sages] is searching out a thing [(trying to understand the reasons for their "fences," their decrees, etc.)].


###### Proverbs 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:3)


שָׁמַ֣יִם לָ֭רוּם וָאָ֣רֶץ לָעֹ֑מֶק וְלֵ֥ב מְ֝לָכִ֗ים אֵ֣ין חֵֽקֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The heaven for height, and the earth for depth, and the heart of kings is unsearchable.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The heavens for height, the earth for depth, and the heart of kings [(the sages)] for fathomlessness.


###### Proverbs 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:4)


הָג֣וֹ סִיגִ֣ים מִכָּ֑סֶף וַיֵּצֵ֖א לַצֹּרֵ֣ף כֶּֽלִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take away the dross from the silver, and a vessel emerges for the refiner.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remove the dross from the silver, and [only then] send the vessel to the refiner.


###### Proverbs 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:5)


הָג֣וֹ רָ֭שָׁע לִפְנֵי־מֶ֑לֶךְ וְיִכּ֖וֹן בַּצֶּ֣דֶק כִּסְאֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take away the wicked from before the king, and his throne shall be established in righteousness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remove the wicked one from before the King, and [only then] will His throne be established in righteousness. [(The community is not absolved of responsibility until the evildoers are removed from its midst and punished.)]


###### Proverbs 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:6)


אַל־תִּתְהַדַּ֥ר לִפְנֵי־מֶ֑לֶךְ וּבִמְק֥וֹם גְּ֝דֹלִ֗ים אַֽל־תַּעֲמֹֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not give thyself airs in the presence of the king, and stand not in the place of great men: for better it is that it be said to thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not plume yourself in the presence of the king, and do not stand in the place of the great [(but keep a respectful distance)].


###### Proverbs 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:7)


כִּ֤י ט֥וֹב אֲמׇר־לְךָ֗ עֲֽלֵ֫ה־הֵ֥נָּה מֵ֭הַשְׁפִּ֣ילְךָ לִפְנֵ֣י נָדִ֑יב אֲשֶׁ֖ר רָא֣וּ עֵינֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Come up here; than that thou shouldst be put lower in the presence of the prince whom thy eyes have seen.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For it is better that it be said to you: "Come up here" than that you be made to go down before the prince, as, [indeed,] your eyes have seen.


###### Proverbs 25:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:8)


אַל־תֵּצֵ֥א לָרִ֗ב מַ֫הֵ֥ר פֶּ֣ן מַה־תַּ֭עֲשֶׂה בְּאַחֲרִיתָ֑הּ בְּהַכְלִ֖ים אֹתְךָ֣ רֵעֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not proceed hastily to litigation, lest thou know not what to do in the end of it, when thy neighbour has put thee to shame.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not be quick to stir up strife, lest [you do not know] what to do in the end when your friend shames you [(by proving himself guiltless)].


###### Proverbs 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:9)


רִ֭יבְךָ רִ֣יב אֶת־רֵעֶ֑ךָ וְס֖וֹד אַחֵ֣ר אַל־תְּגָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Debate thy cause with thy neighbour: and do not reveal the secret of another:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Prosecute your quarrel with your friend [(if you must)], but do not reveal the secret of another [i.e., do not bring up to him the wrongdoing of his ancestors so that others come to know of it)].


###### Proverbs 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:10)


פֶּֽן־יְחַסֶּדְךָ֥ שֹׁמֵ֑עַ וְ֝דִבָּתְךָ֗ לֹ֣א תָשֽׁוּב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
lest he who hears thee betray thee; and lest thy calumny be carried back.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lest the hearer shame you [(by calling you a slanderer)] and you be unable to retract your slander.


###### Proverbs 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:11)


תַּפּוּחֵ֣י זָ֭הָב בְּמַשְׂכִּיּ֣וֹת כָּ֑סֶף דָּ֝בָ֗ר דָּבֻ֥ר עַל־אׇפְנָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A word fitly spoken is like apples of gold in ornaments of silver.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Like golden apples [carved] into silver [-plated] vessels are words cogently spoken.


###### Proverbs 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:12)


נֶ֣זֶם זָ֭הָב וַחֲלִי־כָ֑תֶם מוֹכִ֥יחַ חָ֝כָ֗ם עַל־אֹ֥זֶן שֹׁמָֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Like an earring of gold, and an ornament of fine gold, so is a wise reprover upon an obedient ear.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Like a golden ring among jewelry of finest gold is the reproof of a sage upon a hearing ear.


###### Proverbs 25:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:13)


כְּצִנַּת־שֶׁ֨לֶג**׀** בְּי֬וֹם קָצִ֗יר צִ֣יר נֶ֭אֱמָן לְשֹׁלְחָ֑יו וְנֶ֖פֶשׁ אֲדֹנָ֣יו יָשִֽׁיב׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Like the cold snow in the time of harvest, so is a faithful messenger to those who send him: for he refreshes the soul of his master.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Like the cold of [(a day of)] snow on a harvest day is a faithful emissary to his sender; he revives his master's soul.


###### Proverbs 25:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:14)


נְשִׂיאִ֣ים וְ֭רוּחַ וְגֶ֣שֶׁם אָ֑יִן אִ֥ישׁ מִ֝תְהַלֵּ֗ל בְּמַתַּת־שָֽׁקֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
One who boasts of gifts that he does not give is like clouds and wind without rain.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Like clouds and wind without [the expected] rain, so is one who vaunts himself over a false gift [i.e., a gift that he never gives].


###### Proverbs 25:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:15)


בְּאֹ֣רֶךְ אַ֭פַּיִם יְפֻתֶּ֣ה קָצִ֑ין וְלָשׁ֥וֹן רַ֝כָּ֗ה תִּשְׁבׇּר־גָּֽרֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By long forbearing is a ruler won over, and a soft tongue breaks the bone.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So long as He withholds His wrath the Ruler may be mollified [(by penitence and prayer)], and a soft tongue [(in prayer and supplication)] may break the bone [i.e., the severity of the decree].


###### Proverbs 25:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:16)


דְּבַ֣שׁ מָ֭צָאתָ אֱכֹ֣ל דַּיֶּ֑ךָּ פֶּן־תִּ֝שְׂבָּעֶ֗נּוּ וַהֲקֵאתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hast thou found honey? eat as much as is sufficient for thee, lest thou be sated with it, and vomit it up.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you find honey, eat [only] what you need, lest you are surfeited and vomit it up.


###### Proverbs 25:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:17)


הֹקַ֣ר רַ֭גְלְךָ מִבֵּ֣ית רֵעֶ֑ךָ פֶּן־יִ֝שְׂבָּעֲךָ֗ וּשְׂנֵאֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let thy foot be seldom in thy neighbour’s house; lest he be weary of thee, and hate thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Similarly,] let your feet not frequent the house of your friend, lest he be surfeited with you and hate you.


###### Proverbs 25:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:18)


מֵפִ֣יץ וְ֭חֶרֶב וְחֵ֣ץ שָׁנ֑וּן אִ֥ישׁ עֹנֶ֥ה בְ֝רֵעֵ֗הוּ עֵ֣ד שָֽׁקֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man who bears false witness against his neighbour is like a maul, and a sword, and a sharp arrow.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A maul, and a sword, and a sharp arrow is one who testifies falsely against his friend.


###### Proverbs 25:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:19)


שֵׁ֣ן רֹ֭עָה וְרֶ֣גֶל מוּעָ֑דֶת מִבְטָ֥ח בּ֝וֹגֵ֗ד בְּי֣וֹם צָרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Confidence in an unfaithful man in time of trouble is like a broken tooth, and a foot out of joint.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Like a broken tooth and a slipping foot is a trust [(in a friend)] that deceives in a day of trouble.


###### Proverbs 25:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:20)


מַ֥עֲדֶה־בֶּ֨גֶד**׀** בְּי֣וֹם קָ֭רָה חֹ֣מֶץ עַל־נָ֑תֶר וְשָׁ֥ר בַּ֝שִּׁרִ֗ים עַ֣ל לֶב־רָֽע׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Like one who takes off a garment in cold weather, and like acid upon nitre, is he who sings songs to a heavy heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A worn-out garment on a cold day is like vinegar on nitre [(the vinegar eroding the nitre)], and, [similar to both,] is one who sings upon a bad heart [i.e., one who teaches Torah to a wicked disciple, who has no intention of abiding by his learning.]


###### Proverbs 25:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:21)


אִם־רָעֵ֣ב שֹׂ֭נַאֲךָ הַאֲכִלֵ֣הוּ לָ֑חֶם וְאִם־צָ֝מֵ֗א הַשְׁקֵ֥הוּ מָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thy enemy be hungry, give him bread to eat; and if he be thirsty, give him water to drink:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If your foe be hungry, feed him bread; and if he be thirsty, give him water to drink.


###### Proverbs 25:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:22)


כִּ֤י גֶחָלִ֗ים אַ֭תָּה חֹתֶ֣ה עַל־רֹאשׁ֑וֹ וַ֝יהֹוָ֗ה יְשַׁלֶּם־לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for thou shalt heap coals of fire upon his head, and the Lord shall reward thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For [in doing so] you draw coals [from the fire to heap] upon his head, and the L-rd will reconcile [him] to you.


###### Proverbs 25:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:23)


ר֣וּחַ צָ֭פוֹן תְּח֣וֹלֵֽל גָּ֑שֶׁם וּפָנִ֥ים נִ֝זְעָמִ֗ים לְשׁ֣וֹן סָֽתֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The north wind brings in the rain: so does an angry countenance, a back biting tongue.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A north wind brews a rain, and a secret [i.e., slanderous] tongue, a scornful Countenance.


###### Proverbs 25:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:24)


ט֗וֹב שֶׁ֥בֶת עַל־פִּנַּת־גָּ֑ג מֵאֵ֥שֶׁת (מדונים) [מִ֝דְיָנִ֗ים] וּבֵ֥ית חָֽבֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is better to dwell in the corner of the housetop, than with a brawling woman and in a roomy house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better [for the Shechinah] to dwell on a rooftop corner than [to have] a "contentious wife" [i.e., a sinning congregation] and a house [(a Temple)] of conjunction [i.e., where idolatry is conjoined to His worship].


###### Proverbs 25:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:25)


מַ֣יִם קָ֭רִים עַל־נֶ֣פֶשׁ עֲיֵפָ֑ה וּשְׁמוּעָ֥ה ט֝וֹבָ֗ה מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מֶרְחָֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As cold water to a thirsty soul, so is good news from a far country.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cold water on a weary soul and a good report from a distant land [(on a weary soul) are both restorative to it].


###### Proverbs 25:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:26)


מַעְיָ֣ן נִ֭רְפָּשׂ וּמָק֣וֹר מׇשְׁחָ֑ת צַ֝דִּ֗יק מָ֣ט לִפְנֵֽי־רָשָֽׁע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A righteous man falling down before the wicked is like a muddied fountain, and a polluted spring.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(As loathsome as)] a trodden spring and a fouled [water] source is a righteous man who bows before an evildoer [(fearing to reprove him)].


###### Proverbs 25:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:27)


אָ֘כֹ֤ל דְּבַ֣שׁ הַרְבּ֣וֹת לֹא־ט֑וֹב וְחֵ֖קֶר כְּבֹדָ֣ם כָּבֽוֹד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is not good to eat much honey: so it is honour to set bounds to honour.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Eating too much honey is not good [(It is not good to probe recondite matters (like the Divine Chariot and the creation) before the uninitiated, for they are likely to ridicule what is beyond them)], but the probing of their [the sages'] honor [(their enactments and decrees)] is honor [to them].


###### Proverbs 25:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/25:28)


עִ֣יר פְּ֭רוּצָה אֵ֣ין חוֹמָ֑ה אִ֝֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֤ר אֵ֖ין מַעְצָ֣ר לְרוּחֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that has no rule over his own spirit is like a city that is broken down, and without walls.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A breached city without a wall is the man with no constraint to his spirit.



## Chapter 26

###### Proverbs 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:1)


כַּשֶּׁ֤לֶג**׀** בַּקַּ֗יִץ וְכַמָּטָ֥ר בַּקָּצִ֑יר כֵּ֤ן לֹֽא־נָאוֶ֖ה לִכְסִ֣יל כָּבֽוֹד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As snow in summer, and as rain in harvest, so honour is not seemly for a fool.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As snow in the summer, and as rain in harvest time, so is honor unbefitting the fool.


###### Proverbs 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:2)


כַּצִּפּ֣וֹר לָ֭נוּד כַּדְּר֣וֹר לָע֑וּף כֵּ֥ן קִֽלְלַ֥ת חִ֝נָּ֗ם (לא) [ל֣וֹ] תָבֹֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Like a wandering sparrow, like a flying swallow, so a curse that is causeless comes home to roost.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Like a wandering sparrow and a flying swallow [(which return to their nest], so a vain curse returns to him [(who utters it)].


###### Proverbs 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:3)


שׁ֣וֹט לַ֭סּוּס מֶ֣תֶג לַחֲמ֑וֹר וְ֝שֵׁ֗בֶט לְגֵ֣ו כְּסִילִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A whip for the horse, a bridle for the ass, and a rod for the fool’s back.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A whip [is readied] for the horse, a bridle for the ass, and a rod for the back of fools.


###### Proverbs 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:4)


אַל־תַּ֣עַן כְּ֭סִיל כְּאִוַּלְתּ֑וֹ פֶּֽן־תִּשְׁוֶה־לּ֥וֹ גַם־אָֽתָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not answer a fool according to his folly, lest thou become like him thyself.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Answer not a fool according to his folly [i.e., cantankerously], lest you, too, be likened to him.


###### Proverbs 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:5)


עֲנֵ֣ה כְ֭סִיל כְּאִוַּלְתּ֑וֹ פֶּן־יִהְיֶ֖ה חָכָ֣ם בְּעֵינָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Answer a fool according to his folly, lest he be wise in his own conceit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Answer a fool according to his folly [i.e., if he entices you to idolatry], lest [(if you do not answer him)] he be wise in his eyes [(assuming that you have no answer)].


###### Proverbs 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:6)


מְקַצֶּ֣ה רַ֭גְלַיִם חָמָ֣ס שֹׁתֶ֑ה שֹׁלֵ֖חַ דְּבָרִ֣ים בְּיַד־כְּסִֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that sends a message by the hand of a fool cuts off his own feet, and drinks in damage.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He wears out the feet [of many messengers (whom he must send to amend the mistakes of the first)] and he drinks rancor [(the wrath of the object of his embassy)] — one who sends things with a fool.


###### Proverbs 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:7)


דַּלְי֣וּ שֹׁ֭קַיִם מִפִּסֵּ֑חַ וּ֝מָשָׁ֗ל בְּפִ֣י כְסִילִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Like the legs of a lame man which hang useless, so is a parable in the mouth of fools.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"The thighs [(of other men)] are 'raised' to the lame" — [this is] a parable in the mouth of fools [(who use it as an excuse not to acquire wisdom, claiming it to be "higher" than they are.)]


###### Proverbs 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:8)


כִּצְר֣וֹר אֶ֭בֶן בְּמַרְגֵּמָ֑ה כֵּן־נוֹתֵ֖ן לִכְסִ֣יל כָּבֽוֹד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As one who binds a stone in a sling, so is he that gives honour to a fool.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Like binding a stone in a sling is giving honor to a fool [i.e., both are destined to be cast away].


###### Proverbs 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:9)


ח֭וֹחַ עָלָ֣ה בְיַד־שִׁכּ֑וֹר וּ֝מָשָׁ֗ל בְּפִ֣י כְסִילִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Like a thorn that goes up into the hand of a drunkard, so is a parable in the mouth of fools.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Like a thorn in the hand of a drunkard [(which is destined to prick him)], so is [the following] parable in the mouth of fools:


###### Proverbs 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:10)


רַ֥ב מְחוֹלֵֽל־כֹּ֑ל וְשֹׂכֵ֥ר כְּ֝סִ֗יל וְשֹׂכֵ֥ר עֹבְרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A master craftsman performs everything; but he who hires a fool is as he who hires the passersby.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"The Master created all, and [for His work] He hires [also] the fool and He hires 'wayfarers'" [i.e., idlers — and He feeds them all. Why work then!]


###### Proverbs 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:11)


כְּ֭כֶלֶב שָׁ֣ב עַל־קֵא֑וֹ כְּ֝סִ֗יל שׁוֹנֶ֥ה בְאִוַּלְתּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As a dog returns to his vomit, so a fool returns to his folly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As a dog returns [to eat] what he has vomited, a fool repeats his foolishness.


###### Proverbs 26:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:12)


רָאִ֗יתָ אִ֭ישׁ חָכָ֣ם בְּעֵינָ֑יו תִּקְוָ֖ה לִכְסִ֣יל מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Seest thou a man wise in his own eyes? there is more hope of a fool than of him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Have you seen a man who is wise in his own eyes? There is more hope for one who is a fool [in his own eyes] than for him.


###### Proverbs 26:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:13)


אָמַ֣ר עָ֭צֵל שַׁ֣חַל בַּדָּ֑רֶךְ אֲ֝רִ֗י בֵּ֣ין הָרְחֹבֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The lazy man says, There is a lion in the way; a lion is in the streets.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lazy one says: "There is a lion on the path, a lion between the streets."


###### Proverbs 26:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:14)


הַ֭דֶּלֶת תִּסּ֣וֹב עַל־צִירָ֑הּ וְ֝עָצֵ֗ל עַל־מִטָּתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As the doors turns on its hinges, so does the slothful upon his bed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The door turns on its hinges, and the lazy one on his bed.


###### Proverbs 26:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:15)


טָ֘מַ֤ן עָצֵ֣ל יָ֭דוֹ בַּצַּלָּ֑חַת נִ֝לְאָ֗ה לַהֲשִׁיבָ֥הּ אֶל־פִּֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sluggard buries his hand in the dish; it grieves him to bring it back to his mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lazy man hides his hand in the [hot] kettle [(to warm it)] — he is too weary to return it to his mouth!


###### Proverbs 26:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:16)


חָכָ֣ם עָצֵ֣ל בְּעֵינָ֑יו מִ֝שִּׁבְעָ֗ה מְשִׁ֣יבֵי טָֽעַם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sluggard is wiser in his own eyes than seven men that can give sensible reasons.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lazy one is wiser in his own eyes than seven wise answerers.


###### Proverbs 26:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:17)


מַחֲזִ֥יק בְּאׇזְנֵי־כָ֑לֶב עֹבֵ֥ר מִ֝תְעַבֵּ֗ר עַל־רִ֥יב לֹּא־לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who passes by, and meddles with a strife not his own, is like one who takes a dog by the ears.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As one who seizes a dog's ears is he who grows wrathful over a dispute that is not his [(i.e., he will be "bitten")].


###### Proverbs 26:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:18)


כְּֽ֭מִתְלַהְלֵהַּ הַיֹּרֶ֥ה זִקִּ֗ים חִצִּ֥ים וָמָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As a madman who throws firebrands, arrows, and death,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As one who tires of throwing fire-brands [throws] arrows and death,


###### Proverbs 26:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:19)


כֵּֽן־אִ֭ישׁ רִמָּ֣ה אֶת־רֵעֵ֑הוּ וְ֝אָמַ֗ר הֲֽלֹא־מְשַׂחֵ֥ק אָֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so is a man who tricks his neighbour, and says, But I was only joking!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
(So) is one who deceives his neighbor [(enticing him from the paths of life to the paths of death)], and who says [(when the other becomes aware of the deception)], "I was only joking."


###### Proverbs 26:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:20)


בְּאֶ֣פֶס עֵ֭צִים תִּכְבֶּה־אֵ֑שׁ וּבְאֵ֥ין נִ֝רְגָּ֗ן יִשְׁתֹּ֥ק מָדֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Where no wood is, there the fire goes out: so where there is no talebearer, strife ceases.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Without wood the fire goes out, and without the spouter [of slander] the strife is silenced.


###### Proverbs 26:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:21)


פֶּחָ֣ם לְ֭גֶחָלִים וְעֵצִ֣ים לְאֵ֑שׁ וְאִ֥ישׁ (מדונים) [מִ֝דְיָנִ֗ים] לְחַרְחַר־רִֽיב׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As coals are to burning coals, and wood to fire; so is a contentious man to kindle strife.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As charcoal to [ignite] coal and wood to [fuel] a fire, so, the contentious man to stirring up strife.


###### Proverbs 26:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:22)


דִּבְרֵ֣י נִ֭רְגָּן כְּמִֽתְלַהֲמִ֑ים וְ֝הֵ֗ם יָרְד֥וּ חַדְרֵי־בָֽטֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The words of a talebearer are like dainties, and they go down into the innermost parts of the body.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The words of the spouter [of slander] are like blows and they enter one's innards [(causing dropsy)].


###### Proverbs 26:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:23)


כֶּ֣סֶף סִ֭יגִים מְצֻפֶּ֣ה עַל־חָ֑רֶשׂ שְׂפָתַ֖יִם דֹּלְקִ֣ים וְלֶב־רָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Burning lips and a wicked heart are like an earthenware dish covered with silver dross.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Like silver dross plating earthenware are lips running [(to entice men with all kinds of blandishments)] and a bad heart [beneath].


###### Proverbs 26:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:24)


בִּ֭שְׂפָתָו יִנָּכֵ֣ר שׂוֹנֵ֑א וּ֝בְקִרְבּ֗וֹ יָשִׁ֥ית מִרְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who hates dissembles with his lips, and lays up deceit within him;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With his lips the foe will seem "estranged" [(from hatred)], but within him he will plot guile.


###### Proverbs 26:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:25)


כִּֽי־יְחַנֵּ֣ן ק֭וֹלוֹ אַל־תַּאֲמֶן־בּ֑וֹ כִּ֤י שֶׁ֖בַע תּוֹעֵב֣וֹת בְּלִבּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when he speaks fair, do not believe him: for there are seven abominations in his heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Though his voice be ingratiating, do not trust to it, for there are seven abominations in his heart.


###### Proverbs 26:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:26)


תִּכַּסֶּ֣ה שִׂ֭נְאָה בְּמַשָּׁא֑וֹן תִּגָּלֶ֖ה רָעָת֣וֹ בְקָהָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He whose hatred is covered by deceit, his wickedness shall be revealed before the whole congregation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Though [a deed] that is hateful [to the L-rd] be covered in darkness, his [(the perpetrator's)] evil will be revealed among the multitude.


###### Proverbs 26:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:27)


כֹּֽרֶה־שַּׁ֭חַת בָּ֣הּ יִפּ֑וֹל וְג֥וֹלֵֽל אֶ֝֗בֶן אֵלָ֥יו תָּשֽׁוּב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The one who digs a pit shall fall into it: and if one rolls a stone, it will return upon him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who digs a pit will fall in it, and he who rolls a stone [against others] will have it turn back on him.


###### Proverbs 26:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/26:28)


לְֽשׁוֹן־שֶׁ֭קֶר יִשְׂנָ֣א דַכָּ֑יו וּפֶ֥ה חָ֝לָ֗ק יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה מִדְחֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A lying tongue hates its victims; and a flattering mouth works ruin.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[One who accepts] false speech [i.e., slander] hates his subjects [(Saul accepted slander against David and killed out Nov [his subjects, who assisted David])]; and the smooth [-talking] mouth causes rejection [by the L-rd of the victims of its enticements].



## Chapter 27

###### Proverbs 27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:1)


אַֽל־תִּ֭תְהַלֵּל בְּי֣וֹם מָחָ֑ר כִּ֤י לֹֽא־תֵ֝דַ֗ע מַה־יֵּ֥לֶד יֽוֹם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not boast thyself of tomorrow; for thou knowst not what today may bring forth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not vaunt yourself with tomorrow's day, for you know not what [evil this] day may bring.


###### Proverbs 27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:2)


יְהַלֶּלְךָ֣ זָ֣ר וְלֹא־פִ֑יךָ נׇ֝כְרִ֗י וְאַל־שְׂפָתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let another man praise thee, and not thy own mouth; a stranger, and not thy own lips.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let another praise you, and not your [own] mouth; a stranger, and not your [own] lips.


###### Proverbs 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:3)


כֹּֽבֶד־אֶ֭בֶן וְנֵ֣טֶל הַח֑וֹל וְכַ֥עַס אֱ֝וִ֗יל כָּבֵ֥ד מִשְּׁנֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A stone is heavy, and the sand weighty; but a fool’s wrath is heavier than both of them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The weight of stone and the mass of sand — the anger of a fool [i.e., the L-rd's anger against the world caused by the fool] is greater than both.


###### Proverbs 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:4)


אַכְזְרִיּ֣וּת חֵ֭מָה וְשֶׁ֣טֶף אָ֑ף וּמִ֥י יַ֝עֲמֹ֗ד לִפְנֵ֥י קִנְאָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wrath is cruel, and anger is overwhelming; but who is able to stand before envy?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Anger is cruel and wrath torrential, but who can withstand the rancor [of the L-rd]?


###### Proverbs 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:5)


ט֭וֹבָה תּוֹכַ֣חַת מְגֻלָּ֑ה מֵאַהֲבָ֥ה מְסֻתָּֽרֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Open rebuke is better than hidden love.


###### Proverbs 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:6)


נֶ֭אֱמָנִים פִּצְעֵ֣י אוֹהֵ֑ב וְ֝נַעְתָּר֗וֹת נְשִׁיק֥וֹת שׂוֹנֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Faithful are the wounds of a friend; but the kisses of an enemy are profuse.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Faithful are the wounds of a lover, but cloying are the kisses of a foe.


###### Proverbs 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:7)


נֶ֣פֶשׁ שְׂ֭בֵעָה תָּב֣וּס נֹ֑פֶת וְנֶ֥פֶשׁ רְ֝עֵבָ֗ה כׇּל־מַ֥ר מָתֽוֹק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The full soul loathes a honeycomb; but to the hungry soul every bitter thing is sweet.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The sated soul will tread down a honeycomb, but to the hungry soul all bitter is sweet.


###### Proverbs 27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:8)


כְּ֭צִפּוֹר נוֹדֶ֣דֶת מִן־קִנָּ֑הּ כֵּֽן־אִ֝֗ישׁ נוֹדֵ֥ד מִמְּקוֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As a bird that wanders from her nest, so is a man who wanders from his place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As a bird wandering from its nest is a man wandering from his place [i.e., a Torah scholar who does not review his learning].


###### Proverbs 27:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:9)


שֶׁ֣מֶן וּ֭קְטֹרֶת יְשַׂמַּֽח־לֵ֑ב וּמֶ֥תֶק רֵ֝עֵ֗הוּ מֵעֲצַת־נָֽפֶשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Ointment and perfume rejoice the heart: so does the sweetness of a man’s friend by hearty counsel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[The fragrances of] oil [(balsamum)] and incense rejoice the heart, and the sweet [discourse] of one's friend [avails him] more than his own counsel.


###### Proverbs 27:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:10)


רֵ֥עֲךָ֨ (ורעה) [וְרֵ֪עַ] אָבִ֡יךָ אַֽל־תַּעֲזֹ֗ב וּבֵ֥ית אָחִ֗יךָ אַל־תָּ֭בוֹא בְּי֣וֹם אֵידֶ֑ךָ ט֥וֹב שָׁכֵ֥ן קָ֝ר֗וֹב מֵאָ֥ח רָחֽוֹק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not forsake thy own friend, and thy father’s friend; nor go into thy brother’s house in the day of thy calamity: for better is a neighbour that is near than a brother far off.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not forsake your Friend and the Friend of your father, and do not come [(for aid)] to the house of your brother [Esav and Yishmael] on the day of your misfortune. Better that there dwell [in your midst] the Near One [(He who is near to those who are near to Him)] than a distant brother [i.e., the brother who distanced himself from you (viz. Genesis 27:41)].


###### Proverbs 27:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:11)


חֲכַ֣ם בְּ֭נִי וְשַׂמַּ֣ח לִבִּ֑י וְאָשִׁ֖יבָה חֹרְפִ֣י דָבָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My son, be wise, and make my heart glad, that I may answer him who taunts me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Grow wise, my son, and rejoice my heart, and I will answer my shamer in kind.


###### Proverbs 27:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:12)


עָר֤וּם**׀** רָאָ֣ה רָעָ֣ה נִסְתָּ֑ר פְּ֝תָאיִ֗ם עָבְר֥וּ נֶעֱנָֽשׁוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A prudent man sees the evil, and hides himself; but simpletons pass on, and are punished.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise man sees the [oncoming] evil and hides [from it (i.e., he desists from sinning)], and the fools pass on [i.e., continue in their evil ways] [and] are punished.


###### Proverbs 27:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:13)


קַח־בִּ֭גְדוֹ כִּי־עָ֣רַב זָ֑ר וּבְעַ֖ד נׇכְרִיָּ֣ה חַבְלֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take his garment when he is surety for a stranger, and take his pledge on behalf of an alien woman.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take [(without compunction)] the garment of him who goes security for a stranger, and take his security for an alien woman.


###### Proverbs 27:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:14)


מְבָ֘רֵ֤ךְ רֵעֵ֨הוּ**׀** בְּק֣וֹל גָּ֭דוֹל בַּבֹּ֣קֶר הַשְׁכֵּ֑ים קְ֝לָלָ֗ה תֵּחָ֥שֶׁב לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that blesses his friend with a loud voice, rising early in the morning, it shall be counted a curse to him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[If] one blesses his friend [(for his generosity)] in a loud voice early in the morning, it may become a curse to him [(i.e., he may be beleaguered for his bounty)].


###### Proverbs 27:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:15)


דֶּ֣לֶף ט֭וֹרֵד בְּי֣וֹם סַגְרִ֑יר וְאֵ֥שֶׁת (מדונים) [מִ֝דְיָנִ֗ים] נִשְׁתָּוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A continual dripping on a very rainy day and a contentious woman are alike.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A harrowing drip on a rainy day and a contentious woman are alike.


###### Proverbs 27:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:16)


צֹפְנֶ֥יהָ צָפַן־ר֑וּחַ וְשֶׁ֖מֶן יְמִינ֣וֹ יִקְרָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whoever would hide her hides the wind, like the ointment of his right hand, which betrays itself.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who would guard her [(from her harlotry)] might [(just as well)] guard the wind; and he will call [leprosy to come upon him (so that he can divorce her) just as a leper is cleansed by] the oil [placed upon] his right [thumb].


###### Proverbs 27:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:17)


בַּרְזֶ֣ל בְּבַרְזֶ֣ל יָ֑חַד וְ֝אִ֗ישׁ יַ֣חַד פְּנֵֽי־רֵעֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Iron sharpens iron; so one man sharpens another.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As iron sharpens iron, so does one man [(a Torah scholar)] sharpen his friend [(in halachah)].


###### Proverbs 27:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:18)


נֹצֵ֣ר תְּ֭אֵנָה יֹאכַ֣ל פִּרְיָ֑הּ וְשֹׁמֵ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣יו יְכֻבָּֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who guards the fig tree shall eat its fruit: so he who waits on his master shall be honoured.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who guards the fig tree will eat its fruit, and he who guards his master will be honored.


###### Proverbs 27:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:19)


כַּ֭מַּיִם הַפָּנִ֣ים לַפָּנִ֑ים כֵּ֤ן לֵֽב־הָ֝אָדָ֗ם לָאָדָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As in water face answers to face, so the heart of man to man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As [with] water: the face [that you "show" it] is the face [it shows you]; so the heart of a man [i.e., the love he shows his friend] is the [(heart of the)] man [that he gets in return].


###### Proverbs 27:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:20)


שְׁא֣וֹל וַ֭אֲבַדֹּה לֹ֣א תִשְׂבַּ֑עְנָה וְעֵינֵ֥י הָ֝אָדָ֗ם לֹ֣א תִשְׂבַּֽעְנָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She᾽ol and Avaddon are never full; so the eyes of man are never satisfied.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Sheol and Gehinnom are not sated [(with receiving the wicked)] just as a 'wicked' man's eyes are not sated [(with straying after his evil inclination and filling his lust)].


###### Proverbs 27:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:21)


מַצְרֵ֣ף לַ֭כֶּסֶף וְכ֣וּר לַזָּהָ֑ב וְ֝אִ֗ישׁ לְפִ֣י מַהֲלָלֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The refining pot is for silver, arid the furnace for gold; and a man is tried according to his praise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The refining pot for [assaying and refining] silver, and the crucible for gold, and a man [is assessed] by his praise [(i.e., by praise of his good deeds by his fellow men)].


###### Proverbs 27:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:22)


אִ֥ם־תִּכְתּֽוֹשׁ־אֶת־הָאֱוִ֨יל**׀** בַּ֥מַּכְתֵּ֡שׁ בְּת֣וֹךְ הָ֭רִיפוֹת בַּעֱלִ֑י לֹא־תָס֥וּר מֵ֝עָלָ֗יו אִוַּלְתּֽוֹ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though thou shouldst grind a fool in a mortar among crushed grain with a pestle, yet will his foolishness not depart from him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you crush the fool in a mortar, among the crushed grain, with a pestle, you will not remove his foolishness from him.


###### Proverbs 27:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:23)


יָדֹ֣עַ תֵּ֭דַע פְּנֵ֣י צֹאנֶ֑ךָ שִׁ֥ית לִ֝בְּךָ֗ לַעֲדָרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Be diligent to know the state of thy flocks, and look well to thy herds.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Know [the needs of] your sheep; give heart to your flocks.


###### Proverbs 27:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:24)


כִּ֤י לֹ֣א לְעוֹלָ֣ם חֹ֑סֶן וְאִם־נֵ֝֗זֶר לְד֣וֹר (דור) [וָדֽוֹר]׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For riches are not forever: and does the crown endure to all generations?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For wealth [i.e., gold and silver] does not last forever; nor is it a crown [that is passed on] from one generation to another [(therefore, pay heed, too, to your lesser possessions.)]


###### Proverbs 27:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:25)


גָּלָ֣ה חָ֭צִיר וְנִרְאָה־דֶ֑שֶׁא וְ֝נֶאֶסְפ֗וּ עִשְּׂב֥וֹת הָרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the grass sprouts forth, and the young grass appears, and the herbs on the mountains are gathered in;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[When, in the days of Nissan,] the grass appears and the verdure is seen and the mountain grass is gathered [for the flocks],


###### Proverbs 27:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:26)


כְּבָשִׂ֥ים לִלְבוּשֶׁ֑ךָ וּמְחִ֥יר שָׂ֝דֶ֗ה עַתּוּדִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
lambs shall provide thy clothing, and goats the price of a field:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[then is the time to shear] the lambs for your clothing; and [let] the he-goats [be as] the worth of a field [to you (for you can eat the flesh and sell the hides)].


###### Proverbs 27:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/27:27)


וְדֵ֤י**׀** חֲלֵ֬ב עִזִּ֗ים לְֽ֭לַחְמְךָ לְלֶ֣חֶם בֵּיתֶ֑ךָ וְ֝חַיִּ֗ים לְנַעֲרוֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou shalt have goats’ milk enough for thy food, for the food of thy household, and for the maintenance of thy maidens.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [for your] sufficiency — for your bread and the bread of your household and (for) the livelihood of your maidens — [let] the milk of your goats [suffice].



## Chapter 28

###### Proverbs 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:1)


נָ֣סוּ וְאֵין־רֹדֵ֣ף רָשָׁ֑ע וְ֝צַדִּיקִ֗ים כִּכְפִ֥יר יִבְטָֽח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wicked flee when no man pursues: but the righteous are bold as a lion.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[When their time comes,] the wicked will flee [and fall] though none pursue, and the righteous [(their heart staunch in the L-rd)] will trust [in Him] as a young lion [(trusts in its strength)].


###### Proverbs 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:2)


בְּפֶ֣שַֽׁע אֶ֭רֶץ רַבִּ֣ים שָׂרֶ֑יהָ וּבְאָדָ֥ם מֵבִ֥ין יֹ֝דֵ֗עַ כֵּ֣ן יַאֲרִֽיךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the land is sinful, its princes are numerous: but when there is a judicious man, knowing what is right, he shall endure.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is trouble for the land if their officers are many, and through an understanding, knowing, man it [(the trouble)] is kept off.


###### Proverbs 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:3)


גֶּֽבֶר־רָ֭שׁ וְעֹשֵׁ֣ק דַּלִּ֑ים מָטָ֥ר סֹ֝חֵ֗ף וְאֵ֣ין לָֽחֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A poor man that oppresses the weak is like a sweeping rain which leaves no food.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A poor man [i.e., an ignorant judge] who robs the poor [(by his haste in judgment] is [like] a torrential rain [(which sweeps the field)] and leaves no bread [i.e., no produce].


###### Proverbs 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:4)


עֹזְבֵ֣י ת֭וֹרָה יְהַלְל֣וּ רָשָׁ֑ע וְשֹׁמְרֵ֥י ת֝וֹרָ֗ה יִתְגָּ֥רוּ בָֽם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They who forsake Tora praise the wicked: but such as keep Tora contend with them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The forsakers of Torah praise the wicked, but the keepers of Torah contend with them.


###### Proverbs 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:5)


אַנְשֵׁי־רָ֭ע לֹא־יָבִ֣ינוּ מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּמְבַקְשֵׁ֥י יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה יָבִ֥ינוּ כֹֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Evil men understand not justice: but they who seek the Lord understand all things.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Men of evil will not take to heart the retribution [in store for them (and repent of their wickedness)], but the seekers of the L-rd will take all [(good and evil)] to heart.


###### Proverbs 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:6)


טֽוֹב־רָ֭שׁ הוֹלֵ֣ךְ בְּתֻמּ֑וֹ מֵעִקֵּ֥שׁ דְּ֝רָכַ֗יִם וְה֣וּא עָשִֽׁיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Better is the poor who walks in his uprightness, than he who deals perversely in his ways, though he be rich.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Better a poor man [(even one who is poor in Torah)] who walks in his innocence [(doing good)] than a devious one who is rich.


###### Proverbs 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:7)


נוֹצֵ֣ר תּ֭וֹרָה בֵּ֣ן מֵבִ֑ין וְרֹעֶ֥ה ז֝וֹלְלִ֗ים יַכְלִ֥ים אָבִֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A wise son keeps Tora, but he that is a companion of riotous men shames his father.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The keeper of Torah is an understanding son, but the companion of gluttons shames his father.


###### Proverbs 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:8)


מַרְבֶּ֣ה ה֭וֹנוֹ בְּנֶ֣שֶׁךְ (ובתרבית) [וְתַרְבִּ֑ית] לְחוֹנֵ֖ן דַּלִּ֣ים יִקְבְּצֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who by usury and unjust gain increases his substance, gathers it for him who will graciously regard the poor.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who increases his wealth through graft and interest gathers it for the patron of the poor. [(His swindling comes to the attention of the authorities and they confiscate his property and devote it to public improvements.)]


###### Proverbs 28:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:9)


מֵסִ֣יר אׇ֭זְנוֹ מִשְּׁמֹ֣עַ תּוֹרָ֑ה גַּ֥ם תְּ֝פִלָּת֗וֹ תּוֹעֵבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who turns away his ear from hearing Tora, even his prayer is abomination.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who turns aside his ear from hearing [the] Torah [of another], his prayer, likewise, will be accounted an abomination.


###### Proverbs 28:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:10)


מַשְׁגֶּ֤ה יְשָׁרִ֨ים**׀** בְּדֶ֥רֶךְ רָ֗ע בִּשְׁחוּת֥וֹ הֽוּא־יִפּ֑וֹל וּ֝תְמִימִ֗ים יִנְחֲלוּ־טֽוֹב׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who causes the righteous to go astray in an evil way, he shall fall himself into his own pit: but the innocent shall inherit good.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Those who lead the just astray in an evil way will fall in their own pit, but the innocent will inherit [i.e., benefit from] the good [that they bequeath].


###### Proverbs 28:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:11)


חָכָ֣ם בְּ֭עֵינָיו אִ֣ישׁ עָשִׁ֑יר וְדַ֖ל מֵבִ֣ין יַחְקְרֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The rich man is wise in his own conceit; but the poor that has understanding searches him out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The rich man [i.e., the master] is wise in his eyes, but the poor man [(the disciple)] will question him [(and make him wiser)].


###### Proverbs 28:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:12)


בַּעֲלֹ֣ץ צַ֭דִּיקִים רַבָּ֣ה תִפְאָ֑רֶת וּבְק֥וּם רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים יְחֻפַּ֥שׂ אָדָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When righteous men rejoice, there is great glory: but when the wicked rise, men hide themselves.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the exultation of the righteous there is much glory, but in the rise of the wicked, men are searched out [and libeled].


###### Proverbs 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:13)


מְכַסֶּ֣ה פְ֭שָׁעָיו לֹ֣א יַצְלִ֑יחַ וּמוֹדֶ֖ה וְעֹזֵ֣ב יְרֻחָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that covers up his sins shall not prosper: but whoever confesses and forsakes them shall have mercy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The concealer of his sins will not succeed, but he who acknowledges and forsakes them will be granted mercy.


###### Proverbs 28:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:14)


אַשְׁרֵ֣י אָ֭דָם מְפַחֵ֣ד תָּמִ֑יד וּמַקְשֶׁ֥ה לִ֝בּ֗וֹ יִפּ֥וֹל בְּרָעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Happy is the man who fears always: but he who hardens his heart shall fall into mischief.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Fortunate is the man who fears [punishment for sin] always, but he who hardens his heart [against such fear] will fall into the evil.


###### Proverbs 28:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:15)


אֲֽרִי־נֹ֭הֵם וְדֹ֣ב שׁוֹקֵ֑ק מוֹשֵׁ֥ל רָ֝שָׁ֗ע עַ֣ל עַם־דָּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As a roaring lion, and a hungry bear, so is a wicked ruler over the poor people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A roaring lion and a growling bear is a wicked lord to a poor people.


###### Proverbs 28:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:16)


נָגִ֗יד חֲסַ֣ר תְּ֭בוּנוֹת וְרַ֥ב מַעֲשַׁקּ֑וֹת (שנאי) [שֹׂ֥נֵא] בֶ֝֗צַע יַאֲרִ֥יךְ יָמִֽים׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The prince who lacks understanding is also a great oppressor: but the one who hates covetousness shall prolong his days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A prince lacking understanding will increase plunder, for [he does not understand that] the hater of plunder prolongs [his] days.


###### Proverbs 28:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:17)


אָ֭דָם עָשֻׁ֣ק בְּדַם־נָ֑פֶשׁ עַד־בּ֥וֹר יָ֝נ֗וּס אַל־יִתְמְכוּ־בֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man that is burdened with the blood of any person shall flee to the pit; let none support him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The man who is guilty of another's blood [i.e., one who causes him to sin and is responsible for the loss of his soul], will run [(to seek forgiveness)] to the pit [i.e., until the day of his death]; he will not be abetted [by Heaven to repent ("so that he not be in Gan Eden and his disciple in Gehinnom")].


###### Proverbs 28:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:18)


הוֹלֵ֣ךְ תָּ֭מִים יִוָּשֵׁ֑עַ וְנֶעְקַ֥שׁ דְּ֝רָכַ֗יִם יִפּ֥וֹל בְּאֶחָֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who walks uprightly shall be saved: but he who takes crooked paths shall fall suddenly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The walker in innocence will be saved [(from all the dangers confronting him)], but he who is devious in his ways will fall [even] in one [(and not rise again)].


###### Proverbs 28:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:19)


עֹבֵ֣ד אַ֭דְמָתוֹ יִֽשְׂבַּֽע־לָ֑חֶם וּמְרַדֵּ֥ף רֵ֝יקִ֗ים יִֽשְׂבַּֽע־רִֽישׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who tills his land shall have plenty of bread: but he who follows after vain persons shall have poverty enough.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The worker of his field will be sated with bread, but the runner after vanities will be sated with poverty.


###### Proverbs 28:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:20)


אִ֣ישׁ אֱ֭מוּנוֹת רַב־בְּרָכ֑וֹת וְאָ֥ץ לְ֝הַעֲשִׁ֗יר לֹ֣א יִנָּקֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A faithful man shall abound with blessings: but he who makes haste to be rich shall not go unpunished.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The man of faith [(one who separates his tithes in faith, no one but G-d seeing him do so) will receive] an abundance of blessings, but one who is overhasty to get rich [(at the expense of the poor)] will not be absolved.


###### Proverbs 28:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:21)


הַֽכֵּר־פָּנִ֥ים לֹא־ט֑וֹב וְעַל־פַּת־לֶ֝֗חֶם יִפְשַׁע־גָּֽבֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
To have respect of persons is not good: for a man will transgress for a piece of bread.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[For a judge] to show favoritism is not good. Would a man [(a judge)] incriminate himself for a loaf of bread!


###### Proverbs 28:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:22)


נִ֥בְהָֽל־לַה֗וֹן אִ֭ישׁ רַ֣ע עָ֑יִן וְלֹא־יֵ֝דַ֗ע כִּי־חֶ֥סֶר יְבֹאֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The man of evil eye is eager for wealth: he does not know that want will come upon him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Greedy for wealth is the narrow-eyed man [(he who withholds his tithes)], not knowing that want [(blight)] will befall him [for his greed].


###### Proverbs 28:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:23)


מ֘וֹכִ֤יחַ אָדָ֣ם אַ֭חֲרַי חֵ֣ן יִמְצָ֑א מִֽמַּחֲלִ֥יק לָשֽׁוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who rebukes a man shall find more favour afterwards than he who flatters with the tongue.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One who reproves a man [for his sins] after Me [i.e., to follow Me] will find more favor than the "smoother of the tongue."


###### Proverbs 28:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:24)


גּוֹזֵ֤ל**׀** אָ֘בִ֤יו וְאִמּ֗וֹ וְאֹמֵ֥ר אֵֽין־פָּ֑שַׁע חָבֵ֥ר ה֝֗וּא לְאִ֣ישׁ מַשְׁחִֽית׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who robs his father or his mother, and says, It is no transgression; he is companion of a destroyer.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who steals from his Father and his mother [(the congregation of Israel) by causing Israel to sin (thereby "stealing" His children from Him and good from them)] is a companion to the destroyer [Yeravam ben Nevat].


###### Proverbs 28:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:25)


רְחַב־נֶ֭פֶשׁ יְגָרֶ֣ה מָד֑וֹן וּבֹטֵ֖חַ עַל־יְהֹוָ֣ה יְדֻשָּֽׁן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He that is of a greedy heart stirs up strife: but he that puts his trust in the Lord shall be made rich.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
