The "broadener" of his soul [(to attain all of his lusts)] stirs up [against himself] the attribute of justice, and he who trusts to the L-rd will be "fattened."


###### Proverbs 28:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:26)


בּוֹטֵ֣חַ בְּ֭לִבּוֹ ה֣וּא כְסִ֑יל וְהוֹלֵ֥ךְ בְּ֝חׇכְמָ֗ה ה֣וּא יִמָּלֵֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who trusts in his own heart is a fool: but he who walks wisely, shall escape.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who trusts to [(the machinations of)] his heart is a fool, but the walker in [the way of] the wise [(trusting in the L-rd)] will escape [(all evil)].


###### Proverbs 28:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:27)


נוֹתֵ֣ן לָ֭רָשׁ אֵ֣ין מַחְס֑וֹר וּמַעְלִ֥ים עֵ֝ינָ֗יו רַב־מְאֵרֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who gives to the poor shall not lack: but he who hides his eyes shall have many a curse.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who gives [charity] to the poor man [will suffer] no want, but he who averts his eyes [from him will suffer] much blight.


###### Proverbs 28:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/28:28)


בְּק֣וּם רְ֭שָׁעִים יִסָּתֵ֣ר אָדָ֑ם וּ֝בְאׇבְדָ֗ם יִרְבּ֥וּ צַדִּיקִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the wicked rise, men hide themselves: but when they perish, the righteous increase.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the wicked rise, men hide; but when they go lost, the righteous increase.



## Chapter 29

###### Proverbs 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:1)


אִ֣ישׁ תּ֭וֹכָחוֹת מַקְשֶׁה־עֹ֑רֶף פֶּ֥תַע יִ֝שָּׁבֵ֗ר וְאֵ֣ין מַרְפֵּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He, that being often reproved hardens his neck, shall suddenly be broken, beyond healing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The man [(who is in need)] of rebuke, and stiffens his neck [(not to hear it)] will be suddenly broken with none to heal [him].


###### Proverbs 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:2)


בִּרְב֣וֹת צַ֭דִּיקִים יִשְׂמַ֣ח הָעָ֑ם וּבִמְשֹׁ֥ל רָ֝שָׁ֗ע יֵאָ֥נַֽח עָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the righteous are on the increase, the people rejoice: but when the wicked man bears rule, the people mourn.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the increase of the righteous, the people will rejoice, but in the rule of the wicked, the people will sigh.


###### Proverbs 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:3)


אִישׁ־אֹהֵ֣ב חׇ֭כְמָה יְשַׂמַּ֣ח אָבִ֑יו וְרֹעֶ֥ה ז֝וֹנ֗וֹת יְאַבֶּד־הֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who loves wisdom gladdens his father: but he who keeps company with harlots wastes his substance.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The man who loves wisdom makes his father rejoice, but the consort of harlots loses a fortune.


###### Proverbs 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:4)


מֶ֗לֶךְ בְּ֭מִשְׁפָּט יַעֲמִ֣יד אָ֑רֶץ וְאִ֖ישׁ תְּרוּמ֣וֹת יֶהֶרְסֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The king by justice establishes the land: but he who exacts gifts overthrows it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The king establishes the land through justice, but a man [i.e., a judge] of vanity [(who is precipitate in judgment)] destroys it.


###### Proverbs 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:5)


גֶּ֭בֶר מַחֲלִ֣יק עַל־רֵעֵ֑הוּ רֶ֝֗שֶׁת פּוֹרֵ֥שׂ עַל־פְּעָמָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man who flatters his neighbour spreads a net for his feet.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The man who "smooth-talks" his friend spreads the net for his [own] feet.


###### Proverbs 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:6)


בְּפֶ֤שַֽׁע אִ֣ישׁ רָ֣ע מוֹקֵ֑שׁ וְ֝צַדִּ֗יק יָר֥וּן וְשָׂמֵֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the transgression of an evil man there is a snare: but the righteous man sings and rejoices.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The offense of the wicked one [sets] a snare [for his feet], but the righteous one [(not having walked in his ways)] sings and rejoices.


###### Proverbs 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:7)


יֹדֵ֣עַ צַ֭דִּיק דִּ֣ין דַּלִּ֑ים רָ֝שָׁ֗ע לֹא־יָבִ֥ין דָּֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The righteous man apprehends the cause of the poor: but the wicked man cannot grasp that knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The righteous one knows the suffering of the poor [and tries to alleviate it]; the wicked one does not give heart to know.


###### Proverbs 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:8)


אַנְשֵׁ֣י לָ֭צוֹן יָפִ֣יחוּ קִרְיָ֑ה וַ֝חֲכָמִ֗ים יָשִׁ֥יבוּ אָֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Scornful men stir up a city into strife: but wise men turn away wrath.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Men of mockery will enflame the city, but the wise will turn back wrath.


###### Proverbs 29:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:9)


אִֽישׁ־חָכָ֗ם נִ֭שְׁפָּט אֶת־אִ֣ישׁ אֱוִ֑יל וְרָגַ֥ז וְ֝שָׂחַ֗ק וְאֵ֣ין נָֽחַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If a wise man contends with a foolish man, whether he rage or laugh, there is no rest.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[When] a wise man contends with a fool, whether he [(the wise man)] is angry [with him] or smiles [at him], he will find no peace.


###### Proverbs 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:10)


אַנְשֵׁ֣י דָ֭מִים יִשְׂנְאוּ־תָ֑ם וִ֝ישָׁרִ֗ים יְבַקְשׁ֥וּ נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The bloodthirsty hate the innocent: but the just seek his good.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Men of blood hate the innocent one, but the upright seek his soul [i.e., love him].


###### Proverbs 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:11)


כׇּל־ר֭וּחוֹ יוֹצִ֣יא כְסִ֑יל וְ֝חָכָ֗ם בְּאָח֥וֹר יְשַׁבְּחֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A fool vents all his anger: but a wise man keeping it in subdues it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The fool vents all his wrath, and the wise man thereafter assuages it.


###### Proverbs 29:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:12)


מֹ֭שֵׁל מַקְשִׁ֣יב עַל־דְּבַר־שָׁ֑קֶר כׇּֽל־מְשָׁרְתָ֥יו רְשָׁעִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If a ruler hearken to lies, all his servants are wicked.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The ruler who gives ear to a falsehood — all of his servants are wicked.


###### Proverbs 29:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:13)


רָ֤שׁ וְאִ֣ישׁ תְּכָכִ֣ים נִפְגָּ֑שׁוּ מֵ֤אִיר־עֵינֵ֖י שְׁנֵיהֶ֣ם יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The poor and the deceitful man meet together: the Lord gives light to the eyes of both.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A poor man [i.e., one who is "poor" in Torah] and a man of depth [in Torah] have met [i.e., they are on a par]. The L-rd enlightens the eyes of both.


###### Proverbs 29:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:14)


מֶ֤לֶךְ שׁוֹפֵ֣ט בֶּאֱמֶ֣ת דַּלִּ֑ים כִּ֝סְא֗וֹ לָעַ֥ד יִכּֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The king who faithfully judges the poor, his throne shall be established for ever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The king who judges the poor in truth — his seat will be established forever.


###### Proverbs 29:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:15)


שֵׁ֣בֶט וְ֭תוֹכַחַת יִתֵּ֣ן חׇכְמָ֑ה וְנַ֥עַר מְ֝שֻׁלָּ֗ח מֵבִ֥ישׁ אִמּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The rod and reproof give wisdom: but a child left to himself brings shame to his mother.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The rod and rebuke give wisdom, but the youth who is given free rein shames his mother [viz., Yishmael, who ultimately caused his mother, Hagar, to be cast out.]


###### Proverbs 29:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:16)


בִּרְב֣וֹת רְ֭שָׁעִים יִרְבֶּה־פָּ֑שַׁע וְ֝צַדִּיקִ֗ים בְּֽמַפַּלְתָּ֥ם יִרְאֽוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the wicked are multiplied, transgression increases: both the righteous shall witness their fall.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the increase of the wicked, offense increases, and the righteous will look on at their downfall.


###### Proverbs 29:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:17)


יַסֵּ֣ר בִּ֭נְךָ וִינִיחֶ֑ךָ וְיִתֵּ֖ן מַעֲדַנִּ֣ים לְנַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Correct thy son, and he will give thee rest; and he will give delight to thy soul.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Chastise your son and he will give you peace, and he will give pleasures to your soul.


###### Proverbs 29:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:18)


בְּאֵ֣ין חָ֭זוֹן יִפָּ֣רַֽע עָ֑ם וְשֹׁמֵ֖ר תּוֹרָ֣ה אַשְׁרֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Where there is no vision, the people become unruly: but happy is he who keeps Tora.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When there is no prophecy [i.e., when prophecy is removed from Israel because of their shaming the prophets], the people are breached [i.e., they become corrupt], and fortunate is the keeper of Torah.


###### Proverbs 29:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:19)


בִּ֭דְבָרִים לֹא־יִוָּ֣סֶר עָ֑בֶד כִּי־יָ֝בִ֗ין וְאֵ֣ין מַעֲנֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A servant will not be corrected by words: for though he understands he will not answer.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With words [alone] the servant [i.e., one who rebels against the judges] will not be chastised, for he will perceive that there is no response [i.e., that his reprover remains silent after reproving him (so that words must be "reinforced" by blows)].


###### Proverbs 29:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:20)


חָזִ֗יתָ אִ֭ישׁ אָ֣ץ בִּדְבָרָ֑יו תִּקְוָ֖ה לִכְסִ֣יל מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Seest thou a man that is hasty in his words? there is more hope for a fool than for him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Have you seen a man hasty with his words [i.e., overhasty to respond]? There is more hope for a fool than for him.


###### Proverbs 29:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:21)


מְפַנֵּ֣ק מִנֹּ֣עַר עַבְדּ֑וֹ וְ֝אַחֲרִית֗וֹ יִהְיֶ֥ה מָנֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He who pampers his servant from a child shall have him become his master at last.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[If] one indulges his servant [i.e., his evil inclination] from youth, in the end it will be his master.


###### Proverbs 29:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:22)


אִֽישׁ־אַ֭ף יְגָרֶ֣ה מָד֑וֹן וּבַ֖עַל חֵמָ֣ה רַב־פָּֽשַׁע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
An angry man stirs up strife, and a furious man abounds in transgression.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The man of wrath will stir up the attribute of justice, and the angry man offends greatly.


###### Proverbs 29:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:23)


גַּאֲוַ֣ת אָ֭דָם תַּשְׁפִּילֶ֑נּוּ וּשְׁפַל־ר֝֗וּחַ יִתְמֹ֥ךְ כָּבֽוֹד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man’s pride shall bring him low: but the humble in spirit shall attain honour.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The pride of man will bring him low, and the humble of spirit will court honor.


###### Proverbs 29:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:24)


חוֹלֵ֣ק עִם־גַּ֭נָּב שׂוֹנֵ֣א נַפְשׁ֑וֹ אָלָ֥ה יִ֝שְׁמַ֗ע וְלֹ֣א יַגִּֽיד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whoever is partner with a thief is his own enemy: he hears the adjuration of witnesses, but discloses nothing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who divides with a thief hates his [own] soul; he will hear the adjuration [(in court) to reveal the thief, but (being his "partner,")] he will not speak out.


###### Proverbs 29:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:25)


חֶרְדַּ֣ת אָ֭דָם יִתֵּ֣ן מוֹקֵ֑שׁ וּבוֹטֵ֖חַ בַּיהֹוָ֣ה יְשֻׂגָּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The fear of man brings a snare: but he who puts his trust in the Lord shall be protected.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The snare [of sin] will affright a man, but the truster in the L-rd will be emboldened.


###### Proverbs 29:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:26)


רַ֭בִּים מְבַקְשִׁ֣ים פְּנֵֽי־מוֹשֵׁ֑ל וּ֝מֵיְהֹוָ֗ה מִשְׁפַּט־אִֽישׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Many seek the ruler’s favour; but a man’s judgment comes from the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Many seek [(to have their case adjudicated)] before the ruler, but a man's judgment is from the L-rd.


###### Proverbs 29:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/29:27)


תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת צַ֭דִּיקִים אִ֣ישׁ עָ֑וֶל וְתוֹעֲבַ֖ת רָשָׁ֣ע יְשַׁר־דָּֽרֶךְ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
An unjust man is an abomination to the just: and he that is upright in the way is an abomination to the wicked.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The abomination of the righteous is a man of wrong, and the abomination of the wicked is he who is just in his ways.



## Chapter 30

###### Proverbs 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:1)


דִּבְרֵ֤י**׀** אָג֥וּר בִּן־יָקֶ֗ה הַמַּ֫שָּׂ֥א נְאֻ֣ם הַ֭גֶּבֶר לְאִיתִיאֵ֑ל לְאִ֖יתִיאֵ֣ל וְאֻכָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The words of Agur son of Yaqe, the burden. Thus says the man, I am weary, O God, I am weary, O God, and I am consumed:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These are the words [of Solomon] who collected understanding [and] "vomited" [it]. This prophecy was stated by the man [(Solomon)] about himself]: Because [I said (contrary to the exhortations of the Torah)]: "Because G-d is with me — Because G-d is with me, I can" [(take many wives and multiply gold and horses and not go astray)] —


###### Proverbs 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:2)


כִּ֤י בַ֣עַר אָנֹכִ֣י מֵאִ֑ישׁ וְלֹֽא־בִינַ֖ת אָדָ֣ם לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for I am more brutish than a man, and have not the understanding of a man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Now I see] that I am void of [the intellect of] man and do not have man's understanding.


###### Proverbs 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:3)


וְלֹֽא־לָמַ֥דְתִּי חׇכְמָ֑ה וְדַ֖עַת קְדֹשִׁ֣ים אֵדָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have not learned wisdom; that I should desire to have the knowledge of the holy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I did not learn wisdom, and I [did not] know the knowledge of the holy ones [(having detracted from or added to the words of Moses)].


###### Proverbs 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:4)


מִ֤י עָלָֽה־שָׁמַ֨יִם**׀** וַיֵּרַ֡ד מִ֤י אָֽסַף־ר֨וּחַ**׀** בְּחׇפְנָ֡יו מִ֤י צָֽרַר־מַ֨יִם**׀** בַּשִּׂמְלָ֗ה מִ֭י הֵקִ֣ים כׇּל־אַפְסֵי־אָ֑רֶץ מַה־שְּׁמ֥וֹ וּמַֽה־שֶּׁם־בְּ֝נ֗וֹ כִּ֣י תֵדָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who has ascended up into heaven, and come down again? who has gathered the wind in his fists? who has bound the waters in a garment? who has established all the ends of the earth? what is his name, and what is his son’s name, if thou canst tell?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who [like Moses] ascended to heaven and descended? Who [like Moses] gathered wind [(the furnace-soot) viz. Exodus 9:8-10] in his hand? Who tied the waters in a cloak? [viz. Exodus 15:8]. Who raised [the mishkan, whereby there were founded] all the ends of the earth? [If you would contend that there was someone like Moses,] what is his name what is his son's name? [(How, then, did you not fear to transgress his words?)]


###### Proverbs 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:5)


כׇּל־אִמְרַ֣ת אֱל֣וֹהַּ צְרוּפָ֑ה מָגֵ֥ן ה֝֗וּא לַחֹסִ֥ים בּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Every word of God is proven: he is a shield to those who put their trust in him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All the words of the L-rd are refined [(He wrote nothing to no end and I should have heeded them)]; He is a shield to those who trust in Him.


###### Proverbs 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:6)


אַל־תּ֥וֹסְףְּ עַל־דְּבָרָ֑יו פֶּן־יוֹכִ֖יחַ בְּךָ֣ וְנִכְזָֽבְתָּ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Add not to his words, lest he reprove thee, and thou be found a liar.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not add to His words, lest He prove to you [(that you have sinned through your addition)] and you be foiled.


###### Proverbs 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:7)


שְׁ֭תַּיִם שָׁאַ֣לְתִּי מֵאִתָּ֑ךְ אַל־תִּמְנַ֥ע מִ֝מֶּ֗נִּי בְּטֶ֣רֶם אָמֽוּת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Two things have I asked of thee; do not deny me them before I die:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I ask two things of You; do not withhold [them] from me before I die.


###### Proverbs 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:8)


שָׁ֤וְא**׀** וּֽדְבַר־כָּזָ֡ב הַרְחֵ֬ק מִמֶּ֗נִּי רֵ֣אשׁ וָ֭עֹשֶׁר אַל־תִּֽתֶּן־לִ֑י הַ֝טְרִיפֵ֗נִי לֶ֣חֶם חֻקִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
remove far from me vanity and lies: give me neither poverty nor riches; feed me with my allotted portion:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Keep vanity and falseness far from me. Give me neither poverty nor wealth, but allot me my daily bread.


###### Proverbs 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:9)


פֶּ֥ן־אֶשְׂבַּ֨ע**׀** וְכִחַשְׁתִּי֮ וְאָמַ֗רְתִּי מִ֥י־יְ֫הֹוָ֥ה וּפֶֽן־אִוָּרֵ֥שׁ וְגָנַ֑בְתִּי וְ֝תָפַ֗שְׂתִּי שֵׁ֣ם אֱלֹהָֽי׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
lest I become sated, and deny thee, and say, Who is the Lord? or lest I be poor, and steal, and violate the name of my God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lest [(being rich)] I be sated and [(out of pride)] deny [the L-rd], saying: "Who is the L-rd?" and lest I become impoverished and steal and take the name of my G-d [in vain, in swearing that I have not stolen].


###### Proverbs 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:10)


אַל־תַּלְשֵׁ֣ן עֶ֭בֶד אֶל־אֲדֹנָ֑ו פֶּֽן־יְקַלֶּלְךָ֥ וְאָשָֽׁמְתָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Accuse not a servant to his master, lest he curse thee, and thou be found guilty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not libel a servant [i.e., do not bring judgment against him] to his Master, lest he curse you and you be incriminated.


###### Proverbs 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:11)


דּ֭וֹר אָבִ֣יו יְקַלֵּ֑ל וְאֶת־אִ֝מּ֗וֹ לֹ֣א יְבָרֵֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is a generation that curse their father, and do not bless their mother.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Do not libel even] a generation that curses its father and does not bless [(but curses)] its mother.


###### Proverbs 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:12)


דּ֭וֹר טָה֣וֹר בְּעֵינָ֑יו וּ֝מִצֹּאָת֗וֹ לֹ֣א רֻחָֽץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is a generation that are pure in their own eyes, and yet are not washed from their filthiness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Even] a generation that holds itself pure in its own eyes, and does not cleanse itself of its dung.


###### Proverbs 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:13)


דּ֭וֹר מָה־רָמ֣וּ עֵינָ֑יו וְ֝עַפְעַפָּ֗יו יִנָּשֵֽׂאוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is a generation, O how lofty are their eyes! and their eyelids are lifted up.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Even] a generation of how-haughty-are-its-eyes and how upraised its pupils.


###### Proverbs 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:14)


דּ֤וֹר**׀** חֲרָב֣וֹת שִׁנָּיו֮ וּֽמַאֲכָל֢וֹת מְֽתַלְּעֹ֫תָ֥יו לֶאֱכֹ֣ל עֲנִיִּ֣ים מֵאֶ֑רֶץ וְ֝אֶבְיוֹנִ֗ים מֵאָדָֽם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is a generation, whose teeth are like swords, and their jaw teeth like knives, to devour the poor from off the earth, and the needy from among men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Even] a generation whose [outer] teeth are swords and its inner teeth knives to consume the poor from the land and the needy from [the community of] men.


###### Proverbs 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:15)


לַ֥עֲלוּקָ֨ה**׀** שְׁתֵּ֥י בָנוֹת֮ הַ֤ב**׀** הַ֥֫ב שָׁל֣וֹשׁ הֵ֭נָּה לֹ֣א תִשְׂבַּ֑עְנָה אַ֝רְבַּ֗ע לֹא־אָ֥מְרוּ הֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The leech has two daughters, crying, Give, give. There are three things that are never satisfied; four that never say, It is enough.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The leech [i.e., the grave] has two daughters [Paradise and Gehinnom, each saying]: "Give me, give me." There are three that are never sated, and a fourth that never says "Enough!"


###### Proverbs 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:16)


שְׁאוֹל֮ וְעֹ֢צֶ֫ר רָ֥חַם אֶ֭רֶץ לֹא־שָׂ֣בְעָה מַּ֑יִם וְ֝אֵ֗שׁ לֹא־אָ֥מְרָה הֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She᾽ol; and the barren womb; the earth that is never sated with water; and the fire that never says, It is enough.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Sheol [the grave], the closing up of the womb [i.e., intercourse], the land unsated with water, and the fire [of Gehinnom], which never says "Enough!"


###### Proverbs 30:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:17)


עַ֤יִן**׀** תִּ֥לְעַ֣ג לְאָב֮ וְתָבֻ֢ז לִֽיקְּהַ֫ת־אֵ֥ם יִקְּר֥וּהָ עֹֽרְבֵי־נַ֑חַל וְֽיֹאכְל֥וּהָ בְנֵי־נָֽשֶׁר׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The eye that mocks at his father, and scorns to obey his mother, the ravens of the valley shall pick it out, and the young vultures shall eat it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The eye that mocks a father and spurns a mother's lore will be gouged out by the ravens of the stream [(but not eaten by them, ravens being cruel to their offspring)] and will be eaten by the sons of the eagle [(eagles being merciful to their offspring)].


###### Proverbs 30:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:18)


שְׁלֹשָׁ֣ה הֵ֭מָּה נִפְלְא֣וּ מִמֶּ֑נִּי (וארבע) [וְ֝אַרְבָּעָ֗ה] לֹ֣א יְדַעְתִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There are three things which are too wonderful for me, yea, four which I know not:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There are three [whose ways (i.e., destinations) are] hidden from me, and a fourth that I do not know:


###### Proverbs 30:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:19)


דֶּ֤רֶךְ הַנֶּ֨שֶׁר**׀** בַּשָּׁמַיִם֮ דֶּ֥רֶךְ נָחָ֗שׁ עֲלֵ֫י־צ֥וּר דֶּרֶךְ־אֳנִיָּ֥ה בְלֶב־יָ֑ם וְדֶ֖רֶךְ גֶּ֣בֶר בְּעַלְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the way of the vultures in the air; the way of a snake upon a rock; the way of a ship in the midst of the sea; and the way of a man with a young woman.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The way of an eagle in the heavens, the way of a snake upon a stone, the way of a boat in the heart of the sea, and the way of a man with a maid.


###### Proverbs 30:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:20)


כֵּ֤ן**׀** דֶּ֥רֶךְ אִשָּׁ֗ה מְנָ֫אָ֥פֶת אָ֭כְלָה וּמָ֣חֲתָה פִ֑יהָ וְ֝אָמְרָ֗ה לֹא־פָעַ֥לְתִּי אָֽוֶן׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Likewise the way of an adulterous woman; she eats, and wipes her mouth, and says, I have done nothing wrong.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Such is the way of an adulterous woman: She "eats" and wipes her "mouth" and says: I have done no wrong.


###### Proverbs 30:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:21)


תַּ֣חַת שָׁ֭לוֹשׁ רָ֣גְזָה אֶ֑רֶץ וְתַ֥חַת אַ֝רְבַּ֗ע לֹא־תוּכַ֥ל שְׂאֵֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For three things the earth is disquieted, and for four it cannot bear:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Because of three [things] the earth trembles, and because of a fourth she cannot bear [(i.e., she cannot stand up under her trembling)].


###### Proverbs 30:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:22)


תַּֽחַת־עֶ֭בֶד כִּ֣י יִמְל֑וֹךְ וְ֝נָבָ֗ל כִּ֣י יִֽשְׂבַּֽע־לָֽחֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for a slave when he becomes king; and a fool when he is filled with food;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Because of a servant that attains rulership, and a scoundrel that is sated with bread,


###### Proverbs 30:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:23)


תַּ֣חַת שְׂ֭נוּאָה כִּ֣י תִבָּעֵ֑ל וְ֝שִׁפְחָ֗ה כִּֽי־תִירַ֥שׁ גְּבִרְתָּֽהּ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for an unloved woman when she is married; and a handmaid that is heir to her mistress.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
because of a hateful woman [(a harlot)] who weds a husband, and a maidservant who inherits [the place of] her mistress.


###### Proverbs 30:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:24)


אַרְבָּ֣עָה הֵ֭ם קְטַנֵּי־אָ֑רֶץ וְ֝הֵ֗מָּה חֲכָמִ֥ים מְחֻכָּמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There are four things which are little upon the earth, but they are exceeding wise:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There are four among the small [creatures] of the earth, who have been made wise [by the Creator]:


###### Proverbs 30:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:25)


הַ֭נְּמָלִים עַ֣ם לֹא־עָ֑ז וַיָּכִ֖ינוּ בַקַּ֣יִץ לַחְמָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the ants are a people not strong, yet they prepare their bread in the summer;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The ants, a nation that is not strong, and who [yet] prepare their food in the summer,


###### Proverbs 30:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:26)


שְׁ֭פַנִּים עַ֣ם לֹא־עָצ֑וּם וַיָּשִׂ֖ימוּ בַסֶּ֣לַע בֵּיתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the badgers are but a feeble folk, yet they make their houses in the rocks;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[the] rabbits, a nation that is not powerful, and who [yet] make their abode in the rock;


###### Proverbs 30:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:27)


מֶ֭לֶךְ אֵ֣ין לָאַרְבֶּ֑ה וַיֵּצֵ֖א חֹצֵ֣ץ כֻּלּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the locusts have no king, yet go they forth all of them by bands;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
there is no king to the locusts, and they [yet] go out in a [single] swarm;


###### Proverbs 30:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:28)


שְׂ֭מָמִית בְּיָדַ֣יִם תְּתַפֵּ֑שׂ וְ֝הִ֗יא בְּהֵ֣יכְלֵי מֶֽלֶךְ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The lizard clambers up with her hands, and she is in king’s palaces.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
the spider grasps [walls] with her hands, and she dwells in the palaces of the king.


###### Proverbs 30:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:29)


שְׁלֹשָׁ֣ה הֵ֭מָּה מֵיטִ֣יבֵי צָ֑עַד וְ֝אַרְבָּעָ֗ה מֵיטִ֥בֵי לָֽכֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There are three things which are stately in their tread; four are comely in their stride:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There are three that step securely, and a fourth that walks in confidence:


###### Proverbs 30:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:30)


לַ֭יִשׁ גִּבּ֣וֹר בַּבְּהֵמָ֑ה וְלֹא־יָ֝שׁ֗וּב מִפְּנֵי־כֹֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
a lion which is strongest among beasts, and does not turn away for anyone;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lion, the strongest of the beasts, who does not turn back from anything,


###### Proverbs 30:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:31)


זַרְזִ֣יר מׇתְנַ֣יִם אוֹ־תָ֑יִשׁ וּ֝מֶ֗לֶךְ אַלְק֥וּם עִמּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
a greyhound; a he goat also; and a king, against whom there is no rising up.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
the thigh-belted [hound (the greyhound)], the he-goat, and the king whom none can withstand.


###### Proverbs 30:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:32)


אִם־נָבַ֥לְתָּ בְהִתְנַשֵּׂ֑א וְאִם־זַ֝מּ֗וֹתָ יָ֣ד לְפֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou hast done foolishly in lifting thyself up, or if thou hast thought evil, lay thy hand upon thy mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you have sullied [i.e., demeaned] yourself, [you are destined] to ascend; and if you have plotted [recrimination], put your hand to your mouth [in silence].


###### Proverbs 30:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/30:33)


כִּ֤י מִ֪יץ חָלָ֡ב י֘וֹצִ֤יא חֶמְאָ֗ה וּֽמִיץ־אַ֭ף י֣וֹצִיא דָ֑ם וּמִ֥יץ אַ֝פַּ֗יִם י֣וֹצִיא רִֽיב׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely the wringing of milk brings forth curd, and the wringing of the nose brings forth blood: so the forcing of wrath brings forth strife.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For pressing milk produces butter, and pressing the nose produces blood, and pressing anger produces strife.



## Chapter 31

###### Proverbs 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:1)


דִּ֭בְרֵי לְמוּאֵ֣ל מֶ֑לֶךְ מַ֝שָּׂ֗א אֲֽשֶׁר־יִסְּרַ֥תּוּ אִמּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The words of king Lemu᾽el, the prophecy, that his mother taught him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The words [dedicated] to G-d, [uttered by] the king [(Solomon)], the burden [of the reproof] wherewith his mother chastised him [for his indulgences].


###### Proverbs 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:2)


מַה־בְּ֭רִי וּמַֽה־בַּר־בִּטְנִ֑י וּ֝מֶ֗ה בַּר־נְדָרָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What, my son? and what, son of my womb? and what, son of my vows?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What [did you do that make people call you (pejoratively)] my son [and not the son of your father (an acknowledged tzaddik)]? And what [did you do], the son of my womb? [i.e., the son whom I nurtured in my womb (with additional intercourse, so that he be "whitened and quickened")] And what [did you do], the son of my vows [(whom I vowed would be "whitened" in Torah)]?


###### Proverbs 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:3)


אַל־תִּתֵּ֣ן לַנָּשִׁ֣ים חֵילֶ֑ךָ וּ֝דְרָכֶ֗יךָ לַֽמְח֥וֹת מְלָכִֽין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Give not thy strength to women, nor thy ways to those who destroy kings.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Give not your strength to women [i.e., do not dissipate your strength.] And [conduct not] your ways [in such a manner as] to destroy [the mores] of kings.


###### Proverbs 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:4)


אַ֤ל לַֽמְלָכִ֨ים**׀** לְֽמוֹאֵ֗ל אַ֣ל לַֽמְלָכִ֣ים שְׁתוֹ־יָ֑יִן וּ֝לְרוֹזְנִ֗ים (או) [אֵ֣י] שֵׁכָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is not for kings, O Lemu᾽el, it is not for kings to drink wine; nor for princes to say, Where is strong drink?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is not [right] for kings who [belong] to the L-rd — it is not right for [such] kings — to drink [much] wine, and for [such] princes to imbibe strong drink.


###### Proverbs 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:5)


פֶּן־יִ֭שְׁתֶּה וְיִשְׁכַּ֣ח מְחֻקָּ֑ק וִ֝ישַׁנֶּ֗ה דִּ֣ין כׇּל־בְּנֵי־עֹֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lest he drink, and forget the decree, and pervert the judgment of any of the afflicted.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lest he drink and forget the statute [of the Torah] and [(even worse)] change the law of all the sons of poverty [i.e., the natural law of the meanest of men].


###### Proverbs 31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:6)


תְּנוּ־שֵׁכָ֥ר לְאוֹבֵ֑ד וְ֝יַ֗יִן לְמָ֣רֵי נָֽפֶשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Give strong drink to him that is ready to perish, and wine to those of heavy hearts.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Give strong drink to the lost [i.e., to the wicked, who are destined to go lost], and wine to the bitter of soul [(those afflicted in their poverty or in their mourning)].


###### Proverbs 31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:7)


יִ֭שְׁתֶּה וְיִשְׁכַּ֣ח רִישׁ֑וֹ וַ֝עֲמָל֗וֹ לֹ֣א יִזְכׇּר־עֽוֹד׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let him drink, and forget his poverty, and remember his misery no more.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[For when] he drinks, he will forget his poverty and will no more remember his travail.


###### Proverbs 31:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:8)


פְּתַח־פִּ֥יךָ לְאִלֵּ֑ם אֶל־דִּ֝֗ין כׇּל־בְּנֵ֥י חֲלֽוֹף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Open thy mouth for the dumb, in the cause of all such as are appointed to destruction.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Open your mouth for him who cannot speak [in his own defense], in [behalf of] the judgment of all the sons [whose help] has departed [i.e., orphans].


###### Proverbs 31:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:9)


פְּתַח־פִּ֥יךָ שְׁפׇט־צֶ֑דֶק וְ֝דִ֗ין עָנִ֥י וְאֶבְיֽוֹן׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Open thy mouth, judge righteously, and plead the cause of the poor and needy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Open your mouth, judge righteously, and [obtain] justice for the poor and unfortunate.


###### Proverbs 31:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:10)


אֵֽשֶׁת־חַ֭יִל מִ֣י יִמְצָ֑א וְרָחֹ֖ק מִפְּנִינִ֣ים מִכְרָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who can find a woman of worth? for her price is far above rubies.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who can find the woman of valor? Far more than pearls is her worth.


###### Proverbs 31:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:11)


בָּ֣טַח בָּ֭הּ לֵ֣ב בַּעְלָ֑הּ וְ֝שָׁלָ֗ל לֹ֣א יֶחְסָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The heart of her husband safely trusts in her, and he shall have no lack of gain.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The heart of her husband is secure in her, and he will not lack spoil [i.e., good].


###### Proverbs 31:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:12)


גְּמָלַ֣תְהוּ ט֣וֹב וְלֹא־רָ֑ע כֹּ֝֗ל יְמֵ֣י חַיֶּֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She will do him good and not evil all the days of her life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She returns him good and not evil all the days of her life.


###### Proverbs 31:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:13)


דָּ֭רְשָׁה צֶ֣מֶר וּפִשְׁתִּ֑ים וַ֝תַּ֗עַשׂ בְּחֵ֣פֶץ כַּפֶּֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She seeks wool, and flax, and works willingly with her hands.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She seeks wool and flax and does all with willing palms.


###### Proverbs 31:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:14)


הָ֭יְתָה כׇּאֳנִיּ֣וֹת סוֹחֵ֑ר מִ֝מֶּרְחָ֗ק תָּבִ֥יא לַחְמָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She is like the merchant ships; she brings her food from afar.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She is like the ships of merchants; she brings her bread from afar.


###### Proverbs 31:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:15)


וַתָּ֤קׇם**׀** בְּע֬וֹד לַ֗יְלָה וַתִּתֵּ֣ן טֶ֣רֶף לְבֵיתָ֑הּ וְ֝חֹ֗ק לְנַעֲרֹתֶֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She rises also while it is yet night, and gives food to her household, and a portion to her maidens.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And she arises when it is yet night and prepares food for her household and the allotment of her young maidens.


###### Proverbs 31:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:16)


זָמְמָ֣ה שָׂ֭דֶה וַתִּקָּחֵ֑הוּ מִפְּרִ֥י כַ֝פֶּ֗יהָ (נטע) [נָ֣טְעָה] כָּֽרֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She considers a field, and buys it: with the fruit of her hands she plants a vineyard.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She thinks [to obtain] a field, and she possesses it; from the fruit [i.e., the work] of her palms, she plants a vineyard.


###### Proverbs 31:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:17)


חָֽגְרָ֣ה בְע֣וֹז מׇתְנֶ֑יהָ וַ֝תְּאַמֵּ֗ץ זְרוֹעֹתֶֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She girds her loins with strength, and she makes her arms strong.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She girds her loins with might and strengthens her arms.


###### Proverbs 31:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:18)


טָ֭עֲמָה כִּי־ט֣וֹב סַחְרָ֑הּ לֹא־יִכְבֶּ֖ה (בליל) [בַלַּ֣יְלָה] נֵרָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She perceives that her merchandise is good: her candle does not go out by night.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[When] she takes counsel [with herself, judging] that her commerce is gainful, her candle is not extinguished at night.


###### Proverbs 31:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:19)


יָ֭דֶיהָ שִׁלְּחָ֣ה בַכִּישׁ֑וֹר וְ֝כַפֶּ֗יהָ תָּ֣מְכוּ פָֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She lays her hands to the distaff, and her palms hold the spindle.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She sends her hands to the distaff and her palms hold the spindle.


###### Proverbs 31:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:20)


כַּ֭פָּהּ פָּֽרְשָׂ֣ה לֶעָנִ֑י וְ֝יָדֶ֗יהָ שִׁלְּחָ֥ה לָאֶבְיֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She stretches out her palm to the poor; yea, she reaches forth her hands to the needy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Her palm is spread open to the poor man, and she stretches forth her hand to the unfortunate one.


###### Proverbs 31:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:21)


לֹא־תִירָ֣א לְבֵיתָ֣הּ מִשָּׁ֑לֶג כִּ֥י כׇל־בֵּ֝יתָ֗הּ לָבֻ֥שׁ שָׁנִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She is not afraid of the snow for her household: for all her household are clothed with scarlet.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She does not fear the snow [i.e., the cold] for [those of] her household, for all of her household is clothed in multi [-colored garments].


###### Proverbs 31:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:22)


מַרְבַדִּ֥ים עָֽשְׂתָה־לָּ֑הּ שֵׁ֖שׁ וְאַרְגָּמָ֣ן לְבוּשָֽׁהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She makes herself coverlets; her clothing is fine linen and purple.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She has made [beautiful] spreads for herself; she has clothed herself in fine linen and purple.


###### Proverbs 31:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:23)


נוֹדָ֣ע בַּשְּׁעָרִ֣ים בַּעְלָ֑הּ בְּ֝שִׁבְתּ֗וֹ עִם־זִקְנֵי־אָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Her husband is known in the gates, when he sits among the elders of the land.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Her husband is distinguished [(from his friends by his fine clothing)] in the [city] gates, sitting among the elders of the land.


###### Proverbs 31:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:24)


סָדִ֣ין עָ֭שְׂתָה וַתִּמְכֹּ֑ר וַ֝חֲג֗וֹר נָתְנָ֥ה לַֽכְּנַעֲנִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She makes garments, and sells them; and delivers girdles to the merchant.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She makes linens and sells them, and she gives a sash to the merchant.


###### Proverbs 31:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:25)


עֹז־וְהָדָ֥ר לְבוּשָׁ֑הּ וַ֝תִּשְׂחַ֗ק לְי֣וֹם אַחֲרֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Strength and dignity are her clothing; and she laughs at the time to come.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Strength and glory are her vestments, and she has wrought unto [her] last day [i.e., the day of her death (that it be crowned with a good name)].


###### Proverbs 31:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:26)


פִּ֭יהָ פָּתְחָ֣ה בְחׇכְמָ֑ה וְת֥וֹרַת חֶ֝֗סֶד עַל־לְשׁוֹנָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She opens her mouth with wisdom; and on her tongue is a Tora of steadfast love.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She opens her mouth in wisdom and the Torah of lovingkindness is on her tongue.


###### Proverbs 31:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:27)


צ֭וֹפִיָּה (הילכות) [הֲלִיכ֣וֹת] בֵּיתָ֑הּ וְלֶ֥חֶם עַ֝צְל֗וּת לֹ֣א תֹאכֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She looks well to the ways of her household, and does not eat the bread of idleness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She looks to the ways of [those of] her house [(that they conduct themselves truthfully and modestly)], and she does not eat the bread of indolence.


###### Proverbs 31:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:28)


קָ֣מוּ בָ֭נֶיהָ וַֽיְאַשְּׁר֑וּהָ בַּ֝עְלָ֗הּ וַֽיְהַלְלָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Her children rise up, and call her blessed; her husband also, and he praises her:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Her children have risen and praised her; her husband, and extolled her, [saying]:


###### Proverbs 31:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:29)


רַבּ֣וֹת בָּ֭נוֹת עָ֣שׂוּ חָ֑יִל וְ֝אַ֗תְּ עָלִ֥ית עַל־כֻּלָּֽנָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
many daughters have done virtuously, but thou excellest them all.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Many women have done valorously, but you have risen above all of them.


###### Proverbs 31:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:30)


שֶׁ֣קֶר הַ֭חֵן וְהֶ֣בֶל הַיֹּ֑פִי אִשָּׁ֥ה יִרְאַת־יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה הִ֣יא תִתְהַלָּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Grace is deceitful, and beauty is vain: but a woman who fears the Lord, she shall be praised.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Favor is false, and beauty, vain; the G-d-fearing woman, she is to be praised.


###### Proverbs 31:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Proverbs/r/31:31)


תְּנוּ־לָ֭הּ מִפְּרִ֣י יָדֶ֑יהָ וִיהַלְל֖וּהָ בַשְּׁעָרִ֣ים מַֽעֲשֶֽׂיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Give her of the fruit of her hands; and let her deeds praise her in the gates.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Render her the fruit of her hands [i.e., the reward of her works], and let her deeds extol her in the [city] gates. [(Thus far, the figure [melitzah]; the analogue [mashal] is Torah)].
